# P5 v3 — the independent review before the freeze, 26 October 2026

## 1. Status

A record of review, not a decision. Nothing in the protocol, the code, the tests, the committed records, the decision log or on OSF was changed by it. It is committed with D97, whose only other change is to the draft's line 215, which now cites this file. The fixes it leads to are made by a later sitting; their diff comes back to this review's session for a read-only check before its merge, as D83 checked D82.

## 2. How it was done

Called for by the plan's item 7 (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md`, Order of acts) and by step 1 of the amendment file's order of acts (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md`). Carried out on 26 October 2026 in a Claude Code session on Claude Fable 5.1 (`claude-fable-5-1`, the model its transcript records for every message), from the author's brief of the same date, in the repository at `main` `3fad362` (after D96) with the tracked tree clean. The session had written none of v3 and had seen none of the sittings that did. The object of review is v3 as committed: `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md` against `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md`, `p5_g2_reference.py`, `p5_g3_calibrators.py`, `p5_v3_study.py`, `p5_v3_transcription.py` and `p5_stage2.py` with their tests, `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md`, and the entries D81 to D83 and D86 to D96 of `ROADMAP.md`; v2's frozen blob at `59ca589` and v2's two review records (`docs/protocols/P5_v2_prefreeze_review_2026-10-22.md`, `docs/protocols/P5_v2_second_review_2026-10-22.md`) served as the reference and the model. Four passes:

1. **A workflow of twenty-five agents,** launched at 11:47 UTC on the author's message carrying the word "ultracode", and complete 76 min 27 s later, at 13:03 UTC (4,586,968 ms; 858 tool uses; 5,646,096 tokens; no agent failed, was skipped or returned empty). Seven reviewers, one per dimension of the brief: (1) the v3 text against the plan; (2) the sampler change in the code; (3) the ratio estimator's sampler; (4) G2 and the records; (5) the amendment file; (6) the declarations; (7) the tests and the run paths no test or recorded run has exercised. Two skeptics per dimension, each given that dimension's findings as data and told to refute every one from the files, defaulting to refuted where a claim could not be verified: one through a correctness lens (is the claim literally true of the files at HEAD; does the fix follow and stay within the plan's bounds) and one through a consequence lens (does it bear on a binding rule, a declaration, a run after the freeze, the OSF texts or the sealed set; what severity). A completeness critic given the seven reports, their findings and the skeptics' verdicts, asked what was missing; two skeptics of the critic's items, by the same two lenses; one synthesis agent given every finding with its status. The reviewers and the skeptics of each dimension ran as a pipeline, so a dimension's skeptics started as soon as its report landed; the critic waited for all seven. Every agent inherited the session's model: the twenty-five transcripts record `claude-fable-5-1` on every message and no other model. Each agent's prompt carried the rules below, the facts of where things stood (from the brief and the log, with no conclusion of this session), and, for a reviewer, the dimension's checks from the author's brief with pointers into the files; for a skeptic, the dimension's brief and the findings in JSON; for the critic, the brief's seven lists of checks and the reports verbatim; for the synthesis, every finding with the skeptics' verdicts and the critic's items with theirs. Each agent's structured output carried its full report, which section 9 reproduces unedited.
2. **This session's own checks,** made before the workflow ran and independent of it (section 4).
3. **Verification in this session** of every finding graded must-fix, of eleven of the others, and of the critic's resolutions, against the files at `3fad362`; and a reading in full of the seven reviewers' reports, the critic's and the synthesis (section 4).
4. **The record,** this file, with D97 and the change to line 215.

**The rules every agent's prompt carried.** Read-only: nothing created, edited, moved or deleted inside the repository, and git used only for show, diff, log, ls-files, cat-file, hash-object, rev-parse, grep and blame; no P5 script in any mode, hold modes included, no test, no pricer, no import of a repository module and no Python on a repository file; never a draw of the sealed seeds 20261002 or 20261022, nor a numpy Generator on any seed the protocol names; no emulator loaded and no `.npz` opened, G̃ hashed only; the committed records read as the author's decision 2 allows (below) and otherwise not; Python only as `py -3 -I -c` importing the standard library, every such command listed in the report; no token or credential printed; blob hashes taken from `git show HEAD:<path>`, never from a CRLF working copy; the files themselves read, never a summary of them; evidence cited as path:line at HEAD; nothing reported that was not verified from the files, and no padding. Severities were defined for every agent: must-fix (a disagreement between text and code or plan, a false or incomplete declaration, a binding rule broken, a bug on a path a run after the freeze will take, a sealed-seed risk), same-round (wording, staleness, a missing clause, a weak test, a docstring), note (an observation, a reading to declare, a decision for the author).

**The author's three Plan-Mode decisions,** each on the lean, made in this session on 26 October 2026 before the workflow ran: (1) the workflow's shape, as above, every agent on the session's model; (2) the agents may read the committed JSON and JSONL records and reports (`output/p5_g2_*`, `p5_g2r_*`, `p5v3_study*`, `p5v3_transcription*`) to check a figure a text states, computing no new figure from them and loading no `.npz`, everything in them having been declared as seen; (3) the record carries the agents' reports verbatim.

Two reads went beyond the letter of decision 2 and are declared here: the declarations reviewer read line 2 of `output/p5_g1_tasks.jsonl`, G1's launch line, for a time D88 already states; the tests reviewer's second command read every task line of `output/p5v3_transcription.jsonl` to count its pairs, runs by prior, attempts, routes and m, figures D96 states. The G2 reviewer and its skeptics, the declarations reviewer and the critic's correctness skeptic read the header lines of the study's and the check's records and the fields of the five committed reports named above. No agent opened a `.npz`, drew a seed, imported a repository module or ran a script, a test or the pricer; the text reviewer, its consequence skeptic and the amendment file's correctness skeptic wrote copies of the v2 and v3 blobs to their own scratchpad folders for the diffs. This session, beyond reading and git: standalone `py -3 -I` arithmetic and standard-library reads of the two record headers and the three v3 reports for stated fields (section 4), `sha256sum`, and the scratchpad scripts that generate D97, change line 215 and assemble this record; nothing of P5 was run, no `.npz` opened, nothing under `output/` changed.

## 3. Verdict

**Freeze after fixes; no redesign.** The sampler change is the plan's and nothing more: the draft differs from v2's frozen text at `59ca589` in exactly the eighteen lines D95 names, every other line byte-identical; every change the plan's items 1 to 6 name is present, and nothing under "What v3 does not change" is touched; the constants of candidate (A) in line 68 equal the script's, the study's and the two committed record headers' key for key; the sampler written into `p5_g2_reference.py` is the study's candidate function for function, the one addition declared in D95; the six seeds are the six smallest primes above 149 in line 143's order and collide with no seed of the design; G̃ hashes to `c3adb2e2…`; every blob hash the amendment file states is right; the sealed seeds are drawn on no path, every test that reaches an entry point blocking the draw; the code agrees with the text on every path the study's 55 reference runs and the check's 55 exercised, and reading finds no fault on the paths they did not (the gradient route with a value-and-gradient target, the repeat, two or more kept solutions and the merge after the polish).

Four items must change before the freeze, none of them a change of a registered rule, of the sampler's behaviour on any exercised path, of a seed, a tolerance or the budget: a bug on a scoring path a run after the freeze will take whenever a G3-pilot or test reference fails, an outcome lines 101 and 144 anticipate (`score_set` hands the ratio estimator no Σ_P for a failed pair and stops with an uncaught ValueError; NRE-1 = TESTS-1); the registration texts' contradiction of the plan's binding list on the ratio estimator's sampler (AMD-1); an incomplete AI-use declaration for D91 in line 200 (DECL-1 = V3TEXT-2); and one phrase of line 68 that the code reads more widely than its words on a reachable path (V3TEXT-4, wording only, graded at the boundary). A fifth follows from the amendment file's step 1: the declaration of this review itself in lines 15 and 200 and in the OSF summary, which the fixes' entry makes. The rest is wording, staleness, missing clauses, docstrings and weak tests, worth making in the same round (section 7). The run paths that neither a test nor a recorded run has exercised are listed in section 7 and are a decision for the author (section 8, item 1).

## 5. The session's own findings

- **The brief's CI figure was this session's, not the log's.** The dimension-7 prompt said "D95 reports CI at 364 passed, 8 skipped, 372 collected". The figure came from this session's memory of D95's pull request, not from `ROADMAP.md`, which the tests reviewer and both its skeptics found holds no "364" (D95 records local counts and 372 collected; `ROADMAP.md:5446-5454`). No CI result is in the repository, so the fork cases of the two pool tests, which run only in CI, are on record nowhere a reader of the repository can see (TESTS-5).
- **This review's own reads are to be declared.** v2's line 15 declared each review's reads and computations. For this review the fixes' entry has to add to line 15, to line 200 and to the amendment's line 41: read-only; twenty-five agents on Claude Fable 5.1 and this session; the committed records opened for stated figures only (the headers of the study's and the check's records, the fields of the five committed reports and of v2's two G2 headers, G1's record's launch line, and one count over the check's thirty-six task lines); no `.npz` opened, G̃ hashed; no script, test or pricer run; no seed drawn; standalone arithmetic only. Nothing new was seen: every record opened was declared as seen by D88, D89, D94 or D96.
- **Observed before the workflow and confirmed by it:** the repeat test runs the written-in start for real on the toy and asserts nothing of it (TESTS-2); Stage 2 reads nothing of the attempts, so the records' `start` changes only the digest's input and the files' size (TESTS, section 2b); the two record headers' constants equal the code's key for key (G2, section 3); "best kept solution" is the first kept solution in the pre-polish order (D2-1).

Each with the synthesis's lean, marked as such.

Declare it as the first kept solution in the pre-polish order, no code change, "as the study ran it" kept; or re-sort `kept` by the polished log-target, a no-op at m = 1 so the check still reproduces, but a departure from the study's sampler on an unexercised path, to be declared. Lean: declare.

## 9. The reports, verbatim

Each report is the agent's structured output's `report` field, its text unedited and its headings moved three levels down to sit under this section's: the seven reviewers' in the order of the dimensions, each followed by its two skeptics' (correctness, then consequence); then the completeness critic's, the two skeptics' of its items, and the synthesis. Paths and line numbers in them are the agents' own, at `3fad362`; the scratchpad paths some name are the agents' temporary folders, outside the repository.

### 9.1 Reviewer, dimension 1: the v3 text against the plan

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review, D97 — Dimension 1: the v3 text against the plan

Read at `3fad362`, the tracked tree clean. Read-only throughout: no repository file written, no P5 script, test or pricer run, no repository module imported, no `.npz` opened (G̃ hashed only), no seed drawn. The committed records under `output/` were not opened; every figure below is taken from the decision log as written.

##### 1. Verdict

No must-fix finding. `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md` is v2's frozen blob at `59ca589` with exactly the 18 lines D95 declares changed (1, 7, 9, 15, 54, 68, 75, 137, 140, 142, 143, 200, 204, 209, 210, 213, 215, 216), every other line byte-identical, and a word-level pass shows that within each changed line only the named change moves: line 143's tolerances, its one retry at doubled lengths and its budget of 1,344 core-hours are untouched, as are line 68's acceptance, reweighting rule, Kish floor and reported figures. Every item of the plan's "What v3 changes (exhaustively)" is present in the draft; nothing the plan does not name is changed beyond the title line, which D95 declares; nothing under "What v3 does not change" is altered. Line 68's constants and words agree with candidate (A) as the plan states it, with D93's declared settings and with the code in `p5_g2_reference.py` and `p5_v3_study.py`, including the reading D93 declared (the mass rule's reference is the largest Laplace mass) and the places the prior's density and curvature are taken at a clipped u. The figures of lines 7, 9, 15, 54, 140, 142 and 143 agree with D88, D89, D93, D94 and D96 as written, and the seeds' rule holds arithmetically with none of the six seeds in use. Four points are worth making in the same round (a summary in line 7 that omits the go rule's clause; line 200's attribution of D91; two wordings in lines 68 and 75 that the code reads more narrowly than the words allow) and five notes record readings to declare and two decisions for the author.

##### 2. Findings

**V3TEXT-1 (same-round) — Line 7's enumeration of v3's changes omits the go rule's clause.**
*Claim.* The Status line lists the changes as the start and first proposal of the two samplers, G2's seeds and the clause on its retry's start, the adoption of G̃ and the declarations of what had been seen. It does not name the clause the plan's item 1 adds to line 143's go rule, that a run's core-seconds include its optimiser's, which the draft does carry.
*Evidence.* `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:7` (the list after "exactly the changes the plan names"); `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:33` (item 1, "The go rule"); `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:143` ("a run's core-seconds including its optimiser's (line 68; D95)").
*Bears on.* Line 7, which the freeze commit extends; the plan's item 1 (third bullet) and item 6 (line 7's content).
*Proposed fix.* After "the clause on its retry's start (under Gates)" add "and the clause of its go rule that a run's core-seconds include its optimiser's". The anchor "exactly the changes the plan names" is true as written; the fix is to the gloss.

**V3TEXT-2 (same-round) — Line 200 attributes D91 wholly to Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code; D91 records a first version from another session.**
*Claim.* Line 200 says D91 (the plan, 25 October 2026) ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code. D91 records that the plan's first version was written "in the session that guides the author", naming neither its model nor its medium, and that the Claude Code session on Claude Opus 5.5 reviewed and corrected it. The AI-use declaration is therefore incomplete for D91 in the way its Limitations sentence admits for the earliest sittings.
*Evidence.* `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200`; `ROADMAP.md:4694-4700` ("The first version was written on 25 October 2026, after D89, in the session that guides the author. This Claude Code session checked it read-only"); `ROADMAP.md:4715-4718`.
*Bears on.* Line 200 (Use of generative AI, the Tool sentence); the plan's item 6 (line 200: v3's sittings and models).
*Proposed fix.* "D91 (the plan's review and corrected copy, 25 October 2026) ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code, the plan's first version coming from the session that guides the author, whose model D91 does not record"; or the qualification under Limitations.

**V3TEXT-3 (same-round) — Line 75's "from the same 16 uniform starts" can be read as the reference pair's own start points.**
*Claim.* The ratio estimator's modes are said to be found "from the same 16 uniform starts". In the code the 16 starts are the first block of the ratio estimator's own generator `default_rng([107, p, q, i, j])`: the same in number, distribution and draw order as the reference's, not the same points.
*Evidence.* `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:75`; `p5_g3_calibrators.py:485-488`; `p5_g2_reference.py:577-578`.
*Bears on.* Line 75; the plan's Decision 3 and lines 120-125.
*Proposed fix.* "from 16 starts uniform on [0, 1]⁴, the first block of its own generator as under The exact reference".

**V3TEXT-4 (same-round) — "Two solutions polished to one point counting as one" is, in the code, a merge within 0.01.**
*Claim.* Line 68 (2) says two solutions polished to one point count as one. The code merges a polished kept solution into an earlier polished one when their unit-coordinate distance is at most `DISTINCT` (0.01), the keep rule's own test, and counts the starts that reached either for the one kept; coincidence is not required.
*Evidence.* `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68`; `p5_g2_reference.py:459-463` (the merge), `471-473` (`reached` re-mapped), `474` (`n_merged_after_polish`); `ROADMAP.md:5044-5046` (D93's point 6, the same words).
*Bears on.* Line 68, step (2); the plan's step 2 (lines 97-99).
*Proposed fix.* "two kept solutions polished to within 0.01 of one another counting as one, the starts that reached either counted for it".

**V3TEXT-5 (note) — "Best kept solution" is the first of the kept list, ranked before the polish.**
*Claim.* Line 68 says the pair's record carries, for a direct prior, the accepted direct run's Σ at its best kept solution. In the code that is `kept[0]`: the kept list is in the order of the log-target before the polish (−cost plus log P at u clipped to [0.01, 0.99]) and is not re-ranked by the polished log-target, so with m > 1 the "best" is the best before polishing. The study and the check saw m = 1 in every run, so the reading has had no effect, and the code is bound to the study's by D96.
*Evidence.* `p5_g2_reference.py:443-446, 457, 468, 851`; `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68`; `ROADMAP.md:5151-5152, 5605-5607`.
*Bears on.* Line 68 (the pair's record) and line 75 (which reads that Σ_P).
*Proposed fix.* Declare the reading: "at its first kept solution, the kept list being in the order of the log-target before the polish"; no code change.

**V3TEXT-6 (note) — Step (1) says least_squares minimises the negative log-target; for a direct run it minimises −log L only.**
*Claim.* Line 68 (1) says the negative log-target is minimised from the 16 starts by `least_squares` on the residuals. For a direct run the least-squares objective is ½Σr² = −log L alone; the prior enters at the keep rule and the polish. The sentence's later clauses say so and the plan's step 1 has the same wording, so this is a reading to declare.
*Evidence.* `p5_g2_reference.py:423-427, 434-440, 447-457`; `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68`; `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:91-95`.
*Bears on.* Line 68, step (1); the plan's step 1.
*Proposed fix.* "−log L is minimised ... by least_squares ...; for a direct run the prior enters at the keep rule and the polish below".

**V3TEXT-7 (note) — The retry clause's "its starts ... depending on its own surface": the optimiser's starting points are shared.**
*Claim.* Lines 68 and 143 say a retry pair shares only the generator's stream with the first attempt's pair of the same i and j, its starts and first proposal depending on its own surface. Because the 16 × 4 uniforms and the 4 × 4 normals are the stream's first blocks, a retry pair's optimiser starting points and perturbation draws equal its namesake's; what depends on y is the solutions, hence the chains' starts and Σ. "Its starts" reads as the chains' starts, which is right, but the recorded `starts` will be equal across the two attempts.
*Evidence.* `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68, 143`; `p5_g2_reference.py:577-578, 814, 615`; `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:32, 150`.
*Bears on.* Lines 68 and 143; the plan's item 1, second bullet.
*Proposed fix.* Optionally: "its optimiser's starting points and perturbation draws shared, its solutions, chains' starts and first proposal depending on its own surface".

**V3TEXT-8 (note) — The text names no outcome for a start that raises; the guards look unreachable on a finite G̃.**
*Claim.* `run_chains` raises if no least-squares solution has a finite log-target or no solution is kept; `launch` then records a `task_error` line, the pair gets no record, and `g2_report` refuses a record with a missing pair, an outcome line 143 does not name. On a finite G̃ (D88: every value finite) `least_squares` always returns a point with finite cost and the mass is finite because the eigenvalues are floored, so the guards appear unreachable; v2's start could not raise. A decision for the author whether line 68 should say that such an error counts as a reference failure.
*Evidence.* `p5_g2_reference.py:441-442, 507-508, 589-590, 1160-1164, 1306-1307`; `ROADMAP.md:4441`; `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68, 143`.
*Bears on.* Lines 68 and 143; v3's G2 and the test set's references.
*Proposed fix.* Leave as a declared reading, or add to line 68 that a start keeping no solution is recorded as an error and counts as a reference failure (which the code would then have to record so).

**V3TEXT-9 (note) — What the study and the check did not exercise is declared in the log, not in the draft.**
*Claim.* D95 declares that the gradient route has run only on smooth stand-ins and that its behaviour on a trained ReLU logit is first seen at G3's pilot; D96 declares that every run of the transcription check took the least-squares route at its first attempt and kept one solution, so the gradient route, the repeat at doubled lengths and the keeping of several solutions were not exercised. Line 15 declares the study's stand-ins and the check's exact reproduction but not these limits. A decision for the author.
*Evidence.* `ROADMAP.md:5520-5526` (D95, Declared (6)); `ROADMAP.md:5605-5609` (D96); `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:15`.
*Bears on.* Line 15; the plan's item 6.
*Proposed fix.* Optionally add after the check's sentence: "every run of the study and of the check took the least-squares route at its first attempt and kept one solution; the gradient route has run only on the stand-ins, and the repeat and the keeping of several solutions were not exercised (D95, D96)".

### 9.2 Skeptic, dimension 1, correctness lens

#### D97 skeptic's check — dimension 1, the v3 text against the plan (lens: correctness)

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: correctness — is each claim literally true of the files at HEAD (`3fad362`), read on both sides of the comparison (the draft's line, the plan's item, the log entry, the code, the test), and does the proposed fix follow from the claim within the plan's binding list ("What v3 does not change", `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:69-80`)?

Result: of nine findings, none is refuted, nine stand, none is re-graded. Two are same-round wording defects in declarations (V3TEXT-1, V3TEXT-2), two are same-round wording defects where the text under-describes the code (V3TEXT-3, V3TEXT-4), five are readings to declare or decisions for the author (V3TEXT-5 to V3TEXT-9). One further observation is recorded under section 3.

Groundwork. `git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md` and `git show HEAD:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md` (both LF, both 216 lines) differ at exactly lines 1, 7, 9, 15, 54, 68, 75, 137, 140, 142, 143, 200, 204, 209, 210, 213, 215 and 216 — the 18 lines D95 names (`ROADMAP.md:5209-5211`); every other line is byte-identical, as draft line 7 claims ("the title line apart"). Line 1 is "v2" to "v3" in the title.

##### 2. The findings

###### V3TEXT-1 — stands, same-round
`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:7` lists v3's changes as the two samplers' start and first proposal, "G2's seeds and the clause on its retry's start (under Gates), the adoption of G̃, and the declarations of what had been seen". The plan's item 1, third bullet (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:33`) adds a clause to line 143's go rule, and the draft carries it (`:143`, "a run's core-seconds including its optimiser's (line 68; D95)"); v2's line 143 at `59ca589` has no such words. The code matches line 143 (`p5_g2_reference.py:791-797` time each attempt from before `run_chains`, whose optimiser sits at `:577-592`; `project_cost` reads those seconds at `:1264-1265`), so the defect is confined to the gloss in line 7. The plan's item 6 (`:56`) does not ask line 7 to enumerate the changes, so the fix is in bounds.

###### V3TEXT-2 — stands, same-round
`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200`: "D91 (the plan, 25 October 2026) ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code". `ROADMAP.md:4694-4696`: the plan's first version was written "in the session that guides the author", and "This Claude Code session checked it read-only"; `:4715-4718`: "The first version came from the session that guides the author; the review and the corrected copy came from this Claude Code session on Claude Opus 5.5". The phrase occurs in the log only at `:4695` and `:4716`, never with a model or a medium. D92 (`:4791-4792`) identifies the Claude Code session of D89 to D91 as Opus 5.5, which covers D91's entry and corrected copy, not the first version. The gloss "(the plan ...)" therefore over-attributes; the Limitations sentence of line 200 already has the form the fix needs.

###### V3TEXT-3 — stands, same-round (confidence medium)
`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:75`: "from the same 16 uniform starts". `p5_g3_calibrators.py:485` builds `default_rng([SEED_NRE, p, q, i, j])` (`SEED_NRE = 107`, `:128`) and passes it to `R._run_with_repeat` (`:487-488`), whence `run_chains` (`p5_g2_reference.py:792`) draws the 16 × 4 starts as that generator's first block (`:577`); the reference's generator is `default_rng([83, i, j])` (`:814`). The starts are the same in number, law and draw position, not the same points. Confidence is medium only because the same sentence of line 75 already names the generator and "its draws in the order of The exact reference", which narrows the misreading; the words still invite it, and the proposed wording is accurate.

###### V3TEXT-4 — stands, same-round
`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68` (2): "two solutions polished to one point counting as one". `p5_g2_reference.py:459` merges when `np.linalg.norm(x - k_["x"]) <= DISTINCT` (0.01, `:180`), the polished point against earlier polished kept points; `:460-463` map and skip; `:471-473` re-map `reached` so the survivor counts both solutions' starts; `:474` records `n_merged_after_polish`. The test is the keep rule's own (`:384`, `:391`). `ROADMAP.md:5044-5046` (D93, point 6) uses the draft's words. Because the keep rule defines distinctness at 0.01, "one point" is readable at that resolution; wording, not a reachable-path disagreement.

###### V3TEXT-5 — stands, note
`p5_g2_reference.py:381` orders by `np.argsort(-logf, kind="stable")` on the pre-polish log-target (`:434-439`); `:445-446` walk that order; `:457` records the polished log-target without re-sorting; `:468` appends in that order (the first candidate can never be merged); `:851` and `:839` take `kept[0]`. "Best kept solution" in the pair's record of line 68 therefore means first in pre-polish order. `ROADMAP.md:5151-5152` (D94) and `:5605-5607` (D96): m = 1 in every run so far. A reading to declare; no code change follows.

###### V3TEXT-6 — stands, note
`p5_g2_reference.py:423-427`: `least_squares` on the residuals, cost = ½Σr² = −log L; the prior enters at `:434-440` (keep rule) and `:447-457` (polish). Draft `:68` (1) opens with "the negative log-target ... is minimised ... by least_squares on the residuals", then says the rest; the plan's step 1 (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:91-95`) has the same opening. A reading to declare.

###### V3TEXT-7 — stands, note
`p5_g2_reference.py:814` seeds `default_rng([seed, i, j])` with i within the set; `:577-578` draw the 16 × 4 uniforms and 4 × 4 normals before anything depends on y; `:615` records them as `starts`. For the same (i, j) the two attempts' optimiser starting points and perturbation draws coincide; the solutions (`:583`), the chains' starts (`:591`) and Σ (`:594`) depend on y. The plan says so (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:150`, "drawn 18 times, not 36"; `:32`). Draft `:68` and `:143` are right under one reading of "its starts" and ambiguous under the other.

###### V3TEXT-8 — stands, note
Raises at `p5_g2_reference.py:441-442`, `:507-508`, `:589-590`. Both pool paths catch a pair's exception (`:1178-1179`, `:1189-1190`) and `take` writes a `task_error` line and continues (`:1160-1164`); `read_jsonl` (`p5_g0_pilot.py:608-613`) keeps only kind `task` in `tasks`, so the pair has no record and is not in `done` (`:1149`; a relaunch would retry it); `g2_report` refuses a missing pair (`:1305-1307`). Draft `:143` names reference failures only. Unreachability on a finite G̃ (`ROADMAP.md:4441`): finite residuals give a finite cost; a direct run's `logf` adds log P at u clipped to [0.01, 0.99] (`:434`, `:439`), finite for both Betas (a, b > 1, `:190`); the mass uses Σ with eigenvalues clipped to [1e-10, 1/12] before `slogdet` (`:337-351`, `:378`); `keep_solutions` keeps the first in order, whose mass is the maximum (`:379-387`), so m ≥ 1 whenever `:441` does not raise. v2's uniform start had nothing to raise. A decision for the author.

###### V3TEXT-9 — stands, note
`ROADMAP.md:5520-5526` (D95, Declared (6)) and `:5605-5609` (D96) record what was not exercised. Draft `:15` ("Declared for v3 (D95)") names the study's surfaces, lengths, checks, mass diagnostic and stand-ins, and the check's exact reproduction, but not those limits. The plan's item 6 (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:58`) asks line 15 to gain what was seen, which it does. A decision for the author.

##### 3. Own observations

- `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200` calls D88, D89 and D90 "v3's sittings"; the log heads them "P5 v2:" (`ROADMAP.md:4383`, `:4476`, `:4614`). They are the sittings since v2's freeze that v2's frozen line 200 could not carry (`:4471-4472`). "The sittings since v2's freeze" would be exact. Severity: note. The models line 200 states for them are those the entries record (`:4467-4471`, `:4605-4609`, `:4657-4659`, `:4791-4792`, `:5196-5198`, `:5688-5690`).

### 9.3 Skeptic, dimension 1, consequence lens

#### D97, dimension 1 (the v3 text against the plan): adversarial verification

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: consequence. Each of the nine findings was checked against the files at `3fad362` (the frozen v2 blob at `59ca589` for the diff), read-only; nothing from the repository was run or imported, no `.npz` opened, no seed drawn.

Of nine findings none is refuted; all nine stand on the evidence. One is re-graded: V3TEXT-4 from same-round to must-fix (at the boundary). The other eight keep the reviewer's grades (three same-round, five note). One proposed fix (V3TEXT-9) would be false as worded and needs narrowing. One own observation, at note, is added, with a list of what was checked and found sound.

The line diff was recomputed in the scratchpad: both files have 216 lines and exactly lines 1, 7, 9, 15, 54, 68, 75, 137, 140, 142, 143, 200, 204, 209, 210, 213, 215 and 216 differ; every other line is byte-identical, as D95 declares (ROADMAP.md:5209-5211).

##### 2. The findings

###### V3TEXT-1 — stands, same-round
`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:7` lists the two samplers' start and first proposal, "G2's seeds and the clause on its retry's start (under Gates)", the adoption of G̃ and the declarations; the go rule's clause is not named. The plan names it as a change (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:33`) and the draft carries it: the word diff of line 143 shows "force" became "force, a run's core-seconds including its optimiser's (line 68; D95),". The anchor "exactly the changes the plan names" is true, so the fault is in the gloss, which also leaves out line 9's reason, line 137's list, line 200 and the checklist. The reviewer's fix is right and small; marking the gloss non-exhaustive would be as small. Same-round.

###### V3TEXT-2 — stands, same-round

The sentence is true of the D91 sitting and incomplete about the plan's first version; the draft's Limitations sentence covers only sittings before 11 October 2026. The amendment file names no model (no "Claude" or "Opus" in `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md`), so only line 200 is touched. The proposed qualification is right. Same-round.

###### V3TEXT-3 — stands, same-round
Line 75's "from the same 16 uniform starts": the ratio estimator's run seeds its own generator `default_rng([107, p, q, i, j])` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:485`) and passes it to `R._run_with_repeat` (487-488), whose `run_chains` draws the 16 × 4 uniforms as the stream's first block (`p5_g2_reference.py:577`); the reference's come from `default_rng([83, i, j])` (814). Same scheme, not the same points. The earlier clause of line 75 ("its draws in the order of The exact reference") resolves it for a careful reader, so this is wording. Same-round.

###### V3TEXT-4 — stands, re-graded to must-fix
Line 68 (2): "two solutions polished to one point counting as one". `p5_g2_reference.py:459-463` merges a polished solution into an earlier kept one when their distance is at most `DISTINCT` = 0.01 (line 180), counting it in `n_merged`; 471-473 re-map `reached` and count the starts for the merged solution. D93's point 6 (`ROADMAP.md:5044-5046`) uses the draft's words. Read literally, "one point" is coincidence, which L-BFGS-B from two starts will essentially never produce; the text's rule and the code's therefore differ on a path a post-freeze direct run with m ≥ 2 can take, and the recorded m and the chains' solutions would follow the code. Every run so far had m = 1 (`ROADMAP.md:5151-5152`, `5605-5607`), so the likelihood is low; but the brief's must-fix test is a disagreement between text and code, and line 137 would otherwise require a dated amendment the first time it bites. The reviewer's wording is the right and smallest fix and needs no declaration beyond D97's record. Must-fix, at the boundary with same-round.

###### V3TEXT-5 — stands, note
`p5_g2_reference.py:434-440` forms the keep rule's log-target before the polish (−cost plus the prior at u clipped to [0.01, 0.99]); `keep_solutions` orders by it (381); `_modes_least_squares` appends in that order (445, 468) and records the polished log-target (457) without re-sorting; `reference_pair` reads `kept[0]` (839, 851). "Best kept solution" is therefore the best before polishing. With m = 1 throughout, and Σ_P a preconditioner only (line 75), nothing has turned on it. A reading to declare. Note.

Line 68 (1) says the polish is "on ½Σr² − log P(x_H)", which resolves the opening phrase; the plan's step 1 (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:91-95`) has the same structure. Note.

The uniforms and normals are the stream's first blocks (`p5_g2_reference.py:577-578`), drawn before anything depends on y, from `default_rng([seed, i, j])` (814), and recorded under `starts` (615). On a G̃ with every value finite (`ROADMAP.md:4441`) the cost is finite, the prior at the clipped u is finite and the mass is finite because the eigenvalues are clipped (337-351), so the best-mass solution is always kept and m ≥ 1: the guards look unreachable. v2's `run_chains` at `59ca589` drew uniform starts with no guard; its only raise on the path was `_chol_pd`'s, which HEAD keeps. D94 adds that the study's 55 reference runs were all accepted at the first attempt with m = 1 (`ROADMAP.md:5113-5117`, `5151-5152`). ### 9.4 Reviewer, dimension 2: the sampler change in the code

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review, D97 — Dimension 2: the sampler change in the code

Repository `C:\Users\micha\OneDrive\Documents\roughvollab`, HEAD `3fad362`, tracked tree clean. Blobs reviewed (LF bytes, `git show HEAD:<path> | sha256sum`): `p5_g2_reference.py` `239f506e…`, `p5_v3_study.py` `82605e58…`, `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md` `cad1fcee…`, `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md` `658824f0…`. Read-only; no script, test, pricer or repository module run or imported; no generator seeded; no `.npz` opened.

##### 1. Verdict

The sampler change is sound and may be frozen with the items below taken up in the same round. `run_chains` is `p5_v3_study.candidate_run_chains` ported: after stripping the `R.` prefix, every helper is identical line for line except docstrings and the text of two error messages, and the one addition D95 declares (`_modes_gradient` taking a target's `value_grad_unit`) is the only change of behaviour, on a path the reference never takes. The generator's stream (16 × 4 uniforms, 4 × 4 standard normals, then z and u per step), the constants, the keep rule, Σ from the singular values with the stacked √κ e₁ᵀ row and the clip, the polish's objective, gradient sign and bounds, the floor on κ where line 68 puts it and not where it does not, the chain starts with the cap and the clip, the first proposal, the record's `start` and `under[P]["sigma_P"]`, and the resume guard all agree with draft line 68, the plan's steps 1 to 6 and Decision 3. Reasoning line by line through the paths the transcription check did not exercise (the gradient route, the repeat, m ≥ 2 and the merge after the polish) found no off-by-one, no aliasing, no non-finite value or numpy type reaching the record, no key collision and no Σ that `_chol_pd` cannot factor; no path was found on which the start fails a run that v2's sampler would have completed. Three same-round items: for a direct run with m ≥ 2 the "best kept solution" is the first in the keep rule's pre-polish order and the polish can overturn that order, a reading to declare or a re-sort to decide (D2-1); two docstring phrases fall short of the code (D2-2); no test runs the least-squares route with m ≥ 2 or the merge after the polish (D2-3). One note: a start that raises is recorded as a task error, not a reference failure (D2-4).

##### 2. Findings

**D2-1 — same-round — "Best kept solution" of a direct run is the first in the keep rule's order, which the polish can overturn.**
*Claim.* In `_modes_least_squares` the kept list is built in the keep rule's order, which for a direct run is the log-target with the prior's density at x_H clipped to [0.01, 0.99], before the polish. Each kept solution is then polished and its log-target recomputed at the polished point, and the list is never re-sorted. So `kept[0]`, which the pair's record takes as "the accepted direct run's Σ at its best kept solution" (`under[P]["sigma_P"]`), need not be the kept solution with the largest recorded log-target when m ≥ 2 (a face solution ranks by log P(0.01) in the keep rule and lower once polished inside; a second solution's polish can gain more than the gap). The effect is confined to the preconditioner Σ_P the ratio estimator's sampler reads and to the reading of the record: the chains start at every kept solution, k mod m, whatever the order. The transcription check did not reach the path (55 runs, every one m = 1).
*Evidence.* `p5_g2_reference.py:443-470` (kept built in `kept_idx` order; `lf` recomputed at 457; no re-sort), `851` (`sigma_P` = `kept[0]["sigma"]`); the same in `p5_v3_study.py:288-346` and `566-575`; `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68` ("(2) … in order of log-target from the best (for a direct run the run's own log-target, the prior included) … taken at u clipped"; "the accepted direct run's Σ at its best kept solution").
*Bears on.* Line 68 (the keep rule and the pair's Σ_P); Decision 3 (Σ_P as the preconditioner); any post-freeze direct run under A or B that keeps two or more solutions.
*Proposed fix.* Either declare the reading in line 68 ("its best kept solution" being the first in the keep rule's order), which changes no code and keeps D95's "as the study ran it"; or re-sort `kept` by the polished log-target after the loop (a no-op for m = 1, so the transcription check still reproduces, but a departure from the study's sampler on a path it never ran, to be declared). The author decides.

**D2-2 — same-round — Two phrases of the module docstring fall short of the code.**
*Claim.* (i) Lines 30-32 say κ at x_H clipped to [0.01, 0.99] applies to the recorded Σ_A and Σ_B and do not say that the keep rule's prior density and curvature are taken at the same clipped u, which the code does and line 68 and the constant's own comment state. (ii) Lines 66-68 say that with a target "Σ [is] the given sigma for every solution", whereas the code first clips the given sigma's eigenvalues to [1e-10, 1/12] (`clip_cov`) and records the count clipped, as line 75 says ("clipped as there").
*Evidence.* `p5_g2_reference.py:30-32`, `66-68` against `434-440`, `178-179`, `490`, `510-512`; draft lines 68 and 75.
*Bears on.* The docstring as the code's own account of lines 68 and 75; nothing in a run.
*Proposed fix.* "… the prior's density and curvature for the keep rule, and κ for the recorded Σ_A and Σ_B, at x_H clipped to [0.01, 0.99] (KAPPA_U_FLOOR)"; and "Σ the given sigma, its eigenvalues clipped to [1e-10, 1/12] as the start clips its own, for every solution".

**D2-3 — same-round — No test exercises the least-squares route with m ≥ 2 or the merge after the polish.**
*Claim.* The only assertions on `n_merged_after_polish` are that it is 0; the m = 2 cases tested run the gradient route (a two-Gaussian target) or `chain_starts` alone; the toy `LinearEm` is unimodal. So the merge (`pos_map`, the remap of `reached`, `n_starts_reached` after a merge, m reduced) and a least-squares run with several kept solutions (chain k at solution k mod m with a first proposal per chain from different Σ) are reasoned about here but never run by a test, nor by the transcription check.
*Evidence.* `test_p5_v3_study.py:101`, `297` (== 0), `203-218`, `335-343`; `test_p5_g2_reference.py:784-788`; the code at `p5_g2_reference.py:459-473`; ROADMAP.md D96 ("every one of the 55 runs … kept one solution").
*Bears on.* The paths of line 68 a post-freeze run may take (a bimodal or valley-shaped likelihood under A or B).
*Proposed fix.* A deterministic unit test of `_modes_least_squares` (and `run_chains` through it) on a toy emulator with two least-squares minima: under the uniform prior m = 2, `chain_solution` [0, 1, 0, 1], two different first proposals, `reached` and `starts_elsewhere` summing to 16; under prior A the two kept solutions polished to within 0.01 of one point, `n_merged_after_polish` 1, m 1, the survivor's `n_starts_reached` the sum of both, `optimiser.reached` remapped to 0 or −1, the record JSON-clean.

**D2-4 — note — A start that raises is a task_error line, not a recorded reference failure.**
*Claim.* The start can raise (no finite least-squares solution; `least_squares` or the SVD on non-finite emulator output); `launch` catches the worker's exception, writes a `{"kind": "task_error"}` line and continues; `read_jsonl` does not count that line as the pair's record, so the pair is absent and the report refuses the set rather than recording a reference failure. v2's sampler on the same input would not have passed the acceptance either (its chains never accept on a non-finite target), but it would have recorded a `reference_failure`. With G̃ a finite emulator on the box this needs non-finite input and is not expected; no path was found on which the start fails a run v2's sampler would have completed. The RuntimeError's own text says the run is recorded as an error, so this is a declared choice.
*Evidence.* `p5_g2_reference.py:441-442`, `507-508`, `589-590`, `1160-1166`, `1177-1181`, `1187-1192`, `1281-1290`; `p5_g0_pilot.py:608-614`.
*Bears on.* The reading of a record that contains a task_error line; line 143's "a reference failure on any pilot surface fails the gate".
*Proposed fix.* None required; the author may say in line 68 or 143 that a run whose start raises is recorded as a task error and re-run on resume, or catch the start's exceptions in `_run_with_repeat` and record the attempt as a failure. A decision for the author.

##### 3. Checked and found sound

- **Stream order.** `p5_g2_reference.py:577-578` draw the 16 × 4 uniform block then the n_chains × 4 standard-normal block before anything else; `mh_step` (`278-295`) draws z (`282`) then u (`288`) in every step, inside or not; v2's `run_chains` at 59ca589 drew 4 × 4 uniforms then `mh_step`'s (`git show 59ca589:p5_g2_reference.py:243-272`). As the plan's items 1 to 3 and line 68.
- **Constants.** `p5_g2_reference.py:171-182` equal `p5_v3_study.py:99-114` value for value (the diff shows only comments and `SCALE = R.SCALE`); `sampler_constants` (`552-561`) and `candidate_constants` (`p5_v3_study.py:144-152`) agree key for key, the `draw_order` string identical; the transcription report's `start` and `lengths.start` carry the same values.
- **Keep rule** (`370-394`): order by log-target, stable (`382`); distinct when the unit distance to every kept solution exceeds 0.01 (`383-384`); kept when the Laplace mass is within log 1e4 of the largest mass over all solutions (`377-379`, `386`); at most four (`381`); `reached` the position of the first kept solution within 0.01 of each, else −1 (`389-393`); m ≥ 1 whenever any log f is finite, so the raise at `589-590` is unreachable after `441-442`. The plan's "within log 10⁴ of the best's" is read as the draft's "largest mass among the solutions" (the plan's next sentence says the same); under it the highest-log-target solution can be dropped, as `test_p5_v3_study.py:203-206` asserts.
- **Σ** (`337-351`): the SVD of J, or of J stacked with √κ e₁ᵀ when κ > 0 (`343`), V S⁻² Vᵀ (`347-350`), never an inverse; a zero singular value gives an infinite eigenvalue clipped to 1/12 and counted; the clip to [1e-10, 1/12] and the count recorded; symmetrised. κ > 0 for both priors (`303-310`).
- **KAPPA_U_FLOOR** applies to the keep rule (`434-440`) and to the recorded Σ_A and Σ_B of every kept solution of every run (`465-466`, `469`); the direct run's own Σ_P is at the unclipped polished x_H (`467`), where κ can reach 1.5e12 and the H eigenvalue is clipped to 1e-10 and counted. Line 68's rule exactly.
- **The polish** (`447-458`): objective ½rᵀr − log P(x_H) (`453`); gradient Jᵀr − (d log P/du) e₁ (`450-452`); L-BFGS-B ftol 1e-10, gtol 1e-5, maxiter 200 (`411-416`); H within [1e-6, 1 − 1e-6] (`580-581`); the log-target at the polished point (`457`) with J there by the same bounded difference (`456`); status, nit, fun, message recorded (`458`).
- **fd_jacobian** (`319-334`): step √ε·max(1, |x_i|), backward where the forward point would exceed the upper bound, the actual difference the divisor; `residuals` (`313-316`) clip to [0, 1] and give (y − G̃)/s so that ½Σr² = −log L.
- **Chain starts** (`397-408`): k mod m; `_chol_pd(4 Σ_s) z_k` with Σ_s capped at 0.0025 (`354-357`); clipped to [lo_b, hi_b] (`580-581`, `586`); the count and the raw starts kept. `_chol_pd` (`257-267`) cannot fail on a clipped Σ and has the jitter fallback; numpy's Cholesky reads the lower triangle.
- **First proposal and v2's loops.** `594-595` give 2.38²/4 × the chain's own solution's Σ; `600-613` are v2's byte for byte apart from the acceptance count (`603-604`); the adaptation uses `hist[k, :t+1]`, the start included; the first-500 acceptance is steps 1..500 (`599`, `603-604`, `620`); no off-by-one.
- **The record's start** (`614-621`) carries every field line 68 lists (statuses with nfev and cost `475`; the kept solutions with Σ, Σ_A, Σ_B, eigenvalues and count `468-470`; the starts reaching each `472-473`; chain starts and solution `618`; coordinates clipped `619`; first-500 acceptance `620`; proposal against sample covariance `621`, `518-531`). `under[P]["sigma_P"]`: Σ_A/Σ_B at `kept[0]` of the accepted uniform run (`838-839`), Σ at `kept[0]` of the accepted direct run (`850-851`); `_start_of` (`778-782`); the study's `sigma_P_from_record` (`p5_v3_study.py:566-575`) applies the same rule. On the uniform run and the gradient route `kept[0]` is the best kept solution by construction.
- **The port (b).** After stripping `R.`: `prior_grad_curv`, `residuals`, `fd_jacobian`, `cov_from_jacobian`, `capped_cov`, `clip_cov`, `keep_solutions`, `chain_starts`, `_lbfgs`, `_modes_least_squares`, `_proposal_vs_sample`, `_jsonable` identical except docstrings and two error messages (`442`, `508`); `_modes_gradient` adds the `value_grad_unit` branch (`491-496`; ROADMAP.md:5305-5308); `run_chains` vs `candidate_run_chains` (`p5_v3_study.py:413-471`) differ in the docstring, one error message (`590`), the key `start` in place of `candidate`, no `sampler` tag and no `_DIAG` append (`614-623`). None touches a draw, a solution, a start or a proposal. `test_p5_g2_reference.py:768-800` asserts the equality on the toy for the uniform run, A, B, the gradient route and a value-and-gradient target.

Covered by `test_p5_g2_reference.py:864-890`.

### 9.5 Skeptic, dimension 2, correctness lens

#### D97 skeptic's check, dimension 2 (the sampler change in the code)

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: correctness. The four findings were checked against the files at HEAD (3fad362): p5_g2_reference.py, p5_v3_study.py, p5_g0_pilot.py, p5_g1_emulator.py, p5_v3_transcription.py, the five P5 test files, docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md lines 68 and 75, docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md lines 69-126, ROADMAP.md D95 and D96, and `git diff 59ca589 HEAD -- p5_g2_reference.py`.

Refuted: 0. Standing: 4. Re-graded: 0 (D2-1, D2-2 and D2-3 stay same-round; D2-4 stays a note). Two of the reviewer's line pointers into test_p5_v3_study.py are a dozen lines off (noted under D2-3); the claims they support are true.

##### 2. The findings

###### D2-1 — stands, same-round (confidence high)

The code is as claimed. For a direct run the keep rule orders by the pre-polish log-target: p5_g2_reference.py:434 clips u to [0.01, 0.99], :438 sets logf = −cost + log P(u), :443 calls keep_solutions, which orders by argsort(−logf, stable) at :381. The loop :445-470 walks kept_idx in that order; :453-457 polish each kept solution and recompute lf at the polished point; :468 appends in loop order; :471-476 remap `reached` and return with no re-sort. :851 takes kept[0]["sigma"] as the direct run's sigma_P (:839 the reweighted case). The study's _modes_least_squares (p5_v3_study.py:288-346) is the same code and sigma_P_from_record (:566-575) reads kept[0].

The reordering is possible: for an interior solution the polished log-target is at least the pre-polish one (L-BFGS-B returns its best iterate on ½Σr² − log P), and the gain differs between solutions, so the second kept solution can end above the first. Draft line 68 says "(2) ... in order of log-target from the best (for a direct run the run's own log-target, the prior included)" and later "the accepted direct run's Σ at its best kept solution"; D95 (ROADMAP.md:5320-5322) repeats the phrase without a reading. D96 (ROADMAP.md:5605-5609) confirms the path was not exercised: every one of the 55 runs kept one solution.

One understatement: the effect is not wholly confined to Σ_P and the reading of the record. With m = 3, chain_starts' k mod m (:402) sends chain 3 to kept[0], so the order also decides which solution is doubled; m = 2 and 4 are symmetric. The severity stands: the record's kept list is in line 68's own order, so "best kept solution" = kept[0] is one consistent reading, and the choice between declaring it and re-sorting is the author's. A re-sort would also have to remap `reached` and chain_solution, and would be a departure from "as the study ran it" on a path the study never ran; neither option touches the plan's binding list (P5_v3_plan.md:69-81).

###### D2-2 — stands, same-round (confidence high)

(i) p5_g2_reference.py:30-32 is the docstring's only mention of KAPPA_U_FLOOR and ties it to the recorded Σ_A and Σ_B; step 2 at :25-27 says nothing of the clipped u. The code takes the keep rule's prior density (:438) and curvature (:439) at the clipped u; the constant's comment (:178-179) and draft line 68 state both uses.

(ii) :66-68 says "Σ the given sigma for every solution, a preconditioner only". The code clips the given sigma first (:490, clip_cov) and records its eigenvalues and the count clipped in every kept entry (:510-511); draft line 75 says "clipped as there". In practice a record's Σ_P was already clipped by cov_from_jacobian (:339-346), so the clip changes nothing material, but the docstring is incomplete against line 75 and the code. A docstring: same-round, as graded. The proposed wording follows.

###### D2-3 — stands, same-round (confidence high)

A grep for "merged" over the five P5 test files finds only test_p5_v3_study.py:101 and :297, both asserting n_merged_after_polish == 0. Every m == 2 assertion is on the gradient route (test_p5_v3_study.py:212-213 with _TwoGaussians; test_p5_g2_reference.py:788) or on chain_starts alone (test_p5_v3_study.py:323-332; the reviewer's 335-343 is off by a dozen lines). keep_solutions' unit tests (:187-205; four kept at :194) exercise the keep rule on its own, not _modes_least_squares' merge. LinearEm (test_p5_g2_reference.py:26-40) is a + Mθ, a quadratic cost with one minimum, and every least-squares-route assertion on m is m == 1 (test_p5_g2_reference.py:145; test_p5_v3_study.py:91, 289, 581) or m >= 1 (:395). D96 (ROADMAP.md:5605-5609): the 55 transcription runs each kept one solution.

So the merge at p5_g2_reference.py:459-473 and a least-squares run with m >= 2 are reasoned about but never run by a test. On reading, the code is sound: pos_map receives every position of kept_idx (:462, :464), `reached` is remapped through it (:471), n_starts_reached sums the merged starts (:472-473) and starts_elsewhere is unchanged by a merge (:474). A test is outside the plan's binding list, so the proposed fix is in bounds. The brief's "weak test": same-round.

###### D2-4 — stands, note (confidence high)

The raises are at p5_g2_reference.py:441-442, :507-508 and :589-590 (the last says the run is recorded as an error). launch's take() (:1158-1164) writes a {"kind": "task_error"} line and continues; both pool paths catch Exception (:1177-1181, :1187-1192). read_jsonl (p5_g0_pilot.py:608-614) keeps only header, launch and task lines, so the pair is absent from tasks and g2_report refuses at :1305-1307; main calls g2_report straight after run_set (:1476-1479), so a pilot invocation with such a pair ends in a refusal, not a recorded reference failure.

The v2 counterfactual holds for a target non-finite everywhere: mh_step's comparison (:287-288) is False with nan, or with −inf − (−inf), chains stay at their starts, W = 0 makes split_rhat inf (:648), accept fails, the repeat fails and reference_pair records reference_failure (:830-833). G̃'s evaluate_many (p5_g1_emulator.py:312-324) is a barycentric tensor interpolant on finite values that raises outside the box, and residuals() clips x to [0, 1] (:314), so on G̃ the start can raise only through a numerical pathology such as an SVD that does not converge; not expected. One addition: on resume the pair is re-run (launch's done set, :1148-1149, omits it) and the same deterministic generator raises again, so the set can never be reported without the author's intervention. A note, as graded.

##### 3. Own observations

- p5_g2_reference.py:34-35 (docstring step 4) says the starts are clipped "to the box ([ε, 1 − ε] in H for a direct run)"; the code also sets [ε, 1 − ε] in H for any run with a target (:579-580) and narrows to target.bounds_unit (:489-490, :585). A docstring omission of D2-2's kind, for the same edit. same-round.
- p5_g2_reference.py:397-407 and :591: with m = 3 the keep order decides which solution gets two of the four chains (a nuance for D2-1's entry). note.
- p5_g2_reference.py:1148-1149 with :1158-1164: a pair whose start raised is re-run on every resume from the same deterministic generator, raises again, and g2_report refuses every time (a nuance for D2-4's entry). note.
- p5_g2_reference.py:359-363 against :343-346: a record's Σ_P whose eigenvalue was set to exactly 1/12 or 1e-10 is re-decomposed after the JSON round trip, and round-off can put it a few ulp outside the range, so n_clipped in a gradient-route record can count an eigenvalue cov_from_jacobian already clipped. Harmless to the run; only the reading of n_clipped is affected. note, low confidence that it occurs.

Checked in the same lines and found sound: the stream order (:577-578 then mh_step's z then u, :281-289); the keep rule's constants (:180, :370-394); Σ from the singular values with the √κ row (:339-346); the clipping and the count (:345-346, :359-363); the polish's objective, gradient sign and bounds (:448-455, :409-413); KAPPA_U_FLOOR at the keep rule (:434-439) and the recorded Σ_A and Σ_B (:465-469) but not at the direct run's own Σ (:467); the chain starts with the cap 0.0025 and the clip count (:397-407); the first proposal (:594); the acceptance over the first 500 steps (:600-605); every field of `start` listed by line 68 (:613-621); header's and run_set's `lengths["start"]` against launch's comparison keys (:1076, :1222-1226, :1143) and the transcription check's check_constants and transcription_header (p5_v3_transcription.py:113-125, :165-191); _jsonable's handling of numpy types and non-finite values (:528-543) on every field of the record. The one intended port difference (value_grad_unit, :491-497) is as D95 declares (ROADMAP.md:5305-5308); no other difference between run_chains and candidate_run_chains was found.

##### 4. Run beyond reading

none

### 9.6 Skeptic, dimension 2, consequence lens

#### D97, dimension 2 (the sampler change in the code): the skeptic's verdicts

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: consequence. Each finding was taken as true, checked against the files at HEAD (3fad362) until it was seen not to be plainly false, and then weighed: does it break a binding rule of the plan, make a declaration false or incomplete, change what a run after the freeze will do or record, touch the sealed test set, or leave a reader unable to reproduce a step; and is the severity and the proposed fix right.

Of the four findings, none is refuted, four stand, none is re-graded (D2-1, D2-2 and D2-3 stay same-round; D2-4 stays note). One observation of the reviewer's own was found that the findings do not carry (a reading of the plan's \"the best's\", already made explicit in the draft; a note). Nothing was found in the lines read that bears on a binding rule of the plan, on the declarations of draft lines 7, 9, 15 and 200, on a run after the freeze, or on the sealed seeds.

The review was read-only: git, sed, grep, diff and one standard-library json command on the transcription report (section 4). No repository module was imported, no script or test run, no .npz opened.

##### 2. The findings

###### D2-1 — stands; same-round

The claim is verified. In `_modes_least_squares` (p5_g2_reference.py:418-475) the keep rule orders the solutions by the pre-polish log-target, for a direct run −cost plus log P at u clipped to [0.01, 0.99] (:434-438), and `keep_solutions` returns `kept_idx` in that order (:381). The loop at :445-470 polishes each kept solution (:446-455), recomputes `lf` at the polished point with the unclipped x_H (:457), merges a solution polished to within 0.01 of an earlier kept entry (:459-463) and appends in `kept_idx` order (:468-470); nothing before the return at :474 re-sorts `kept`. So `kept[0]` is the first in the keep rule's order, and after the polish another kept entry can carry the larger recorded `logf` when m ≥ 2. `reference_pair` reads `kept[0][\"sigma\"]` as the direct run's Σ_P (:851). The uniform run has no polish and the gradient route keeps the order (:509-512), so the ambiguity is the direct run's alone. The study's code is the same (p5_v3_study.py:313-344; `sigma_P_from_record` :566-575). The draft says \"its best kept solution\" and \"in order of log-target from the best\" (P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68); both readings of \"best\" are open, and the keep rule's order is one of them.

Consequence: Σ_P is a preconditioner only (draft :75; p5_g3_calibrators.py:422-428), so no posterior, gate figure or score changes under either reading, and the record's `logf` fields show which entry is first. The path was not exercised (ROADMAP.md:5605-5609; the transcription report's 55 runs). One nuance the finding understates: the keep order also fixes chain k's solution, k mod m (:402-405, :593), hence which z-draw perturbs which solution and, at m = 3, which solution gets two chains. That does not bear on the present agreement of text and code (the draft's step 4 uses the same order), but it confirms that the second proposed fix, a re-sort, changes the chains on that path and would need a declaration, as the finding says. The smallest fix is the first: declare the reading in line 68. Same-round is the right grade: a wording clause, not a plain disagreement, with a consequence confined to a preconditioner.

###### D2-2 — stands; same-round

(i) The module docstring's step 2 (:25-27) states the keep rule without the clipped u, and :30-32 attaches the clipping to the recorded Σ_A and Σ_B only, whereas the code clips u for the keep rule's density and curvature (:434-438), the constant's comment says so (:178-179) and draft :68 states both uses. (ii) The docstring's :65-68 says \"Σ the given sigma for every solution\", whereas `_modes_gradient` clips the given sigma's eigenvalues by `clip_cov` (:490; the function's docstring at :360-363 says \"as the start clips its own\") and records `eig` and `n_clipped` on each kept entry (:510-511), as draft :75 says (\"clipped as there\"). Both phrases fall short as claimed. Consequence: the docstring only; no run or declaration changes, and since the script hash list is appended at the freeze commit (draft :7) the edit needs no declaration. The proposed wording matches the code. Same-round is right.

###### D2-3 — stands; same-round

Verified from the tests. `n_merged_after_polish` is asserted only at test_p5_v3_study.py:101 and :297, both equal to 0. The m = 2 cases are the gradient route on `_TwoGaussians` (test_p5_v3_study.py:207-213; test_p5_g2_reference.py:784-788) and `chain_starts` on its own (test_p5_v3_study.py:323-333); the least-squares route is asserted at m = 1 (test_p5_v3_study.py:91, :289; test_p5_g2_reference.py:145), and the toy-job assertion `c[\"m\"] >= 1` (test_p5_v3_study.py:395) runs `LinearEm` through a toy file (:359, :688). Every emulator in the tests is `LinearEm`, a + Mθ (test_p5_g2_reference.py:26-40; the fixture :245-253; test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:29), whose least-squares problem is convex with one minimum: no test can reach m ≥ 2 or the merge on the least-squares route, so the merge and remap (p5_g2_reference.py:459-473), chain k at solution k mod m with several least-squares solutions (:397-408, :593) and a first proposal per chain from different Σ (:594) are never run by a test. The transcription check did not reach them either (ROADMAP.md:5605-5609; the report's `n_sampler_runs_compared` is 55 and D96 states every run kept one solution).

Consequence: a post-freeze run with a bimodal or valley-shaped likelihood takes these paths (the plan expects and reports such pairs, P5_v3_plan.md:107-109), and a defect there would show only after the freeze. Same-round is right; the fix is a test, outside the frozen text and within the plan (the tests build toy objects only, draft :15), and the figures it would assert follow from the code read.

###### D2-4 — stands; note

Verified. The start raises at :441-442, :507-508 and :589-590 (the last unreachable in practice: `keep_solutions` keeps the solution of largest finite mass unless one within 0.01 of it is already kept, :379-388, so m ≥ 1 whenever any logf is finite). `launch`'s `take` writes a `task_error` line and continues (:1158-1166) from the except clauses at :1180 and :1190; `read_jsonl` keeps header, launch and task kinds only (p5_g0_pilot.py:592-614), so the pair is absent from `tasks`, not among `done` (:1140-1141), re-run on a resume, and `g2_report` refuses a set with a pair missing (:1281-1290 and the `rec is None` check). With a finite emulator the raise at :442 cannot fire (scipy's `least_squares` refuses non-finite residuals at the initial point; finite residuals give a finite cost; a direct run adds log P at u clipped to [0.01, 0.99]), so the path needs non-finite emulator output. G̃'s finiteness could not be checked here (loading it is forbidden); D94's 36 pairs and D96's 55 runs produced no error line, which is consistent. The v2 counterfactual holds by reading of the removed lines in `git diff 59ca589 HEAD` (uniform x, 2.38²/4 · (1/12) · I): on a non-finite target `mh_step` never accepts (:289-290), `split_rhat` is inf when W = 0 (:646-648), `accept` is False (:691-696), the repeat fails and `reference_pair` records `reference_failure` (:823). The message at :590 declares the choice. Note is right; nothing required.

##### 3. Own observations

- note — P5_v3_plan.md:103 says the Laplace mass must be \"within log 10⁴ of the best's\", readable as the best-by-log-target solution's mass; the draft (:68, \"the largest mass among the solutions\") and the code (:379, :381-388) take the largest mass, the only reading under which the plan's own justification (\"any mode dropped then holds under 10⁻⁴ of the mass\") is true, and test_p5_v3_study.py:200-205 documents it. The draft is explicit, so nothing changes; since the plan is never edited, D97's record could carry the reading in one line.

Checked and found sound in the lines read (no finding): the stream order (:577-578; `mh_step`'s z then u, :282, :288); the constants (:172-182) against draft :68 and the study's (p5_v3_study.py:103-114); Σ from the singular values, Σ_P from J stacked with √κ e₁ᵀ, the clipping to [1e-10, 1/12] and the count (:337-351); the polish objective, gradient sign and bounds (:447-455, :581); KAPPA_U_FLOOR applied at :434-438 and :465-466 and not at :467, as draft :68 says; the chain starts with the cap and the clip count (:354-358, :397-408); the first proposal per chain (:594); the acceptance over the first min(burn, 500) steps (:600-604); the record's fields (:620-631) against draft :68; `_run_with_repeat` (:785-800); `header`'s `start` and `run_set`'s `lengths.start` (:1077, :1226) against `launch`'s comparison of `lengths` (:1140) and the transcription check's `check_constants` and `transcription_header` (p5_v3_transcription.py:113-125, :165-191): a resume of a G2 record at other start constants is refused with `lengths` named; the port: a mechanical diff of p5_g2_reference.py:304-547 against p5_v3_study.py:184-430 (R. prefixes stripped) shows only `clip_cov`'s position, one docstring word, the error-message texts, a \"line 75\" cross-reference and the `value_grad_unit` branch (:491, :494-496; D95's one declared addition, ROADMAP.md:5305-5308); `run_chains` (:564-627) against `candidate_run_chains` (p5_v3_study.py:398-470) differs only in the diagnostics key and tag and the `_DIAG` append; `_jsonable` (:534-549) turns inf and nan into None and numpy scalars into Python numbers, and no numpy type or key collision in the start reaches the record.

##### 4. Run beyond reading

One command, standard library only:

`py -3 -I -c \"import json; r=json.load(open('output/p5v3_transcription_report.json', encoding='utf-8')); print(sorted(r.keys())); print({k: r[k] for k in r if isinstance(r[k], (int, float, bool, str)) and len(str(r[k])) < 120})\"` — to check the counts D96 states (36 pairs, 55 sampler runs and 55 sample arrays compared, `reproduced` true); no new figure was computed, and no .npz was opened.

### 9.7 Reviewer, dimension 3: the ratio estimator's sampler

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review, D97 — dimension 3: the ratio estimator's sampler, the gradient route

Read-only, at `main` 3fad362. Files read in full or in the ranges named: `p5_g3_calibrators.py` (the docstring and constants, `nre_target`, `NRETarget`, `_trim_start`, `nre_posterior`, `pilot_surfaces`, `predict_rows`, `scoring_projection`, `run_g3`, `scored_networks`, `score_set`, `main`) and `git diff 59ca589 HEAD -- p5_g3_calibrators.py`; `p5_g2_reference.py` (the docstring and constants, `prior_logpdf`, `log_target`, `mh_step`, `prior_grad_curv`, `clip_cov`, `keep_solutions`, `chain_starts`, `_lbfgs`, `_modes_least_squares`, `_modes_gradient`, `run_chains`, `_run_with_repeat`, `reference_pair`, `project_cost`, `g2_status`, `require_g2`); `p5_v3_study.py` `_modes_gradient` and `StandInTarget`; `p5_stage2.py` `load_refs`, `load_scores`, `measures` and the completeness checks; the draft's lines 68, 75, 93, 101, 137, 140-144; the plan's lines 28-126 and 201; ROADMAP D91, D93 (the gradient route), D94 (its outcome), D95 (5203-5545) and D96 (5546-5700); `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py` (lines 1-60, 100-118, 170-420) and `test_p5_g2_reference.py` 736-800; `.github/workflows/tests.yml`, `requirements.txt`, `requirements-p5.txt`; the two v2 review records for the form. No script, test or pricer was run; no module imported; no record under `output/` opened; nothing written in the repository.

##### 1. Verdict

Freeze after one must-fix and one wording fix. The gradient route as written does what line 75 says: the chains sample the float32 network's logit plus log P(H) through `nre_target`'s callable, only the optimiser sees the float64 copy, the gradient is exact in unit coordinates with the prior's analytic part and no Jacobian factor, Σ_P reaches the start from the reference record under the training prior and serves, clipped as the reference clips its own, as a preconditioner only, the generator and the draw order are the reference's, the lengths G2's report fixes reach G3's predictions, the scoring and the cost projection, and the record keeps what line 75 promises. One must-fix: `score_set` does not remove a failed reference as `pilot_surfaces` does, so the scoring of a set holding a reference failure, a declared outcome under lines 101 and 144, stops at that pair with an uncaught refusal from `NRETarget` and cannot complete on resume; line 75 says nothing of such a pair. One same-round: line 75's "H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces" reads as the opposite of the code's bounds ε inside the faces. Three notes, each already declared in substance: the torch test's value tolerance against D95's words, the lateness of the Σ_P refusal, and the route's being unexercised on a trained network with CI never running the torch tests.

##### 2. Findings

**NRE-1 — must-fix — The scoring stops at a pair whose reference failed: `score_set` hands `NRETarget` no Σ_P.**
*Claim.* `score_set` builds its rows from every record of the set at the level with no status filter (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:887`), reads Σ_P as `((r.get("under") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get("sigma_P")` (`:906`) and passes it to `nre_posterior`. A record with status `reference_failure` has no `under[P]`: `reference_pair` initialises `under` to `{}` (`p5_g2_reference.py:817`) and returns before filling it on a uniform failure (`:823`) or, on a direct failure under one prior, with only the other prior's figures (`:845`). Σ_P is then `None`, `NRETarget` raises `ValueError` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:432-434`), uncaught, after the header (`:880`) and any ensemble rows (`:895-900`) are written; on resume the pair is in `todo` again and the run stops at it again. A reference failure on the test set is an anticipated outcome: line 101 lets P5 go on with up to 10% of a level's pairs failed and Stage 1 removes them from the count, and line 144 removes a failed G3-pilot reference from the gate's set. `pilot_surfaces` removes failures for G3 (`:513`); `score_set` does not, for `--score test` or `--score g3`. Line 75 presumes every scored pair carries a Σ_P under the training prior and says nothing of a pair whose reference failed. D95's declaration covers only a record made by v2's script (ROADMAP.md:5349-5351). Stage 2 never needs a score row of a failed pair: `load_refs` drops them before `measures` (`p5_stage2.py:217-220`) and the completeness check is by (calibrator, prior, level, slot) presence (`:464-467`, `:843-849`), so skipping them is safe. No test scores a set holding a failed reference (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:351-354`, three records, all `ok`).
*Evidence.* `p5_g3_calibrators.py:887, :906, :432-434, :880, :895-900, :513`; `p5_g2_reference.py:817, :823, :845`; draft lines 75, 101, 144; `p5_stage2.py:217-220, :464-467, :843-849`; ROADMAP.md:5349-5351; `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:351-354`.
*Bears on.* Draft line 75 (the Σ_P clause) and lines 101 and 144; the plan's Decision 3 ("the reference precedes the scoring of every pair", which a failed reference does not); the runs `--score test` and `--score g3` after the freeze; precedence (line 137: the script governs where the text is silent).
*Proposed fix.* In `score_set` restrict the rows to records with status `ok` (for both calibrators, Stage 2 reading no row of a failed pair; or, keeping the ensemble's rows as v2 wrote them, guard the ratio estimator's branch alone) and record the pairs skipped, by label, in the header or a line; add a test that scores a set holding one failed reference and asserts the ratio estimator skips it and the rest are scored. In line 75 (its record clause, or beside line 101) one clause: a pair whose reference failed is not scored by the ratio estimator, its pair being outside every measure by Stage 1. The wording is the author's, since the plan lists the scoring among what v3 does not change while Decision 3 names `p5_g3_calibrators.py` and the record as what the route changes.

**NRE-2 — same-round — "H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces" reads as the opposite of the bounds the code sets.**
*Claim.* Line 75 writes the gradient route's bounds as "H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces", which reads as |x_H − face| ≤ ε. The code bounds H to [ε, 1 − ε] (`NRETarget.bounds_unit`, `p5_g3_calibrators.py:442`; `run_chains`, `p5_g2_reference.py:579-581`; `_modes_gradient`, `:488-489`), which line 68 states correctly as "H's bounds ε = 1e-6 inside the faces". `NRETarget`'s docstring (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:428`) and D95's account (ROADMAP.md:5340) carry the same phrase.
*Evidence.* Draft line 75 against line 68; `p5_g3_calibrators.py:428, :442`; `p5_g2_reference.py:488-489, :579-581`; ROADMAP.md:5340.
*Bears on.* Draft line 75, a line the plan lists as changed (item 6; Decision 3); the frozen text's reading of the optimiser's bounds.
*Proposed fix.* Line 75: "H's bounds ε = 1e-6 inside the faces, as there"; the docstring at `p5_g3_calibrators.py:428` likewise. The merged entry stands; D97 may note the reading.

**NRE-3 — note — The torch test's value check is at 1e-3 relative, which D95 describes as "within float32 rounding".**
*Claim.* The test checks the float32 callable against the float64 value with `abs(v32 − f) < 1e-3 · max(1, |f|)` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:285-286`); D95 says "the value within float32 rounding of the network as trained" (ROADMAP.md:5360-5361). Float32 evaluation of the design's trunk differs from float64 by orders of magnitude less than 1e-3, so the bound would pass a far larger disagreement than rounding; it still catches a missing prior term or a wrong input (both O(1)), and the prior's part is checked separately to 1e-12 (`:287-291`).
*Evidence.* `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:285-291`; ROADMAP.md:5360-5361.
*Bears on.* The strength of the test guarding line 75's "the stationary distribution ... is unchanged"; D95's account of the test.
*Proposed fix.* Tighten the tolerance to what one run in `.venv-p5` shows the toy trunk needs (1e-5 · max(1, |f|) is a candidate), and let D97 record that the test's bound is the figure in the file, not float32's rounding.

**NRE-4 — note — A record without Σ_P is refused late and by a traceback.**
*Claim.* The refusal lives in `NRETarget`'s constructor (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:432-434`), reached in G3 only after the cell's networks at n and 2n are trained (`run_g3`: training at `:712` precedes `predicted` at `:722`, up to two runs of the 4 h budget) and in the scoring after the header (`:880`) and any ensemble rows (`:895-900`) are written; it is a `ValueError`, not a `SystemExit`, so the run ends in a traceback whose message names the cause. D95's "refused at the first posterior" is accurate. With v3's reference script always writing Σ_P (`p5_g2_reference.py:837-851`), the path is reached only by a foreign record or by a failed reference (NRE-1).
*Evidence.* `p5_g3_calibrators.py:432-434, :712, :722, :880, :895-900`; `p5_g2_reference.py:837-851`; ROADMAP.md:5349-5351.
*Bears on.* The runs `--g3` and `--score` after the freeze; D95's declaration.
*Proposed fix.* A decision for the author: a check before any training or network load (`require_pilot`, or the top of `score_set`, over the set's accepted records: Σ_P present and 4 × 4 under both priors) raising `SystemExit`, keeping `NRETarget`'s refusal as the last guard; or leave as declared once NRE-1 removes the failed-reference case.

**NRE-5 — note — The gradient route is unexercised on a trained ReLU network, and CI never runs the torch tests.**
*Claim.* What exercises the route: the torch test's gradient check on an untrained random MLP of the design's trunk (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:240-303`); the route through `nre_posterior` on a parameter-free quadratic module whose one mode L-BFGS-B reaches to 1e-5 (`:206-222`); the two-Gaussian target with m = 2 and the keep rule under a constant Σ (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:784-788`); the study's 72 stand-ins with finite-difference gradients (ROADMAP.md:5138-5144). The transcription check took the least-squares route in all 55 runs (D96). No test or run has put L-BFGS-B on a piecewise-linear logit plus log P(H); D95 point (6) declares this (ROADMAP.md:5520-5526) and the recorded statuses and m are the diagnostic `_trim_start` keeps (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:471-476`). CI installs `requirements.txt` only (`.github/workflows/tests.yml`: numpy, scipy, matplotlib), so `_needs_torch` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:24`) skips both torch tests there; the only run is the author's in `.venv-p5` (ROADMAP.md:5364 and D95's count, 17 passed).
*Evidence.* `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:24, :206-222, :240-303`; `test_p5_g2_reference.py:784-799`; `.github/workflows/tests.yml`; `requirements.txt`; ROADMAP.md:5138-5144, :5364, :5520-5526, D96.
*Bears on.* Line 75's "the modes are found by L-BFGS-B" on a real network, first seen at G3's pilot; the review's own reach.
*Proposed fix.* None required: a reading to carry into D97. If a guard before G3 is wanted, a torch test that trains the toy ratio estimator for a few epochs and runs `nre_posterior` on it, asserting only a status, finite kept log-targets and recorded optimiser statuses, would show the route runs on a ReLU logit without claiming convergence.
