### 9.8 Skeptic, dimension 3, correctness lens

#### D97 skeptic's check — Dimension 3, the ratio estimator's sampler and the gradient route

Repository `C:\\Users\\micha\\OneDrive\\Documents\\roughvollab`, HEAD 3fad362 (confirmed by `git rev-parse HEAD`; no modified tracked file). Read-only; nothing run but `git show`, `git diff`, `sed`, `grep`, `awk`, `cat`.

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: correctness, each claim read against the files at HEAD and against the other side of every comparison (the draft's lines 68, 75, 101, 137, 144; the plan's lines 69-77 and 120-126; ROADMAP D93 to D96; the tests, read and not run).

Of the five findings, **none is refuted, five stand, none is re-graded**: NRE-1 must-fix, NRE-2 same-round, NRE-3 note, NRE-4 note, NRE-5 note. Two findings carry a small inaccuracy that does not touch the verdict (NRE-1's \"has no under[P]\" is over-general; NRE-5 cites D94 for a fact stated in D93). Four own observations are recorded, one of which (the test docstring) extends NRE-3 and is same-round; the others are notes.

##### 2. The findings

###### NRE-1 — the scoring stops at a pair whose reference failed (must-fix; stands, high confidence)

`p5_g3_calibrators.py:887` builds the rows from every record of the set at the level, with no status filter; `:906` reads Σ_P as `((r.get(\"under\") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get(\"sigma_P\")`; `nre_posterior` constructs `NRETarget(run, y, sigma_P)` at `:488`, and `:432-434` raise `ValueError` on `None`. No `try`/`except` lies between (`:479-498`, `:850-912`), so the run ends in a traceback. The header is written at `:879-882` before the loop; `run_g3` builds the cells prior → level → kind with `CALIBRATORS = (\"ensemble\", \"nre\")` (`:136`, `:692-698`) and `train_seeds` keeps that order (`:797`, `:810`), so the ensemble rows of the same cell (`:894-903`) precede the stop. On resume `todo` (`:891`) holds the pair again.

`p5_g2_reference.py:817` initialises `under` to `{}`; `:822-825` return on the uniform run's failure with `under` empty. The reviewer's \"a record with status reference_failure has no under[P]\" is over-general: a direct-run failure under B (`:842-847`) returns with `under[\"A\"]` already written (`:838-840` or `:850-851`), so under prior A the ratio estimator samples the failed pair and writes a row Stage 2 never reads, and under prior B the run stops. The consequence, a stop at the first prior lacking Σ_P, is unchanged.

Line 101 lets P5 go on with up to 10% of a level's pairs failed; line 144 removes a failed G3-pilot reference from the gate's set; `pilot_surfaces` removes failures (`:513-515`) and `score_set` does not. Line 75 presumes a Σ_P \"from its reference record under the training prior\" and is silent on a failed reference; D95's declaration (ROADMAP.md:5350-5351) covers a record made by v2's script only. Stage 2 drops failures in `load_refs` (`p5_stage2.py:217-220`), `measures` iterates the accepted pairs (`:457-467`), and `load_scores` keys rows by (calibrator, prior, q, pair) and `present` by (calibrator, prior, j, q) (`:268-276`), so a skipped failed pair breaks no check. The only test that scores a set holds three records all `\"ok\"` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:348-353`, `:381`). At 59ca589 `nre_posterior` took no Σ_P (the diff), so the crash path is v3's.

The severity holds: a bug on a path a run after the freeze may take; (A)'s 36/36 on the pilot does not exclude a failure among 1,200 test pairs. The fix follows from the claim. Its narrowest form guards the ratio estimator's branch alone, since the plan lists \"the scoring\" among what v3 does not change (`P5_v3_plan.md:69-77`) while Decision 3 names `p5_g3_calibrators.py` and the record as what the route changes (`:125`); the reviewer rightly leaves the wording to the author.

###### NRE-2 — \"H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces\" (same-round; stands, high confidence)

Line 75: \"L-BFGS-B (ftol = 1e-10, gtol = 1e-5, maxiter = 200; H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces)\". Line 68: \"H's bounds ε = 1e-6 inside the faces, where both priors' densities vanish\". The code bounds H to [ε, 1 − ε]: `NRETarget.bounds_unit` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:442`), `run_chains` (`p5_g2_reference.py:579-581`), `_modes_gradient` (`:488-489`, `np.maximum`/`np.minimum` against the target's bounds), `EPS_H = 1e-6` (`:177`). The docstring at `:428` and ROADMAP.md:5340 carry the phrase. \"Within ε of the faces\" reads as the ε-neighbourhood of the faces, the opposite of what is set; line 68's wording is unambiguous. Line 75 is a changed line, so the fix is within bounds.

###### NRE-3 — the value check at 1e-3 relative (note; stands, medium confidence)

`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:285-286`: `assert abs(v32 - f) < 1e-3 * max(1.0, abs(f))`. ROADMAP.md:5360-5361: \"the value within float32 rounding of the network as trained\". The test's own docstring (`:244-245`) carries the same phrase, uncited. The prior's part is checked at 1e-12 relative (`:287-291`). The magnitude claim (float32 evaluation of the 39 → 128 → 128 → 128 → 1 trunk differing from float64 by orders of magnitude less than 1e-3) is a reasonable estimate from the float32 casts at `:410` and `:415` and three 128-wide accumulations at ~6e-8 relative each, but it cannot be verified from the files without running the test, which the rules forbid; hence medium confidence. The fix is proportionate.

###### NRE-4 — the refusal is late and by traceback (note; stands, high confidence)

The refusal is the constructor's `ValueError` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:432-434`). In `run_g3` the networks at n and 2n are trained (`:710-713`) before `predicted(n)` (`:722`); more precedes the refusal than the claim says, since the ensemble cell's whole ladder at the same (prior, level) runs before the ratio estimator's first posterior (`:136`, `:692-698`), which strengthens the finding. In the scoring the header (`:879-882`) and the ensemble rows (`:894-903`) precede. D95's \"refused at the first posterior\" (ROADMAP.md:5350-5351) is accurate. v3's reference writes `sigma_P` in both branches (`p5_g2_reference.py:838-839`, `:850-851`) whenever the status is ok, so the path is reached only by a foreign record or a failed reference. `require_pilot` exists (`:622`) for the check the fix proposes.

###### NRE-5 — the route unexercised on a trained ReLU network; CI never runs the torch tests (note; stands, high confidence)

The exercises are as listed: the gradient check on an untrained `make_mlp(39, 1)` after `torch.manual_seed(7)` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:246-248`, `:261-283`); `nre_posterior` on the parameter-free `Quadratic` with the mode checked to 1e-5 (`:203-217`); `_TwoGaussians` with Σ = sd² I constant (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:738-744`) giving m == 2 (`:784-788`); the study's 72 stand-in runs (ROADMAP.md:5138-5144), whose gradient is the bounded 2-point finite difference by D93 at ROADMAP.md:4868-4871 (not at the cited 5138-5144, a citation gap); D96's \"Every one of the 55 runs took the least-squares route\". D95's point (6) (ROADMAP.md:5520-5526) declares the gap; `_trim_start` keeps the statuses and m (`:472-476`). CI installs `requirements.txt` only (numpy, scipy, matplotlib) on Python 3.12; `_needs_torch` (`:24`) marks six tests in the file, not \"both\" (the reviewer means the two tests of the brief); D95 records 6 skipped (torch) and 17 passed in `.venv-p5` (ROADMAP.md:5449-5452), and 5364 says \"CI skips the torch tests\". Nothing refutes the claim.

##### 3. Own observations

1. **note** — `p5_g2_reference.py:832-847`: a direct-run failure under B leaves `under[\"A\"]` filled, so under prior A `score_set` samples the failed pair and writes a row Stage 2 never reads (`p5_stage2.py:217-220`, `:457-467`), while under B the run stops. The fix by status ok covers both; a fix by Σ_P present alone would not skip the prior-A row.
2. **note** — `p5_g3_calibrators.py:479-498` with `p5_g2_reference.py:507-508` and `:589-590`: the start's `RuntimeError`s (no finite solution; no solution kept) are not caught in `nre_posterior` or `score_set`, whereas the reference's launcher records a worker's exception as a `task_error` line and continues (`p5_g2_reference.py:1158-1163`, `:1174-1178`). On a finite network within [ε, 1 − ε] × [0, 1]³ both are unreachable (log P(H) finite inside; `keep_solutions` keeps the best finite mass, `:381-391`), so a note only.
3. **same-round, extends NRE-3** — `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:244-245`: the test's docstring states \"the value within float32 rounding of the network as trained\"; it should state the figure the assertion at `:286` enforces, whatever the author sets.
4. **note (citation)** — NRE-5's finite-difference statement belongs to D93 at ROADMAP.md:4868-4871; the cited 5138-5144 (D94) does not say it.

Checked and found sound in the same lines, no finding: `NRETarget.__call__` is `nre_target`'s callable, the float32 network under `torch.no_grad()` (`:410-419`, `:436`, `:444-445`), so the chains' stationary distribution is line 75's and only the optimiser sees the float64 copy; `value_grad_unit` feeds unit coordinates straight into the network, whose θ input is the mapped [0, 1]⁴ (`:415`, `:451`), so no Jacobian factor is needed or doubly applied, and the prior's d/du log P(u) (`p5_g2_reference.py:303-311`) is added once to g[0] (`:456`) with the value at H = H_LO + u·H_W (`:455`); `copy.deepcopy(...).double().eval()` leaves the trained network untouched (`:438`; the test asserts the dtypes at `:252`); the refusal of a missing or mis-shaped Σ_P (`:432-434`, `:440-441`); the generator `default_rng([107, p, q, i, j])` with j 1-based from `run[\"j\"]` (`train_calibrator` `:354`, `jj = int(j)`; restored by `load_run` `:371-385`), the records' j 0-based (`pilot_surfaces` `:508`, `score_set` `:887`), line 75's \"counted from 1 as defined below\", and the test's `[107, 1, 1, 0, 1]` (`:221`); the draws' order is the reference's since the same `run_chains` runs (`p5_g2_reference.py:577-578`, `:600-613`); `_trim_start` keeps m, the kept solutions' x and log-target and the optimiser's statuses (`:472-476`), nothing line 75 promises lost; Σ_P's path from `reference_pair` (`:838-839` the uniform run's Σ_A/Σ_B at kept[0]; `:850-851` the direct run's Σ at kept[0]) through `pilot_surfaces` (`:516-518`) and `predict_rows` (`:561`) / `score_set` (`:906`) under the cell's training prior; the doubled lengths reach both G3 and the scoring (`main` `:950-951`, `post_kw` from `require_g2`, whose `g2_status` reads the passing attempt's `lengths` at `:1393-1394`; `run_g3` `:706`; `score_set` `:906-907`), with rhat_max and ess_min at the reference's defaults; `clip_cov` (`:360-368`) clips the eigenvalues to [1e-10, 1/12] as `cov_from_jacobian` does (`:337-351`), \"clipped as there\"; the keep rule with a constant Σ reduces to log f within log 10⁴ (`:381`, `:384-391`) and the distinctness rule still applies; the generator's q is the training seed value (6 for a replacement, `load_run`'s meta), matching line 75's \"training seed q\".

### 9.9 Skeptic, dimension 3, consequence lens

#### D97 skeptic's verdicts — dimension 3, the ratio estimator's sampler (lens: consequence)

Repository `C:\Users\micha\OneDrive\Documents\roughvollab`, HEAD `3fad362`, tracked tree clean. Everything below was read from `git show HEAD:<path>` (and `git show 59ca589:...` for v2's draft), with `git diff 59ca589 HEAD -- p5_g3_calibrators.py`. No script, test or pricer was run; no module imported; no `.npz` opened; no generator created; nothing written in the repository.

##### 1. Lens and summary

For each finding the claim was checked against the files, then weighed for consequence: a binding rule of the plan broken, a declaration made false or incomplete, a change to what a run after the freeze does or records, the sealed set, or a reader's ability to reproduce a step from the frozen text and scripts.

Of five findings, **none is refuted and none is re-graded**: NRE-1 stands at must-fix, NRE-2 at same-round, NRE-3, NRE-4 and NRE-5 at note. One finding, NRE-5, contains a false supporting sentence that D97 should not copy: the torch test does run L-BFGS-B on a piecewise-linear logit plus log P(H) (an untrained network); the accurate claim is that no *trained* network has seen the route and no convergence is asserted.

##### 2. The findings

###### NRE-1 — the scoring stops at a pair whose reference failed (must-fix; stands)

Verified. `score_set` builds a level's rows with no status filter (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:887`) and reads Σ_P with `((r.get("under") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get("sigma_P")` (`:906`); `nre_posterior` constructs `NRETarget` before `_run_with_repeat` runs and before any line is appended for the pair (`:487-488`); `NRETarget` raises `ValueError` on `None` (`:432-434`); nothing in `score_set` (`:904-911`) or `main` (`:976-986`) catches it. A `reference_failure` record has `under = {}` when the uniform run fails (`p5_g2_reference.py:817`, `:822-825`), and `under` filled for the first prior only when a direct run under the second fails (`:841-847`), so Σ_P under at least one training prior is missing on every failed record. The header (`:879-882`) and the ensemble's rows (`:894-903`; `CALIBRATORS` puts ensemble first, `:136`) are written before the crash; on resume the pair is in `todo` again (`:891`) and the run stops again.

Consequence. Line 101 lets P5 go on with up to 10% of a level's test pairs failed and line 144 removes a failed G3-pilot reference from the gate's set, so a failed reference is an anticipated state of both sets the scoring reads. `pilot_surfaces` skips failures (`:513-515`); `score_set` does not. Line 75 presumes Σ_P for every scored pair and says nothing of a failed one. D95's declaration covers only a record made by v2's script (`ROADMAP.md:5350-5351`). In v2 the ratio estimator ran on a failed pair harmlessly (the diff shows `nre_target` needed no Σ_P); the crash is v3's. Stage 2 never needs the row: `load_refs` drops failed pairs (`p5_stage2.py:216-221`), `measures` iterates over accepted pairs (`:458`) and the completeness checks are per (calibrator, prior, level, slot) (`:463-467`, `:843-852`). The scoring test's three records are all `ok` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:351-354`). A bug on a path `--score test` and `--score g3` take whenever any reference fails: must-fix.

The fix. Guarding the ratio estimator's branch alone on `status == "ok"` is the smallest change and keeps the ensemble's rows as v2 wrote them; recording the skipped labels is cheap; a test with one failed record is warranted. The clause belongs in line 75's record clause: line 101 is not among the lines the plan's item 6 and Decisions name, and line 7 declares every other line byte-identical to v2's, so a clause beside 101 would widen the changed set and falsify line 7. Decision 3 names `p5_g3_calibrators.py` and the record as what the route changes, and no measure reads a failed pair, so the plan's \"the scoring\" is not moved. No declaration beyond D97's fix entry is needed; the amendment's bullet on the ratio estimator's sampler could carry the half-sentence.

###### NRE-2 — \"H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces\" (same-round; stands)

Verified. Line 75 writes the gradient route's bounds as \"H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces\"; line 68 writes \"H's bounds ε = 1e-6 inside the faces\" (`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68`, `:75`). The code bounds H to [ε, 1 − ε]: `NRETarget.bounds_unit` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:442`), `run_chains` with a target (`p5_g2_reference.py:580-581`), `_modes_gradient`'s intersection (`:488-489`), handed back for the chain starts (`:586`). The phrase recurs in the docstring (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:428`) and in D95 (`ROADMAP.md:5340`); D95 also uses it for line 68's polish bound (`ROADMAP.md:5255`), which the draft states as \"inside\", so the phrase is D95's gloss. A reader of the frozen text could set the bounds in the wrong region; the text speaks here, so line 137's precedence does not rescue it. Line 75 is already in the plan's list; the fix is four words there and in the docstring. Same-round.

###### NRE-3 — the value check at 1e-3 relative (note; stands)

Verified. `abs(v32 − f) < 1e-3 · max(1, |f|)` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:285-286`); the prior's part to 1e-12 (`:287-291`); the gradient to 1e-7 relative (`:284`). The docstring (`:244`) and D95 (`ROADMAP.md:5360-5361`) call the value check \"within float32 rounding\". Float32's epsilon is about 1.2e-7; the float32 path rounds the unit coordinates and the standardised quotes (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:410`, `:415`) and accumulates rounding through three 128-wide layers, of order 1e-6 relative for a well-conditioned network. The exact figure the toy trunk needs is unmeasured here (the test may not be run); the structural claim holds. The bound still catches an O(1) error, and the gradient and prior checks are tight, so the consequence is a weak clause in an otherwise tight test and a mildly over-stated account in D95. Note; D97 records that the bound is the file's 1e-3; tightening needs the author's run in `.venv-p5`.

###### NRE-4 — the refusal comes late and by a traceback (note; stands)

Verified. `ValueError` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:432-434`), not `SystemExit`. In `run_g3` it is reached from `predicted(n)` (`:722`) after `get_or_train` at n and 2n (`:712`); the loop order prior, level, kind with ensemble first (`:692-698`) means the ensemble's whole ladder at that prior and level precedes it too, so the claim understates the work, though every run is saved and reused on resume (`:651-654`). In `score_set` it follows the header and the ensemble's rows. `pilot_surfaces` removes failed records (`:513-515`), so G3 needs a status-ok record without Σ_P, which v3's `reference_pair` never writes (`p5_g2_reference.py:837-851`), and `require_pilot`'s emulator check (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:633-634`) and v3's record names (`p5_g2_reference.py:198-207`) make a foreign record remote; the scoring's realistic path is NRE-1. D95's wording is accurate. Note; an early `SystemExit` is the author's decision and within the plan.

###### NRE-5 — unexercised on a trained ReLU network; CI never runs the torch tests (note; stands, one sentence corrected)

The title and the CI claim hold: `.github/workflows/tests.yml` installs `requirements.txt` (numpy, scipy, matplotlib) and pytest; `_needs_torch` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:24`) skips both torch tests; D95 says so (`ROADMAP.md:5363-5364`) and records the `.venv-p5` run, 17 passed (`:5450-5452`). The two-Gaussian target with m = 2 (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:784-788`), the quadratic module (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:206-222`), the study's finite-difference stand-ins (`p5_v3_study.py:532-563`; `ROADMAP.md:5138-5144`) and D96's 55 least-squares runs (`ROADMAP.md:5605-5609`) are as described.

The sentence \"No test or run has put L-BFGS-B on a piecewise-linear logit plus log P(H)\" is false: `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:297-300` runs `R.run_chains` with `target = NRETarget` on `make_mlp(39, 1)`, an untrained ReLU network of the design's trunk, so `_modes_gradient`'s L-BFGS-B (`p5_g2_reference.py:499-505`) has run on exactly such a target; the assertions are only that the route ran, kept at least one solution and clipped Σ, and the statuses are not read. The accurate reading, which D97 should carry instead: the route is unexercised on a *trained* network, no convergence is asserted anywhere, and its behaviour on a real network is first seen at G3's pilot with the recorded statuses and m as the diagnostic (`ROADMAP.md:5520-5526`; `_trim_start`, `p5_g3_calibrators.py:472-476`). D95's point (6) carries the same imprecision. Note; the optional test proposed would add training and status assertions to what `:297-300` already does.

##### 3. Own observations

1. **Note.** The torch test already runs the gradient route on an untrained ReLU network (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:297-300`); D95's point (6) (`ROADMAP.md:5520-5526`) and NRE-5's sentence should be restated exactly in D97: a mechanics run on no trained network, no convergence assertion, no inspection of the optimiser's statuses.
2. **Note.** The keep rule's prior term differs between routes: the gradient route's `logf` is the log-target at the solution with log P at the raw x_H bounded to [ε, 1 − ε] (`p5_g2_reference.py:455`, `:499-506`); the least-squares route takes the prior's density for the keep rule at u clipped to [0.01, 0.99] (`:434`, `:439`; line 68 (2)-(3)). Line 75 says the keep rule \"reduces to the log-target within log 10⁴ of the best\", which the code matches (`keep_solutions`' ½ log det Σ is constant under a constant Σ, `:378`, `:386`); the reading to declare is that line 75's \"as there\" does not import line 68's [0.01, 0.99] clipping. No disagreement between text and code.
3. **Note.** D95's account of line 68's polish bound reads \"H within ε = 1e-6 of the faces\" (`ROADMAP.md:5255`) where the draft's line 68 says \"inside\": NRE-2's phrase is D95's gloss carried into line 75 and the docstring; the merged entry stands and the two places v3 may change take the fix.
4. **Checked, no change.** `score_set`'s resume compares its header to the call only for the Stage 1 digest (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:867-869`) and makes no `_check_resume` on `post_kw` or `expected` as `run_g3` does (`:689`); v2's code, unchanged by the diff. In practice the lengths cannot differ between a scoring's runs, since `post_kw` comes from G2's reports (`main :950-951`; `g2_status`, `p5_g2_reference.py:1391`), which are not written over, and `expected` from `p5_calibrators_trained.json`, which `refuse_overwrite` protects (`:969`).

Checked and found sound, with nothing to report: `NRETarget.__call__` is `nre_target`'s float32 callable, so the chains' target is the network as trained (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:436`, `:444-445`) and only the optimiser sees the float64 copy (`:438`, `:451`); `value_grad_unit` works wholly in unit coordinates, the network taking unit coordinates as input (`:415`, `:451`) and `prior_grad_curv` being d/du at u = x_H (`p5_g2_reference.py:303-310`), matching `prior_logpdf`'s argument (`:221`), so no Jacobian factor is missing or doubled; `copy.deepcopy(...).double().eval()` leaves the trained network float32 (`:438`; `test :255`); `bounds_unit` (`:442`) is intersected in `_modes_gradient` (`:488-489`) and governs the chain starts' clipping (`:586`); the refusals of a missing and a mis-shaped Σ_P (`:432-434`, `:440-441`); the generator `[107, p, q, i, j]` with p from `PRIOR_INDEX`, q the run's training seed (6 for a replacement, as line 75's \"training seed q\" says; `load_run :374-384`), i the reference's i counted from 0 and j = `run[\"j\"]` from `LEVEL_INDEX`, 1-based (`:485`, `:694`), against `pilot_surfaces`' `j_ref = j − 1` (`:508`, `:511`) and `score_set`'s `r[\"j\"] == j − 1` (`:887`); the draws' order the reference's (`run_chains :577-578`, then `mh_step`); `_trim_start`'s keys cover line 75's promise of m, the kept solutions with their log-targets and the optimiser's statuses (`:472-476`), nothing promised lost; Σ_P's path from `reference_pair` (`p5_g2_reference.py:838-839`, `:850-851`) through `pilot_surfaces` (`:516-518`), `predict_rows` (`:561`) and `score_set` (`:906`) under the training prior; the doubled lengths reach `nre_posterior` in G3 and the scoring through `post_kw` (`main :950-951`, `:953`, `:984`; `run_g3 :706`; `score_set :907`), `g2_status` taking the passing attempt's lengths (`p5_g2_reference.py:1391`); `clip_cov` clips to [1e-10, 1/12] as `cov_from_jacobian` does (`:360-367`, `:349`), line 75's \"clipped as there\"; CI never runs the torch tests.

### 9.10 Reviewer, dimension 4: G2 and the records

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review (D97) — dimension 4: G2 and the records

Read-only, at `main` 3fad362 (tracked tree clean; untracked files only). Every file was read from the HEAD blob (`git show HEAD:<path>`); the committed records under `output/` were read only for the figures the brief allows, with the standard library's `json`; the `.npz` archives were not opened, and G̃ was only hashed. No P5 script, test or pricer was run and no repository module imported.

##### 1. Verdict

Sound for the freeze in this dimension. Draft line 143 agrees with `p5_g2_reference.py` on the six seeds and their rule, on the pair key `default_rng([83, i, j])` with i counted within each set, on the dropped retry clause (a retry pair now shares only the generator's stream with the first attempt's pair of the same i and j) and on the go rule's clause that a run's core-seconds include its optimiser's; `g2_report`, `g2_status`, `require_g2` and `main` carry the retry's conditions, the hand-off of doubled lengths and the refusals as line 143 and line 93 require. v3's records go under the names the plan's item 5 lists, and nothing writes over v2's. Nothing in the scripts, the hold modes or the tests reaches a draw of the sealed test set. The committed study and transcription records carry the written-in sampler's constants key for key (the float `fd_rel_step` included) and G̃'s SHA-256, their pairs are keyed `[83, i, j]`, and the fields D94 and D96 quote read as those entries state them. No must-fix. Three same-round items: the study script's sources now name v3's gate records while its comments, docstring and a test still say v2's, with a hold mode that will read v3's G2 records under v2's labels once G2 has run; the amendment's OSF summary says v3's "gate records" are renamed where only the reference's are; and the seed exclusion sets in three test files other than the reference's are partial. One note: the timed window of a run includes its acceptance diagnostics as well as its optimiser.

##### 2. Findings

**G2R-1 (same-round). `p5_v3_study.py`'s SOURCES now name v3's gate records; its comments, docstring and a test pin the stale reading, and its hold mode will read v3's G2 records as v2's once G2 has run.**
*Claim.* `SOURCES` and `SOURCE_REPORTS` read `R.SETS["g2"]` and `R.SETS["g2r"]`, which since D95 are `output/p5v3_g2_pilot.jsonl`, `p5v3_g2r_pilot.jsonl` and their reports, while the comment on the same line ("v2's committed records (D89), read only"), the module docstring and `estimate()`'s docstring still describe v2's. Today the hold mode prints "no record at output\p5v3_g2_pilot.jsonl" and "estimate: v2's G2 reports not found", and `--run` is refused for want of the records, as D95 declares. D95 does not say what happens after v3's G2: `estimate()` reads any report that exists at `SOURCE_REPORTS`, so the hold mode will then print v3's G2 projection as "from v2's reports", and with a retry's record present `read_sources()` (which needs 18 pairs per set) will list v3's 36 gate pairs as "v2 ok" or "v2 reference_failure". `--run` stays refused: by `E.refuse_overwrite` on the committed study report and, were that moved aside, by `launch`'s header check against the committed study record, whose seeds are v2's. The transcription check is unaffected: `T.STUDY` is `S.PATHS` (the study's own record and samples), `T.v2_surfaces` draws v2's surfaces from the seeds in the study's header through `pilot_points` and `noise_draws`, and nothing it imports from `S` depends on `SOURCES`.
*Evidence.* `p5_v3_study.py:139-140`; `:7-10, 49-58, 72`; `:1087-1098`; `:709-722`; `:1153-1179` (the sources read at 1155, `--run` refused at 1178-1179 before `refuse_overwrite` at 1180); `p5_g2_reference.py:198-203`; `p5_g0_pilot.py:592-597`; `ROADMAP.md:5502-5511`; `test_p5_v3_study.py:67` (asserts `SOURCES` equals `R.SETS`' jsonl paths) and `:635` (reads `R.SETS`' reports under the comment "v2's committed reports, read only"); `p5_v3_transcription.py:75, 140-149, 164-166`.
*Bears on.* `p5_v3_study.py`, outside the frozen scripts (plan line 145); D95's declaration (1); the study script's hold mode after v3's G2 (plan, Order of acts, item 11). No run after the freeze takes this script.
*Proposed fix.* Either set `SOURCES` and `SOURCE_REPORTS` to v2's literal names (`output/p5_g2_pilot.jsonl`, `p5_g2r_pilot.jsonl`, `p5_g2_report.json`, `p5_g2r_report.json`), which makes the comment, the docstring and the hold mode true again, amending `test_p5_v3_study.py:67` and the comment at 635 with it (the committed record's header already names v2's files, so the record is unaffected); or, if the script is to stay as D93 and D94 ran it, extend D97's declaration to the post-G2 behaviour and the two refusals that keep `--run` safe.

**G2R-2 (same-round). The amendment's OSF summary says v3's "gate records" go under `output/p5v3_*`; only the reference's records do.**
*Claim.* "Version 3's gate records go under new names (`output/p5v3_*`)" overstates. `SETS` renames the exact reference's four records, exactly as the plan's item 5 lists them; G3's own records (`p5_g3_ladder.jsonl`, `p5_g3_report.json`, the `p5_g3` and `p5_calibrators` folders, `p5_calibrators.jsonl`, `p5_calibrators_trained.json`), the scores (`p5_scores_<set>.jsonl`) and Stage 1 and 2's outputs keep v2's names. No record can be written over, since v2 reached none of them, and the code agrees with the plan; it is the registration summary's sentence that a reader would take to cover G3's gate record.
*Evidence.* `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:34`; `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:54`; `p5_g2_reference.py:198-207`; `p5_g3_calibrators.py:143-150, 967`; `p5_stage2.py:157-166`; the draft names no record path but `output/p5_g1_emulator.npz` (line 7) and `output/p5_g0_main_quotes.csv` (line 22).

Version 3's reference records, gate G2's two attempts, G3's pilot references and the test set's, go under new names (`output/p5v3_g2_*`, `p5v3_g2r_*`, `p5v3_g3_pilot_refs*`, `p5v3_ref_test*`); version 2's stay in place as history; the later records (G3's ladder and report, the calibrators, the scores, Stage 1 and 2) keep the scripts' names, none of which version 2 reached."

**G2R-4 (note). A run's recorded seconds include its acceptance diagnostics as well as its optimiser.**
*Claim.* Line 143's new clause says a run's core-seconds include its optimiser's. The window `_run_with_repeat` times opens before `run_chains` (the 16 optimiser starts, the polish, the chains) and closes after `accept()`, so the split R-hat and the variogram effective sizes of all four parameters over the 4 × sample steps are inside it too. This is v2's window unchanged, D94's 34.8 core-hours were projected from the same window, and the effect is conservative. The plan lists one clause for line 143 and nothing more, so the draft should not gain words.

*Proposed fix.* None to the draft or the code; record the reading in D97's entry, or leave it.

### 9.11 Skeptic, dimension 4, correctness lens

#### P5 v3 review, D97 — dimension 4 (G2 and the records): the skeptic's pass

Repository `C:\\Users\\micha\\OneDrive\\Documents\\roughvollab`, HEAD `3fad362`, tracked tree clean (the untracked files are the same eleven the brief lists). Read-only throughout; no repository module imported, no script or test run, no `.npz` opened (G̃ and the sample archives hashed only). Working copies are CRLF; every text hash below is of the git blob (`git show HEAD:<path> | sha256sum`).

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: correctness — is each claim literally true of the files at HEAD, could a line, key, index base or count have been misread, and does the proposed fix follow and stay within \"What v3 does not change\"?

Four findings examined. **Refuted: 0. Standing: 4. Re-graded: 0** (G2R-1, G2R-2 and G2R-3 stay same-round; G2R-4 stays a note). Every cited line was read in its surroundings and every comparison's other side (the plan's text, v2's frozen text, the entry, the test, the record header where a figure was stated) was read too.

##### 2. The findings

###### G2R-1 — the study script's SOURCES name v3's records; its words and a test say v2's — **stands, same-round**

- `p5_v3_study.py:139-140` build SOURCES and SOURCE_REPORTS from `R.SETS[\"g2\"]` and `R.SETS[\"g2r\"]`, which at HEAD are `output/p5v3_g2_pilot.jsonl`, `p5v3_g2r_pilot.jsonl`, `p5v3_g2_report.json`, `p5v3_g2r_report.json` (`p5_g2_reference.py:198-203`). The same line's comment says \"v2's committed records (D89), read only\"; the module docstring says \"each y read from v2's committed records\" (`:7-8`); `read_sources`'s docstring (`:710`) and `estimate`'s (`:1088`) say v2's.
- Today's hold mode, traced: `read_jsonl` returns a `None` header for a missing file (`p5_g0_pilot.py:595-597`); `read_sources` raises \"no record at <path>: the study runs on v2's 36 pilot pairs ...\" (`p5_v3_study.py:716-717`); `main` catches it and prints \"sources: ...\" (`:1165-1166`); `estimate` skips missing reports and returns `None` (`:1095-1096, 1108-1109`), so `:1174` prints \"estimate: v2's G2 reports not found; no figure\"; `--run` is refused at `:1178-1179` (\"the study needs v2's two G2 records\") before `refuse_overwrite` at `:1180`. D95 declares exactly this (`ROADMAP.md:5502-5511`) and nothing about the state after v3's G2.
- After v3's G2, traced: `g2_report` writes `projection` with `median_unit_s` and `base_steps` (`p5_g2_reference.py:1271-1273, 1346`), so `estimate` returns a figure and `:1169` prints \"estimate (from v2's reports, output\\\\p5v3_g2_report.json)\". `read_sources` requires both files and 18 tasks each (`:713-720`); v3's sets have 18 (6 points × 3 levels, `make_jobs` `:1205-1208`), so once a retry's record exists `:1163-1164` lists v3's 36 pairs each as \"v2 {status}\". With only the first attempt's record present, the second read raises and nothing is listed.
- `--run` stays refused: `refuse_overwrite([PATHS[\"report\"]])` (`:1180`; `p5_g1_emulator.py:177-183`) meets the committed `output/p5v3_study_report.json` (tracked, `git ls-files output/`); were it moved aside, `check_surfaces` (`:739-749`, v3's `g2_surfaces`) would pass on v3's records, and `R.launch` would then refuse on the header's `seeds` (`p5_g2_reference.py:1144-1147`): the committed header's seeds are v2's (79, 89, 139; 131, 137, 149 — header line of `output/p5v3_study.jsonl`, printed), the new header's would be v3's (`study_header` `:758-762`).
- The transcription check is unaffected: `STUDY = S.PATHS` (`p5_v3_transcription.py:75`), `v2_surfaces` draws from the study header's seeds (`:140-149`), and the check takes from `S` only PATHS, N_PAIRS_EXPECTED, SEALED_SEEDS and `refuse_sealed` (`:66, 75-78, 96, 100, 145, 174`); importing `p5_v3_study` evaluates SOURCES but reads nothing.
- `test_p5_v3_study.py:67` asserts `S.SOURCES == {\"g2\": R.SETS[\"g2\"][\"jsonl\"], ...}`; `:635` carries the comment \"v2's committed reports, read only\". The committed record's header `sources` already names `output\\\\p5_g2_pilot.jsonl` and `output\\\\p5_g2r_pilot.jsonl` (printed), so the record is untouched by either fix.
- Bounds: the plan places the study script outside the frozen scripts (`P5_v3_plan.md:145`); the amendment's registered-file list omits it (`P5_v3_amendment.md:15`). Either proposed fix is free of the freeze; the choice between re-pointing SOURCES (which also means the committed script no longer equals the one that ran at `633b470`, a point for the author) and extending the declaration is the author's. Severity same-round (staleness, a docstring, a test) is right.

###### G2R-4 — a run's recorded seconds include its acceptance diagnostics — **stands, note**

- `_run_with_repeat`: `t0` before `run_chains`, `seconds` after `accept` (`p5_g2_reference.py:791-797`). The optimiser runs inside `run_chains` (`:576-592`), its time kept apart as `optimiser_seconds` (`:592, 615`). `accept` computes split R-hat and `ess_bw` (the between-within variogram estimator, `:671-674`) for all four parameters on the sampling-phase samples `[n_chains, sample, 4]` (`:691-699`).
- The window is v2's unchanged (`git show 59ca589:p5_g2_reference.py`, `_run_with_repeat` at 427-441) and the study ran it unchanged (`633b470`, 432-440; the study reuses `R._run_with_repeat`, `p5_v3_study.py:11, 608`). `project_cost` reads `a[\"seconds\"]` (`:1264-1265`). D94's figures (`ROADMAP.md:5128-5131`) match the committed checks file: projected 34.785 core-hours, median 68.305 s per 50,000 steps, 1.528 units per pair, 55 runs measured (printed, not recomputed).
- Including the diagnostics enlarges the seconds and so the projection: conservative. Draft line 143 says \"a run's core-seconds including its optimiser's (line 68; D95)\"; the plan lists one clause for line 143 (`P5_v3_plan.md:33`) and says the optimiser's seconds fall inside the timed window (`:118`). A reading for the record; no change due. Note is right.

### 9.12 Skeptic, dimension 4, consequence lens

#### D97 skeptic's check — dimension 4 (G2 and the records), lens: consequence

Repository at HEAD 3fad362, read-only. Nothing in the repository was written; no P5 script, test or pricer was run; no module was imported; no emulator or `.npz` was opened; the committed records were read only for the figures the brief allows, with the standard library.

##### 1. Lens and summary

The lens is consequence: each finding was taken as true, checked against the files, and then weighed for whether it breaks a binding rule of the plan, makes a declaration false or incomplete, changes what a run after the freeze does or records, touches the sealed test set, or leaves a reader unable to reproduce a step; and whether the severity and the proposed fix are right.

Of the four findings, none is refuted and none is re-graded: G2R-1 stands at same-round, G2R-2 at same-round, G2R-3 at same-round (a note would be defensible, the author's call), G2R-4 at note. Two refinements are recorded under own observations.

##### 2. The findings

###### G2R-1 — the study script's SOURCES now name v3's gate records: stands, same-round

`p5_v3_study.py:139-140` set `SOURCES` and `SOURCE_REPORTS` from `R.SETS["g2"]` and `R.SETS["g2r"]`, under the comment "v2's committed records (D89), read only"; `p5_g2_reference.py:198-203` name those sets `output/p5v3_g2_pilot.jsonl`, `p5v3_g2r_pilot.jsonl` and their reports. The module docstring (`p5_v3_study.py:6-9`, `:49`, `:58-59`) and `estimate`'s docstring (`:1087-1089`) still say v2's records.

Today, as D95 declares (`ROADMAP.md:5502-5511`): `read_sources` (`:709-722`) raises "no record at <path>" through `read_jsonl`'s None for a missing file (`p5_g0_pilot.py:592-597`), `main` prints it under "sources:" (`:1165-1166`), and `estimate` finds no report, so `main` prints "estimate: v2's G2 reports not found" (`:1174`). D95 says nothing about the state after v3's G2, and the finding's reading of that state holds: `estimate` reads any existing file at `SOURCE_REPORTS` (`:1094-1098`) and `main` labels it "estimate (from v2's reports, ...)" (`:1169`); with a retry record present, `read_sources` accepts any two 18-pair records and `main` prints each pair as "v2 {status}" (`:1164`); without a retry record the listing still fails on the missing g2r file and only the estimate line is mislabelled.

`--run` stays refused: `E.refuse_overwrite([PATHS["report"]], "study")` (`:1180`; `p5_g1_emulator.refuse_overwrite` refuses when the file exists) against the committed `output/p5v3_study_report.json`. Were that moved aside, `check_surfaces` (`:739-750`, which calls `R.g2_surfaces`, now v3's seeds) would pass on v3's records, and `launch`'s header check (`p5_g2_reference.py:1144`, keys compared include `seeds`) would refuse against the committed study record, whose header carries v2's seeds (79, 89, 139; 131, 137, 149, read from the header line). The transcription check is unaffected: `T.STUDY = S.PATHS` (`p5_v3_transcription.py:75`); `v2_surfaces` builds v2's surfaces from the study header's seeds (`:140-150`) and never touches `SOURCES` or `g2_surfaces`. `test_p5_v3_study.py:67` pins `SOURCES` to `R.SETS`; `:635` reads `R.SETS`' reports as "v2's committed reports".

Consequence: no binding rule, no declaration of the draft or of the OSF texts, no run after the freeze (the plan places the script outside the frozen scripts, `P5_v3_plan.md:145`; its order of acts, item 11, runs G2 and onwards from the frozen scripts). Staleness in comments, docstrings and hold-mode labels, and a log declaration silent on a later state: same-round as defined.

On the fix: HEAD's `p5_v3_study.py` is the same blob as at 633b470, the study's run commit (`git rev-parse`, 60870cf). Option 1 would be the first change to the script since the run and would need a declaration saying so (D95's "nothing in `p5_v3_study.py` changes", `ROADMAP.md:5440-5441`, becoming historical). Option 2 is the smaller and keeps the script identical to the one that ran. Both are within the plan's bounds.

###### G2R-2 — the amendment's "gate records go under output/p5v3_*": stands, same-round

`docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:34` reads "Version 3's gate records go under new names (`output/p5v3_*`); version 2's stay in place as history." The plan's item 5 (`P5_v3_plan.md:54`) names exactly four patterns and the code agrees: `p5_g2_reference.py:198-207` renames only the reference's four records. G3's own records keep v2's names: `p5_g3_calibrators.py:143-146` (`p5_g3_ladder.jsonl`, `p5_g3_report.json`, the `p5_g3` and `p5_calibrators` folders, `p5_calibrators.jsonl`), `:149-150` (`p5_scores_<set>.jsonl`), `:967` (`p5_calibrators_trained.json`); `p5_stage2.py:157-166` (`p5_scores_test.jsonl`, `p5_g3_report.json`, `p5_stage1_*`, `p5_stage2_*`). G3 is a gate, so "gate records" as a class overstates, and the glob also covers the study's and the transcription's records. The draft names no record path but `output/p5_g1_emulator.npz` (line 7) and `output/p5_g0_main_quotes.csv` (line 22), by grep.

Consequence: no rule broken, no run changed, nothing can be written over (v2 reached no G3 or Stage record, and every write goes through `refuse_overwrite`). The sentence is in the text pasted on OSF at step 3 of the order of acts (`:55`), so it must be reworded before then, which is this round. It is a description of a change, not a declaration of what was seen, so same-round rather than must-fix. The proposed wording matches the plan's item 5 and the code and needs no declaration beyond D97's fix entry.

###### G2R-3 — v2's six seeds join the reference's exclusion sets only: stands, same-round

`test_p5_g2_reference.py:68-69` (`excluded`) and `:382-383` (`used`) each hold the 19 seeds of `P5_v3_plan.md:47`, v2's six and the sealed pair included, and assert v3's six disjoint; the plan's item 3 (`:48`, one set) is met, and draft line 15 claims only that v2's six "join the seeds the reference's tests check no seed in use falls on", which is true. The other checks are partial as claimed: `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:52` checks {109, 113, 107} against a set without 20261022, 127, 131, 137, 139, 149 or v3's six; `test_p5_stage2.py:32` checks 127 against a tuple without 20261022, 131 to 149 or 151 to 179; `test_p5_g1_emulator.py:38` checks 73 against (20261002, 7, 61, 62). The scripts' seeds are at `p5_g3_calibrators.py:128`, `p5_stage2.py:133`, `p5_g1_emulator.py:89`. `git grep -w` of 151, 157, 163, 167, 173 and 179 over `*.py` and `*.md` finds no other seed use: the remaining hits are line-number citations in `docs/gate_checks`, the declarations at draft lines 15 and 143 and the amendment's line 32, and tests drawing v3's own G2 seeds on toy maps (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:216-225`, `:899`; `test_p5_v3_transcription.py:55`), which draft line 15 declares.

Consequence: nothing false, nothing at risk now. The gap is that a later version re-choosing one of those scripts' seeds onto 20261022, 127 or any of the twelve G2 seeds would pass those three checks; the literal set at `test_p5_g2_reference.py:68` would not catch it either. A weak test is same-round by the definition; the tests are outside the hash list, so the fix is free of the freeze and needs no declaration. The author could treat it as a note, since the plan's item is met and the sets are v2's.

###### G2R-4 — a run's recorded seconds include its acceptance diagnostics: stands, note

`p5_g2_reference.py:785-799` (`_run_with_repeat`): `t0` before `run_chains` (`:791`), `accept` called (`:793`), `seconds` taken after it (`:797`); so each run's seconds cover the optimiser (inside `run_chains`, `:576-592`, with `opt_seconds` recorded separately under `start` at `:615`), the chains and `accept`'s diagnostics; `accept` (`:691-699`) computes `split_rhat` and `ess_bw` for all four parameters on the sampling-phase samples. `project_cost` (`:1251-1273`) takes the median of `a["seconds"]` per unit of the lengths in force. v2's `_run_with_repeat` at 59ca589 (lines 427-441) has the same window, so it is unchanged; D94's projection (`ROADMAP.md:5128-5131`) says the optimiser's seconds lie inside the runs' timed windows. Draft line 143's clause and line 68's last sentence are true and match the plan's one clause (`P5_v3_plan.md:33`, `:118`). Including `accept`'s time makes the projection larger, so the go is harder: conservative. No change to the draft or the code; a note for D97's record is right. One word is not verified: the code's estimator is named `ess_bw` and line 68 calls it the between-within estimator; its body was not read, so "variogram" is the reviewer's word, not the file's.

### 9.13 Reviewer, dimension 5: the amendment file

#### D97 review, dimension 5: the amendment file

Repository at `3fad362`, the tracked tree clean (`git status --porcelain --untracked-files=no` empty). Read-only: git show, diff, rev-parse, cat-file, ls-files, log; sha256sum; grep, sed, diff, ls, wc. No P5 script, test, pricer or module was run or imported; no `.npz` was opened (G̃ and the sample archives were hashed and sized only); no Python was run. A copy of 59ca589's blob and of HEAD's draft was written to the scratchpad for the line diff.

##### 1. Verdict

Sound in substance with one must-fix. Every blob hash the file states (v2's six G2 files, the study's four, the check's three, G̃'s hash and size) is correct and cited to the right entry; the numbering D95/D96/D97/D98/D99 is consistent throughout; the record table has v2's form plus a filled G̃ row; the order of acts follows the plan's with the shifted numbers and D86/D87/D83's precedent; the sampler, gradient-route and seed bullets agree with draft lines 68, 75 and 143; \"What had been seen\" agrees with D88 to D96 and the draft's line 15; and the diff of 59ca589's blob against the draft confirms \"every other line byte-identical, the title line apart\" (exactly the 18 lines D95 names). The must-fix: \"What is not changed\" (line 37) and the 695FW paragraph (line 47) say the calibrators' architecture and training are unchanged, dropping the plan's binding exception for the ratio estimator's sampler's start and first proposal, which Decision 3 extended and draft line 75 (under Architecture and training) changes; the public text would contradict the protocol it registers and the summary's own third bullet. Same-round: step 2 does not say what becomes of draft line 7's future-tense last sentence at the freeze; bullet 2 puts Σ_P in each run's record where line 68 puts it in the pair's; line 41's \"which passed under the rule the plan bound\" is ambiguous about the gradient route, which was outside the verdict rule; the declaration omits the hold-mode runs and pre-flights D88 to D96 declare, where v2's ended \"and nothing else\"; step 1 omits line 137 and says nothing about D96's attestation if a fix touches the sampler's code; two citations are stale. Two notes.

##### 2. Findings

**AMD-1 (must-fix). \"What is not changed\" and the 695FW paragraph drop the plan's exception for the ratio estimator's sampler.**
Claim: line 37 lists \"the calibrators, their architecture and training\" as unchanged without the plan's qualifying clause, and line 47 says \"the hypotheses, priors, calibrators, tolerances and the sealed test set are unchanged\" and names no change to the ratio estimator's sampler; yet Decision 3 took the gradient route, draft line 75 (under Architecture and training) changes the ratio estimator's start and first proposal, draft line 7 names that change among v3's, and the summary's own third bullet (line 31) states it.
Evidence: `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:37`, `:47`; `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:74` (\"except the start and first proposal of the ratio estimator's sampler, if Decision 3 extends (A) to it\"); `ROADMAP.md:4672-4674`; `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:75` (in the diff against 59ca589) and `:7`; amendment `:31`.
Bears on: the OSF summary and the paragraph pasted at steps 3 and 4; the plan's binding section \"What v3 does not change\" (plan 69-78); draft line 75.
Fix: line 37, \"the calibrators, their architecture and training, except the start and first proposal of the ratio estimator's sampler, by the gradient route (above)\"; line 47, \"Version 3 changes the exact reference's start and first proposal, whose failure at gate G2 stopped version 2 (decision-log entry D89), and by the same route the ratio estimator's sampler's, draws fresh seeds for G2 and adopts the emulator built under version 2; the hypotheses, the priors, the calibrators' architecture and training otherwise, the tolerances and the sealed test set are unchanged.\"

**AMD-2 (same-round). Step 2 does not say what becomes of draft line 7's future-tense last sentence at the freeze.**
Claim: draft line 7 ends \"The hash list ..., and the numpy, scipy and torch versions the runs use, are appended to this line at the freeze commit, as v2's were.\" Step 2 has the freeze commit change only \"DRAFT.\" to \"FROZEN on <freeze-date>.\" and append the list, \"Nothing else moves\". v2's pre-freeze line 7 carried no such sentence; its freeze commit appended \"The hash list (under Analysis plan) is the SHA-256 of each file's git blob at the commit that adds this line, which changes none of them: ...\" after the last sentence. Left as it is, the frozen line says the list is still to be appended, immediately before the list; replaced, the commit changes more than step 2 names.
Evidence: `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:7` (last sentence); `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:54`; `git diff 59ca589^ 59ca589 -- docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md` (hunks at lines 7 and 216 only); `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:192`.
Bears on: step 2; draft line 7 at the freeze commit; plan item 6.
Fix: name the sentence's fate in step 2, either kept as the list's lead-in with the appended text continuing it (\"They are: ...\") or replaced by the list's own lead-in as v2's line carries it, and say so in the entry that records the freeze.

**AMD-3 (same-round). Bullet 2 places Σ_P in each run's record and describes the direct runs' start as the uniform run's.**
Claim: line 30 says \"The record of each run carries its start and, under each prior, the covariance the ratio estimator's sampler reads.\" Draft line 68 puts the start in each sampler run's record and Σ_P under each prior in the pair's record; D96 says the same. The bullet's \"solutions of a bounded least-squares optimiser ... a first proposal scaled to the local Gauss–Newton covariance\" describes the uniform run; a direct run's kept solutions are polished by L-BFGS-B on the full negative log-target and its Σ_P adds the prior's curvature.
Evidence: `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:30`; `docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:68`; `ROADMAP.md:5609-5614`.
Bears on: the summary's description of the sampler change; draft line 68.
Fix: \"Each sampler run's record carries its start, and each pair's record, under each prior, the covariance the ratio estimator's sampler reads\"; and \"(for a direct run under prior A or B, each kept solution polished by L-BFGS-B on the full negative log-target, its covariance gaining the prior's curvature)\".

**AMD-4 (same-round). \"which passed under the rule the plan bound\" is ambiguous about the gradient route.**
Claim: line 41's \"... and the gradient route on stand-in targets, which passed under the rule the plan bound\" can be read as a pass of the gradient route under the verdict rule; that rule names the 36 pairs, the comparisons and the cost, and the route's outcome was reported beside the verdict, not inside it.
Evidence: `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:41`; `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:158-159`; `ROADMAP.md:5060-5062`, `:5136-5144`.
Bears on: \"What had been seen\"; D94's verdict.
Fix: \"... and the gradient route on stand-in targets; the study passed under the rule the plan bound, and the stand-in runs, outside that rule, were each accepted and within tolerance (D94).\" The author may add that the route had not run on a trained network (D95, Declared (6)).

At v2's precedent the pasted summary was the amendment file's text at the freeze commit, character for character (`ROADMAP.md:4267-4270`), so the text must be right before the freeze. The proposed fix restores the plan's own clause and is within bounds.

##### 3. Own observations

- `amendment:41` against `draft:15` (note; another dimension's line): line 41 names the plan's nine-agent review (D91) and the independent review (D97) and says the test suites draw v3's G2 seeds in memory (confirmed at `test_p5_g2_reference.py:216-220, 388-394`); draft line 15's v3 passage names neither review, so the two declarations differ in reach; step 1's update of line 15 may align them.

- `amendment:35` and `:41` anticipate the re-ticking and D97 in texts written for pasting at the freeze, as v2's were and as step 1 provides; no change asked.
- Checked and found sound: all thirteen blob hashes at `amendment:25` (nine by `git show HEAD:<path> | sha256sum`, four `.npz` by `sha256sum` on disk) and G̃'s hash and size (2,584,804 bytes) at lines 15, 25 and 67, agreeing with D89 (`4589-4600`), D94 (`5184-5191`) and D96 (`5630-5635`); the 18 changed draft lines are exactly those D95 names; line 11's account of the stop notice matches D90 (`4621-4646`); line 29's figures match D89; line 32's rule gives 151, 157, 163, 167, 173, 179 in line 143's order; line 31 matches draft line 75; draft lines 7, 204, 209, 210, 213, 215 and 216 are as step 2 presumes, G̃'s hash already in line 7; D95–D99 are used consistently (lines 7, 15, 25, 30–35, 41, 47, 53, 57); the Record table has v2's rows plus one for G̃.

none (git show, git diff, git rev-parse, git log, git ls-files, sha256sum, diff, grep, sed, awk, cut, ls, wc only; no `py -3 -I -c` command was run).

### 9.16 Reviewer, dimension 6: the declarations

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review, D97: dimension 6, the declarations

Reviewed at HEAD 3fad362 (tracked tree clean; untracked files only). Read-only: the draft, the plan, v2's frozen text at 59ca589, ROADMAP.md D86 to D96, the two v2 review records, the amendment file, the test file, the reference script's constants, the committed record headers (first line of each jsonl) and the small reports. Nothing was run beyond the three standard-library commands listed under section 4.

##### 1. Verdict

The declarations are substantially right: every date, count, seed, hash and model that lines 7, 9, 15, 140 and 200 state was traced to the entry, record header or report it rests on and found as stated; the diff against 59ca589 confirms exactly the 18 lines D95 names and no other; the checklist reopens exactly the plan's five boxes with 216 in its pre-freeze wording; and the test file draws v3's six seeds and holds v2's six in its exclusion set as line 15 says. Two declarations must change before the freeze. Line 200 attributes D91 wholly to Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code, while D91 says the plan's first version came from "the session that guides the author", a session distinct from the Opus session that reviewed and corrected it and whose model the log does not record. Line 7's enumeration of "exactly the changes the plan names" omits the go rule's new clause, the Precedence line's new entries and the checklist. Five same-round items concern the completeness of lines 9, 15 and 200 against the entries' "Declared" and "Who did what" bullets, judged by the grain at which v2's line 15 declared v2's sittings; two notes are wording and a decision for the author.

##### 2. Findings

**DECL-1 (must-fix). Line 200 attributes D91 wholly to Claude Opus 5.5; D91 records no model for the session that wrote the plan's first version.**
Claim: line 200 says "D91 (the plan, 25 October 2026) ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code, D91's review of the plan by nine agents on Claude Fable 5.1 from that session". D91 says "The first version was written on 25 October 2026, after D89, in the session that guides the author. This Claude Code session checked it read-only ..." and, under Who did what, "The first version came from the session that guides the author; the review and the corrected copy came from this Claude Code session on Claude Opus 5.5, its agents on Claude Fable 5.1." The phrase "the session that guides the author" occurs nowhere else in the log, and no entry names that session's model. The AI-use declaration therefore states a model for a sitting the log does not record one for, which v2's line 200 took care never to do ("the decision log dates every sitting without naming a model for the earlier ones").
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200; ROADMAP.md:4694-4696, 4715-4718; grep of ROADMAP.md for "guides the author" returns only 4695 and 4716; ROADMAP.md:5196-5198 and 5688-5694 place D89 to D92 in the Opus session, which is the reviewing session.
Bears on: draft line 200 (Tool, v3's sittings); the plan's item 6, docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:63; the Oxford declaration the line is written under.
Proposed fix: line 200 to read, for D91, "D91's plan was first written in the session that guides the author, whose model D91 does not record; its read-only review by nine agents on Claude Fable 5.1 and the corrected copy came from the Claude Code session on Claude Opus 5.5 (D91)"; or, if the author can name that session's model from its transcript, the fixes entry records it with the transcript's label, as D88, D93 and D95 did, and line 200 carries it. Either way the Limitations clause should say which v3 sitting's model is unrecorded, as it does for sittings before 11 October 2026.

**DECL-2 (must-fix). Line 7's enumeration of "exactly the changes the plan names" is incomplete.**
Claim: line 7 says v3 is v2's text "with exactly the changes the plan names (...): the start and the first proposal of the exact reference's sampler ... and of the ratio estimator's ..., G2's seeds and the clause on its retry's start (under Gates), the adoption of G̃, and the declarations of what had been seen; every other line is byte-identical ...". The plan's exhaustive list also changes line 143's go rule ("a run's core-seconds include its optimiser", a binding clause of a gate), line 137's Precedence (D92, D93 and D95 join the entries that read the scripts) and the checklist; the draft carries all three, and line 7's list, introduced as exact, names none of them. A reader who takes the list as the whole of what changed is misled about a gate rule.
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:7; docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:33, :60, :62, :64-66; the clause at docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:143 ("a run's core-seconds including its optimiser's (line 68; D95)") and the entries at :137; the diff of git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md against HEAD's draft (18 lines: 1, 7, 9, 15, 54, 68, 75, 137, 140, 142, 143, 200, 204, 209, 210, 213, 215, 216).
Bears on: draft line 7; plan item 6 (:56) and "What v3 changes (exhaustively)" (:28-67); the OSF summary that paraphrases it (docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:27-35, which does name the go rule's clause at :32).
Proposed fix: after "the clause on its retry's start" add "and the go rule's clause on the optimiser's seconds (under Gates), the entries added under Precedence", and after "the declarations of what had been seen" add "with the sittings under Reporting and disclosure and the five reopened boxes under Before freezing"; or replace the enumeration with "the lines the plan's items 1, 3, 4 and 6 name".

**DECL-3 (same-round). Line 15 says v3's seeds are drawn by the tests "in place of" v2's, but the tests still draw default_rng(79), (89) and (131) on toy maps.**
Claim: line 15 declares the tests draw "G2's pilot sets of both attempts (v3's, 151, 157 and 163; 167, 173 and 179, in place of v2's 79, 89 and 139; 131, 137 and 149, which join the seeds the reference's tests check no seed in use falls on; D95)". The tests do draw all six of v3's and hold v2's six in the exclusion set, but they also still draw noise_draws(89, ·) as toy records' noise with {"points": 79, "noise": 89} as seed labels, and pilot_points(79, 4) and pilot_points(131, 4) for a freshness assertion. Nothing of the design is touched (the draws reproduce v2's already-seen pilot θ on a toy map), but "in place of" is inexact.
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:15; test_p5_g2_reference.py:216-220 and 386-394 (v3's six), :64-69 and :381-383 (the exclusion sets), :260, :263-264, :395, :516-519, :639 (79, 89 and 131 still drawn); ROADMAP.md:5296-5300.
Bears on: draft line 15; plan item 3 (docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:46-48).
Proposed fix: "G2's pilot sets of both attempts (v3's, 151, 157 and 163; 167, 173 and 179; and still, on toy maps, v2's 79, 89 and 131, as a toy record's seed labels and in a check that v3's points differ from v2's), v2's six 79, 89, 139, 131, 137 and 149 joining the seeds the reference's tests check no seed in use falls on (D95)".

**DECL-4 (same-round). Line 15 does not say what the transcription check and the study left unexercised.**
Claim: line 15 reports the check as having "reproduced the study's reference records and their samples exactly (D96)", which matches D96's narrowed wording, but carries neither D96's statement of the check's reach (mass lines, stand-in runs and grid checks not re-run; every one of the 55 runs took the least-squares route at its first attempt and kept one solution, so the gradient route, the repeat at doubled lengths and the keeping of several solutions were not exercised) nor D95's declaration (6) that the gradient route has run only on the study's smooth stand-ins and that its behaviour on a trained ReLU network is first seen at G3's pilot. D94 likewise records m = 1 in every run and no repeat. These are what a reader needs to discount the sampler's untested branches.
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:15; ROADMAP.md:5603-5608 (D96), 5520-5525 (D95, Declared (6)), 5155-5158 and 5110 (D94).
Bears on: draft line 15; plan item 6 (:58); G2's retry and G3's pilot, the first runs after the freeze to take those branches.
Proposed fix: after the check's sentence add: "The check re-ran the references only, not the study's mass lines, stand-ins or grid checks; in the study and the check every run took the least-squares route at its first attempt and kept one solution, so the repeat at doubled lengths, the keeping of several solutions and the gradient route on a trained network have not been exercised; the gradient route has run only on the study's stand-in targets, and its behaviour on a network is first seen at G3's pilot (D95, D96)."

**DECL-5 (same-round). Line 15 omits the v3 sittings' read-only acts and hold modes that v2's line 15 declared for v2's sittings.**
Claim: v2's line 15 declared, sitting by sitting, what each pre-freeze sitting ran or read (D81's readers' runs and scratch scripts, the second review's arithmetic and hash, D82's import parse, D83's read-only check). v3's line 15 declares the four runs and the tests but none of the sittings' acts: D91's review of the plan read the committed G2 records and computed figures from the raw records (the amendment file's "What had been seen" names that review; line 15 does not, so the two documents differ); D93's hold mode was run from the command line and read v2's G2 records and reports; D95's hold modes read the study's committed record; D96 ran the check's hold mode as a pre-flight and read the partial record during the run; D88's pre-flight hold mode priced an identity check over 35 quotes, and the recording sittings read the outputs in Python, D88's calling load_emulator() at the 200 held-out points and D89's and D94's calling require_g1() and g2_status().
Evidence: git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md line 15; docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:15; ROADMAP.md:4706-4707 and 4711-4714 (D91), 4938-4947 (D93), 5497-5500 (D95), 5636-5641 (D96), 4391-4398 and 4443-4444 (D88), 4568-4569 (D89), 5167-5168 (D94); docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:41.
Bears on: draft line 15; box 204, to be re-ticked on this line; consistency with the OSF summary.
Proposed fix: one sentence after the check's: "Also declared: the plan's review (D91) read the committed G2 records read-only and computed figures from them that the plan does not carry; the hold modes of the study's script (D93), of the reference's script (D95) and of the check (D95, D96) read committed records, loaded no emulator and wrote nothing; D96 read the check's partial record during the run; and each recording sitting (D88, D89, D94, D96) read the run's output in Python, D88's evaluating G̃ at its held-out points, none computing a posterior." The amendment file's line 41 then matches.

**DECL-6 (same-round). Line 200's v3 clauses omit D90's and D92's acts and leave D95's "two declared departures" unnamed.**
Claim: under What the tool did, the v3 clause lists D91, D93, D88/D89/D94, D95 and D96 but not that the tool fixed the pool bug and wrote the pool-path test (D92) or drafted the stop notice's text and read the update back (D90); both appear only as labels in the sittings clause. Under What the author did, the v3 clause omits that the author posted and approved the stop notice on OSF (D90), agreed the plan (D91), and merged D95 and agreed to the tag of 4519d50 (D96); and "the two declared departures of D95" names neither (D95's Who did what: the amendment of three of the study's test assertions, and the title line beyond the plan's list).
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200; ROADMAP.md:4657-4659 (D90), 4715-4716 (D91), 4791-4792 and 4723-4727 (D92), 5530-5532 and 5510-5513 (D95), 5688-5689 (D96).
Bears on: draft line 200; plan item 6 (:63) and Order of acts steps 1 and 4 (:182, :185).
Proposed fix: tool clause: insert "drafted the stop notice and read the update back (D90), fixed the pool bug in p5_g2_reference.py with a test of both pool paths (D92)". Author clause: "posted and approved the stop notice on 695fw (D90); agreed the plan and answered its eleven decisions, all on the assistant's lean (D91); ...; and chose, in Plan Mode, G2's six seeds by the rule fixed in advance, who runs the transcription check, the title line beyond the plan's list and the amendment of three of the study's test assertions (D95); merged D95 and agreed to the tag of its second commit (D96)".

**DECL-7 (same-round). Line 9's "nothing else of the design changes" omits the ratio estimator's route and the two clauses of line 143.**
Claim: line 9 says v3 "changes the sampler's start and first proposal to candidate (A) ..., draws fresh seeds for G2, and adopts G̃; nothing else of the design changes (the plan, What v3 does not change)". Line 7 itself names the ratio estimator's sampler (the gradient route, not candidate (A) as such), and the plan's items 1 and 3 drop the retry's start clause and add the go rule's clause; the plan's list of what does not change carries the ratio estimator as an exception and the two clauses. Line 9 is narrower than line 7 and than the plan.
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:9 and :7; docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:31-33, :74, :76, :120-125.
Bears on: draft line 9 (plan item 6, :57).
Proposed fix: "... to candidate (A) of the plan, ..., and the ratio estimator's to the same start by a gradient route; replaces the retry's start clause and adds the optimiser's seconds to the go rule; draws fresh seeds for G2; and adopts G̃; nothing else of the design changes (the plan, What v3 does not change)."

**DECL-8 (note). Wording: "the adopted sampler" in lines 7 and 140 is an anachronism; line 7 cites D89 without D90 and D95 without D96.**
Claim: lines 7 and 140 say the study "ran the adopted sampler"; the study ran candidate (A), adopted only when it passed (D94), as line 15 says. Line 7 cites D89 for v2's stop but not D90, the notice of the stop on 695fw that the amendment file's Status cites beside it; and cites D95 for the changes while D96 changed lines 15, 200 and 215.
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:7, :140, :15; ROADMAP.md:5130-5132, 4614-4618, 5651-5656; docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:7.
Bears on: draft lines 7 and 140.
Proposed fix: "ran candidate (A), since adopted," in both lines; "(D89; the stop notice on 695fw, D90)" and "(..., D91; D95, D96)" in line 7.

**DECL-9 (note). Line 200 names no model for v2's post-freeze sittings D86 and D87, and the log records none.**
Claim: line 200's v2 text names models up to D85 (23 October 2026) and its v3 clause starts at D88; D86 (whose OSF acts "the second review's session guided ... and checked ... read-only") and D87 name no model in their entries. Now that line 200 is open, the author may decide whether to carry them.
Evidence: docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200; ROADMAP.md:4338-4341 (D86), 4348-4382 (D87); git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md line 200.
Bears on: draft line 200 (Tool; Limitations).
Proposed fix: a decision for the author: add "v2's post-freeze sittings, D86 and D87 (23 October 2026), were held in the second review's session and in Claude Code; the log names no model for them", or leave the log to carry it.

### 9.17 Skeptic, dimension 6, correctness lens

##### 1. Lens and summary

Each of the nine findings was checked against the files at HEAD (`3fad362`): the draft's lines 7, 9, 15, 140, 200 and 202-216; v2's frozen text from `git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md` (lines 7, 9, 15, 54, 140, 200, 202-216) and a line diff of that blob against HEAD's draft; the plan in full; the amendment file in full; ROADMAP.md entries D86 to D96 in full; and the seed-drawing lines of the five P5 test files. Nothing was run beyond git, sed, awk, grep, diff and wc.

Result: none of the nine is refuted; all nine stand at the severity given. Two are must-fix (DECL-1, DECL-2), five same-round (DECL-3 to DECL-7), two notes (DECL-8, DECL-9). DECL-3's proposed fix is itself incomplete (all six of v2's seeds are still drawn, not three); DECL-4 cites one ROADMAP line three lines off (5110 for 5113); DECL-7 is at the low end of same-round. Three own observations are recorded, one of them the correction to DECL-3's fix.

Checked and found sound in the same lines (no finding): the 18 changed lines are exactly 1, 7, 9, 15, 54, 68, 75, 137, 140, 142, 143, 200, 204, 209, 210, 213, 215 and 216, each named by the plan's items 1, 3 or 6, the title line apart; the reopened boxes are exactly the plan's five (204, 209, 210, 213, 215), every other box keeps v2's state, and 216 reads "- [ ] OSF registration amended." as the plan's item 6 requires before the freeze; line 215's D97 clause matches line 200's; line 140 agrees with D88, D89, D93 and D94; line 15's dates and counts agree with the entries (G1 launched 05:10:45 UTC on 24 October and assembled 00:45:21 UTC on 25 October, ROADMAP.md:4407-4416; G2's two attempts on 25 October, 4484-4548; 9 + 13 = 22 accepted pairs and 18 + 18 = 36 surfaces; the study from 19:09:59 UTC on 25 October to 00:17:01 on 26 October, 5109-5111; the check on 26 October, 5594); line 15's check sentence carries D96's narrowed wording ("reference records and their samples"); the models line 200 gives for D88, D89, D90, D92, D93, D94, D95 and D96 and for D91's nine agents (4 reviewers + 4 verifiers + 1 critic) agree with each entry's Who did what; v3's six seeds and v2's six in the tests' exclusion sets agree with line 15 (test_p5_g2_reference.py:64-69, 381-383).

##### 2. The findings

###### DECL-1 (must-fix, stands; confidence high)

Draft :200: "D91 (the plan, 25 October 2026) ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code, D91's review of the plan by nine agents on Claude Fable 5.1 from that session". ROADMAP.md:4694-4696 contrasts "the session that guides the author", which wrote the first version, with "This Claude Code session", which reviewed it; :4715-4718 repeats the split and names Opus 5.5 only for the review and the corrected copy. D95's own account (:5277-5278, "D88 to D92 and D94 on Claude Opus 5.5") is where the attribution originates. The fix stays on a plan-named line.

###### DECL-2 (must-fix, stands; confidence medium on severity, high on the facts)

Draft :7 introduces its list with "exactly the changes the plan names (...)" and ends it with "every other line is byte-identical". The plan's exhaustive list also names the go rule's clause (plan :33, :62), line 137's entries (:60) and the checklist (:64-66); the draft carries all three (:143 "a run's core-seconds including its optimiser's (line 68; D95)", :137 "D92, D93 and D95", :204-216). The byte-identical sentence itself holds: the diff gives exactly the 18 lines. Severity is a judgement: the line cites the plan as its authority, but a gate rule is missing from a list introduced as exact. Must-fix kept under "an incomplete declaration".
