### 9.18 Skeptic, dimension 6, consequence lens

#### D97 skeptic's pass, dimension 6 (the declarations): lens of consequence

Repository `C:\\Users\\micha\\OneDrive\\Documents\\roughvollab`, HEAD `3fad362`, read-only. Every citation is to a file at HEAD unless a commit is named; v2's frozen text was read with `git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md`.

##### 1. Lens and summary

Each finding was supposed true, checked against the files, and then weighed by what it would do: break a binding rule, make a declaration false or incomplete, change what a run after the freeze does or records, touch the sealed set, or leave a reader unable to reproduce a step.

Of nine findings, none is refuted; all nine stand on the files. One is re-graded: DECL-2 from must-fix to same-round. DECL-1 stays must-fix. DECL-3 stands but its count and its proposed wording need correcting (all six of v2's seeds are still drawn, not three). DECL-4 to DECL-7 stand at same-round; DECL-8 and DECL-9 stand as notes.

##### 2. The findings

###### DECL-1 (must-fix, confirmed)

`docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v3_draft.md:200` says D91 \"ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code\". `ROADMAP.md:4694-4700` and `4715-4718` split that sitting: the plan's first version was written \"in the session that guides the author\"; the review (nine agents on Claude Fable 5.1) and the corrected copy came from the Claude Code session on Claude Opus 5.5. The phrase \"guides the author\" occurs in the log only at 4695 and 4716; a grep for guid, brief and claude.ai over ROADMAP 4383-5700 finds no other account of that session; the plan's Status (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:3-5`) names no model or product. The Opus attribution traces to D95's summary (`ROADMAP.md:5277-5280`), not to D91.

Consequence: the Oxford AI-use declaration names a model, and a product, for a sitting the log records neither for; the Limitations clause (\"the model for sittings before 11 October 2026 is not recorded\") becomes incomplete; the tool clause \"wrote the plan with candidate (A) (D91)\" inherits the gap. No run, seed or rule is touched, but an incomplete and misleading statement in that line is what must-fix is for. The proposed fix is the smallest one and within plan item 6 (`plan:63`). The fixes entry should note that D95:5277-5280 read D91 as Opus, D95 standing as merged.

###### DECL-2 (must-fix, re-graded same-round)

The fact holds. `draft:7` introduces its list as \"exactly the changes the plan names (...)\" and omits the go rule's clause (`plan:33`, `:62`; carried at `draft:143`), line 137's entries (`plan:60`; `draft:137` \"D92, D93 and D95\"), line 200's sittings (`plan:63`) and the checklist (`plan:64-66`). The diff against 59ca589 gives exactly the 18 lines D95 names.

Consequence: the clause alters no computation (`ROADMAP.md:4912-4913`, `5130-5131`: the optimiser's seconds already fall inside each run's timed window); the plan is cited in the same sentence; the OSF summary names the clause (`docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:32`); line 7 is rewritten at the freeze anyway. An incomplete summary in a line that cites the complete list beside it is \"a missing clause\": same-round. The reviewer's first wording is right; the alternative (\"the lines the plan's items 1, 3, 4 and 6 name\") is also accurate, since item 5 touches no draft line (no `p5v3` in the draft) and item 2 is code only.

###### DECL-3 (same-round, confirmed; count and fix corrected)

`draft:15` says v3's six are drawn \"in place of v2's 79, 89 and 139; 131, 137 and 149\". v3's six are drawn (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:216-220`, `225`, `388-393`, `899`) and v2's six are in the exclusion sets (`:64-69`, `:381-383`). But the reviewer undercounts: `test_p5_v3_transcription.py:234-243` draws all six of v2's seeds through `T.v2_surfaces` and asserts them equal to `R.pilot_points(79, 4)`, `R.noise_draws(89, 4)`, `R.noise_draws(139, 2)`, `R.pilot_points(131, 4)`, `R.noise_draws(137, 4)` and `R.noise_draws(149, 2)`; 89 is also drawn at `test_p5_g2_reference.py:260, 516, 518, 639, 700` and `test_p5_v3_study.py:336`; 79 and 131 at `test_p5_g2_reference.py:395`. D95 (`ROADMAP.md:5412-5414`) said only that one test moved from 79 and 89 to 151 and 157; line 15 generalised it. `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:41` has the same inexactness.

Consequence: nothing of the design is seen anew (v2's pilot θ and ε, declared under D89, on toy maps); the declaration is inexact. Same-round. The proposed wording must name all six of v2's seeds and the transcription check's test, not \"79, 89 and 131\" in the reference's tests alone.

###### DECL-4 (same-round, confirmed)

`draft:15` carries \"the gradient route on stand-in targets\" and D96's narrowed result exactly (`ROADMAP.md:5547-5549`, `5600-5602`), but nothing on what was left unexercised: `5603-5609` (no mass lines, stand-ins or grid checks re-run; every run least-squares at its first attempt, one solution kept; gradient route, repeat and several solutions outside the check), `5520-5526` (D95 (6)), `5151-5152` and `5113` (D94: m = 1 in every run; no repeat).

Consequence: G2's retry, any pair with m > 1 and G3's pilot are the first runs to take those branches; a reader of the frozen text cannot tell that from line 15. Not false, so not must-fix; plan item 6 does not require it, but line 15 is open and v2's line 15 declared the limits of what was computed. The proposed sentence duplicates \"the gradient route on stand-in targets\" and can be shortened.

###### DECL-5 (same-round, confirmed)

Each act is in the log: D91's review read the committed G2 output and computed figures the plan does not carry (`ROADMAP.md:4696-4697`, `4706-4707`); D93's hold mode run from the command line (`4938-4947`, `5066-5073`); D95's hold modes (`5496-5501`); D96's pre-flight and reads during the run (`5636-5642`); D88's hold mode, \"an identity difference of 0.0 over 35 quotes in 41 s\" (`4396-4400`), and `load_emulator()` at the 200 held-out points (`4443-4444`); D89's `g2_status()` and `require_g1()` (`4567-4569`); D94's `require_g1()` (`5166-5167`). `amendment:41` names D91's review; `draft:15` does not. v2's line 15 declared D81's readers' runs, the second review's arithmetic and hash, D82's import parse and D83's check.

Consequence: no posterior computed, no emulator loaded by a hold mode, so nothing seen goes beyond line 15; but v3's line 15 is below v2's grain and differs from the OSF text. Same-round. The fix should say D88's hold mode priced an identity check, since \"read-only\" there is the log's word for \"wrote nothing\".

###### DECL-6 (same-round, confirmed)

`draft:200`'s v3 tool clause omits D90's acts (`ROADMAP.md:4657-4659`) and D92's (`4723-4727`, `4791-4792`), present only as labels in the sittings clause; the author clause omits the OSF act (`4657`), \"agreed the plan\" (`4715`), the merge and the tag (`5688-5689`); \"the two declared departures of D95\" are the title line and the three amended assertions (`5511-5514`, `5530-5532`), unnamed. The material omission is the author's act on the registration record (D90); the rest is housekeeping v2's line 200 did not itemise either. Same-round; the fix could be trimmed to D90, D92 and the naming of the departures.

###### DECL-7 (same-round, confirmed; note defensible)

`draft:75` says the ratio estimator's posterior \"is sampled by the sampler of The exact reference\", so line 9's \"the sampler\" can be read to cover both uses; but the same line gives its start \"by the gradient route\", not (A), and `draft:7` lists the two separately, so `draft:9` is narrower than line 7 and than `plan:74`, `:76`, `:120-125`. The retry clause and the go rule's clause are reached only through the plan's list, which line 9 cites. No rule or run affected; wording. Same-round.

###### DECL-8 (note, confirmed)

`draft:7` and `:140` \"ran the adopted sampler\": (A) was adopted after the pass (`ROADMAP.md:5098`, `5136-5137`); `draft:15` says \"ran candidate (A)\". `draft:7` cites D89 without D90 (`amendment:7` cites both) and \"(D91; D95)\" without D96, which changed lines 15, 200 and 215 (`5643-5653`). Also: `draft:15`'s \"Declared for v3 (D95)\" heads a passage D96 edited. Note.

`ROADMAP.md:4338-4341` (D86) and `4348-4382` (D87) name no model; a case-insensitive grep for opus, fable and model over 4243-4382 returns nothing. v2's line 200 gives the second review's session's model, so D86's guiding session has one by reference, while D86's \"this sitting\" and D87's have none. ### 9.19 Reviewer, dimension 7: the tests and the unexercised paths

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review, dimension 7: the tests, CI, and the unexercised run paths

Read-only, at `main` 3fad362, the tracked tree clean. Nothing imported, run or drawn from the repository's scripts; the two Python commands under section 4 read the committed records with the standard library only.

##### 1. Verdict

The P5 test files, the CI workflow and the two recorded runs (D94's study, D96's transcription check) agree with what D95 and D96 declare, and every count reproduces from the files: 372 tests collected (339 functions and 33 parametrised cases, `CONTRIBUTING.md:30`), 8 skips in CI, all of them torch tests, and the local counts D95 gives per file. One finding is must-fix and lies on a path a run after the freeze will take: `score_set` scores every pair of a set whatever its reference's status, and under v3 a failed pair has no Σ_P, so `NRETarget` raises an uncaught ValueError and `--score g3` or `--score test` crashes at the first reference-failure pair, a case the protocol anticipates (Stage 1 tolerates failures up to 10% per level) and no test covers. The other findings are tests worth strengthening in the same round, on paths the reading finds sound but which no test drives to their end (the repeat's fresh start, m > 1 by the least-squares route, the calibrators' entry point past its refusals), and observations for the author on what has never been exercised anywhere: the gradient route on a trained ReLU network at the written acceptance, the repeat and m > 1 on G̃, and the fork case, which runs only in CI, whose result for the review commit is not in the repository.

##### 2. Findings

**TESTS-1 (must-fix). `score_set` crashes on a reference-failure pair: `NRETarget` refuses the missing Σ_P with an uncaught ValueError.**
*Claim.* `score_set` selects every record of the set at the level with no filter on `status` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:887`) and, for the ratio estimator, passes `((r.get(\"under\") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get(\"sigma_P\")` (`:906-907`). A pair whose reference failed has `under == {}`: `reference_pair` initialises the record with `\"under\": {}` and returns it on `run is None` (`p5_g2_reference.py:816-825`). Σ_P is then None and `NRETarget.__init__` raises ValueError (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:432-434`), which neither `nre_posterior` (`:479-498`) nor `score_set` catches: the run stops with a traceback at the first failed pair of the first ratio-estimator cell, after that cell's ensemble rows were written, and a resume stops at the same pair. v2 scored failed pairs too (`git show 59ca589:p5_g3_calibrators.py`, lines 818 and 837) but its `nre_posterior` needed no Σ_P, so the crash is new to v3. No test runs `score_set` over references holding a failed record (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:341-390` and `:545-588` use all-`ok` or empty refs); the only tests of a missing Σ_P are the direct ValueError at `:235-236` and `:293-294`. Stage 2 never reads a failed pair's score rows: `measures` iterates the accepted `pairs` (`p5_stage2.py:460-463`), and the synthetic scores of `test_p5_stage2.py:223-262` already hold rows for the planted failed pair without effect.
*Bears on.* Draft line 75 (Σ_P read from the pair's reference record) and line 101 (the test set scored after Stage 1, which tolerates reference failures); the plan's \"What v3 does not change: the scoring\"; the runs `--score g3` and `--score test` after the freeze whenever any G3-pilot or test reference failed (`pilot_surfaces` removes failures for the gate, `:513`, but `score_set` does not).
*Proposed fix.* In `score_set` take `rows` as the records with `r.get(\"status\") == \"ok\"`, for both calibrators (a failed pair's score is never read); add to `test_seeds_scoring_and_the_retraining_rule`, or a new torch test, one reference with `status: reference_failure` and `under: {}` among `refs`, asserting that `score_set` returns the count for the accepted pairs only and writes no row for the failed one; keep the direct ValueError test for an `ok` record without Σ_P (D95's declared refusal). State in D97's record that the fix changes no registered rule.

**TESTS-2 (same-round). `test_repeat_then_reference_failure` runs the repeat but asserts nothing about its start.**
*Claim.* The test monkeypatches only `split_rhat` to 1.5 (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:164`), so `_run_with_repeat` runs the real `run_chains` twice, at (200, 400) and (400, 800), on the same generator continuing (`p5_g2_reference.py:785-800`), and `run_chains` draws the 16 × 4 starts and 4 × 4 perturbations first on every call (`:574-576`): the repeat with the new start is exercised. The assertions (`:166-170`) check the lengths, `accepted is False`, `rhat[\"H\"] == 1.5`, the seed and the empty `under`, and nothing about the second attempt's `start`: that it exists, has 16 starts and differs from the first's, as line 68 requires (\"the start and first proposal above run again from the same generator continuing (16 fresh optimiser starts)\"). The repeat has never run on G̃: D94 and D96 report every run accepted at its first attempt, and the committed check record has one attempt for every (pair, prior).
*Bears on.* Draft line 68, the repeat clause; the plan's candidate (A), item 6; any G2 or test reference whose first attempt fails acceptance.
*Proposed fix.* Add `assert all(\"start\" in a and a[\"start\"][\"n_starts\"] == 16 and a[\"start\"][\"route\"] == \"least_squares\" for a in att)` and `assert not np.array_equal(att[0][\"start\"][\"starts\"], att[1][\"start\"][\"starts\"])`; optionally replay a generator through the first run (as `:812-836` does) and assert the second attempt's starts are the next 16 × 4 uniforms.

**TESTS-3 (same-round). No test drives the least-squares route to m > 1, so the multi-solution bookkeeping and the polish-merge branch are unexercised by any test or run.**
*Claim.* Every emulator the reference's tests use is the linear toy `LinearEm` (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:26-42`), whose posterior has one mode; the tests assert `m == 1` (`:145`, `:783`). m = 2 is reached only through the gradient route on `_TwoGaussians` (`:784-788`), where `_modes_gradient` has neither polish nor merge. The least-squares route's bookkeeping for several kept solutions (`p5_g2_reference.py:440-475`: `pos_map`, the remap of `reached`, `n_starts_reached` per kept solution) and its merge of two solutions polished to one point (`:459-462`) are exercised only through the hand-built unit test of `keep_solutions` (`:850-851`); the merge branch runs in no test. On G̃, all 55 runs of D94 and D96 kept one solution (confirmed from the committed check record: m = 1 for 55 of 55). Reading finds no bug.
*Bears on.* Draft line 68, steps 2 to 4; the plan's candidate (A), step 4 (\"Several modes ... Such a pair fails, as G2 already requires\"); any reference after the freeze whose likelihood has two modes.
*Proposed fix.* A toy emulator in memory symmetric in x_H (for example `LinearEm` on θ with H folded about the box's centre): under \"uniform\" assert `m == 2`, `sum(n_starts_reached) + starts_elsewhere == 16`, `chain_solution == [0, 1, 0, 1]` and that `accept` fails, then `reference_pair` recording a reference failure whose attempts carry m = 2; under \"A\" assert both kept solutions polished and `n_merged_after_polish == 0`; with a second toy whose two minima polish to one point under the prior, assert m = 1 with `n_merged_after_polish == 1`.

**TESTS-4 (same-round). `p5_g3_calibrators.main` is never driven past its refusals: the hand-off of G2's lengths into `post_kw` and the `hashes` dict of `--score` are untested; `p5_stage2.main --run` likewise.**
*Claim.* `test_entry_points_hold_and_refuse` (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:393-404`) stops at \"is not G̃\" or \"no emulator\"; `run_g3`, `train_seeds` and `score_set` are tested as functions with `post_kw` supplied by the test. The entry point's own work (`require_pilot` on the G3-pilot record, `st = R.require_g2()`, `post_kw = {\"burn\": st[\"burn\"], \"sample\": st[\"sample\"]}`, `p5_g3_calibrators.py:951-954`; the `hashes` dict for `--score`, `:981-984`; the `trained_path` round trip, `:971-978`) runs in no test, so a retry's doubled lengths reaching the ratio estimator's sampler is checked only in the reference's entry point (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:506-574`, with recorders). `p5_stage2.main`'s `--run` branch (`p5_stage2.py:968-980`) is driven by no test either: `S.main` is called for the hold and `--stage1` (`test_p5_stage2.py:313`, `:538-539`, `:557`), `S.run` directly.
*Bears on.* Draft line 75 (lengths doubled with the reference's after a retry) and line 143 (\"the ratio estimator's sampler included\"); the runs `--g3`, `--train`, `--score g3`, `--score test` and `p5_stage2.py --run` after the freeze.
*Proposed fix.* A numpy-only test driving `C.main` for `--g3`, `--train` and `--score test` with `_HAS_TORCH` forced True and `require_torch`, `E.emulator_for_run`, `E.load_emulator`, `E.emulator_info`, `R.SETS`, `PATHS`, `run_g3`, `train_seeds` and `score_set` replaced by recorders (the pattern of `test_the_retry_and_the_hand_off_of_g2s_lengths`), a passed retry report of lengths (20000, 80000) at `R.SETS[\"g2r\"][\"report\"]` and a 48-pair toy G3-pilot record with the toy's hash; assert `post_kw == {\"burn\": 20000, \"sample\": 80000}` reaches `run_g3` and `score_set` and that `hashes` carries G̃, the G3 report and the set's references. For Stage 2, drive `S.main([\"--run\", ...])` once on the synthetic inputs.

**TESTS-5 (note). CI's eight skips are the torch tests; the fork cases of the two pool tests run only in CI, whose result is not in the repository.**
*Claim.* With `requirements.txt` (numpy, scipy, matplotlib) on Python 3.12 (`.github/workflows/tests.yml:17-29`) torch is absent, so exactly eight tests skip: the six `_needs_torch` tests of `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py` (`test_models_losses_and_batch_construction`, `test_training_loop_stops_and_statuses`, `test_calibrator_outputs_in_box_units_and_the_nre_posterior`, `test_autograd_gradient_against_a_float64_finite_difference_on_a_toy_network`, `test_toy_g3_ladder_end_to_end`, `test_seeds_scoring_and_the_retraining_rule`; `:24`, `:120`, `:143`, `:175`, `:239`, `:305`, `:341`) and the two of `test_layer3_deep_hedging.py` (`test_torch_autograd_sanity`, `test_gate1_tiny_recovers_delta_direction`; `:18`, `:63`, `:68`). The fork cases (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:680-692`, `test_p5_v3_study.py:348-354`) skip on Windows and run on Linux, so 364 pass in CI if all pass. The figure \"364 passed, 8 skipped\" the brief attributes to D95 is not in `ROADMAP.md` at HEAD (D95 says \"The suite collects 372 tests\", 5452-5454), and no CI result for 579b525 or 3fad362 is recorded in the repository, so the fork path's passing at HEAD could not be verified from the files. The 372 reproduces: 339 `def test_` functions over the 30 root-level test files plus 33 parametrised cases (binance 4, identifiability 3, kline 2, layer1c 6, p5_g2 1, p5_v3_study 1, rough_heston_cf 8, roughvol_core 6, rv_series 2); no conftest.py, pytest.ini or pyproject.toml alters collection.
*Bears on.* The plan, item 2 (the pool test \"must pass under both start methods\"); draft line 15 (\"run on the author's machine and in CI\"); D97's record.
*Proposed fix.* No code change. For the author: cite CI's run for the review commit in D97 or the freeze entry, so the fork case's passing is on record.

**TESTS-6 (note). The gradient route has never run on a trained ReLU network at the written acceptance and lengths; the `value_grad_unit` branch runs only on toys.**
*Claim.* The route on a network runs in three torch tests, all skipped in CI: a hand-built quadratic logit at rhat_max 1.1, ess_min 50 (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:205-236`); an untrained ReLU trunk through `run_chains` at burn 200, sample 400, with no acceptance (`:297-302`); toy-trained networks (3 epochs on 64 to 128 surfaces) through `run_g3` and `score_set` at rhat_max 5.0, ess_min 1.0 and lengths 100/200 and 50/100 (`:318-321`, `:359-361`). On G̃ the route ran only on the study's stand-ins (144 runs, D94) through `grad_unit` with finite differences; `value_grad_unit` (`p5_g2_reference.py:491-496`) is exercised by the toy `_WithValue` target (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:794-799`) and `NRETarget` only. R-hat ≤ 1.01 and ESS ≥ 2,000 on a network target, and L-BFGS-B's behaviour on a piecewise-linear logit, are first seen at G3's pilot, as D95's declaration (6) says (ROADMAP 5503-5509).
*Bears on.* Draft line 75; the plan, Decision 3; the run `--g3`.
*Proposed fix.* None required; the author decides whether one `nre_posterior` at the written lengths on a toy-trained network in `.venv-p5` (minutes, writes nothing, sees nothing of G̃) is worth recording; D97's record should carry declaration (6) forward.

**TESTS-7 (note). Draft line 15's inventory of generators the tests draw omits the ratio estimator's `[107, p, q, i, j]`.**
*Claim.* Line 15 lists 73; 151, 157, 163; 167, 173, 179; 97, 101; `[83, i, j]`; prefixes of 109 and 113. The torch tests also draw `default_rng([107, 1, 1, 0, 1])` explicitly (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:228-234`) and through `nre_posterior` (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:485`), as v2's test already did (`git show 59ca589:test_p5_g3_calibrators.py`, line 216); the study's tests draw it at q = 0 (`test_p5_v3_study.py:78`, `:460`, `:472`), a slot the design never uses. ###### 2a. What each P5 test file exercises, and what skips (part a)

- `test_p5_g2_reference.py` (20 functions, 21 cases): the literals including v3's six seeds and their prime rule, the exclusion set with v2's six, v3's record names and the start's constants (`test_literals_are_the_written_ones`); split R-hat and ESS on synthetic chains; the weighted summaries, quantisation and priors; the sampler on a linear toy against its exact Gaussian posterior with the start's diagnostics (`test_sampler_on_a_linear_emulator_matches_its_gaussian_posterior`); the repeat then a reference failure (`test_repeat_then_reference_failure`); reweighting, forced direct runs, the grid and whole-box checks (`test_reweighting_direct_runs_and_grid_against_the_gaussian`, `test_g2_grid_adds_the_mean_under_B_and_the_quantiles_under_A`); the draws on pilot seeds and the refusals of `main` before any load, with the sealed-draw guard (`test_draws_surfaces_and_the_sealed_set`); a toy pilot's record, resume and report at pool 1 (`test_toy_pilot_record_resume_and_report`); the coverage covariate and the weighted effective size; the pilot set's fixed points, the retry's seeds, `project_cost` and `g2_status` on hand-written reports with `main`'s retry and G2 refusals (`test_g2_pilot_set_fixed_points_retry_and_go_no_go`); the test set's definition on a recorder and the version guard (`test_the_test_set_is_fixed_and_the_entry_point_guards_the_versions`); the retry's hand-off of lengths through `main` with `run_set` and `g2_report` replaced by recorders (`test_the_retry_and_the_hand_off_of_g2s_lengths`); the four-standard-error tolerance; the refusals of `--n`/`--noise-seed`, another emulator and a changed emulator hash (`test_the_test_set_and_the_emulator_are_fixed_before_any_draw_or_run`); both pool paths at pool 2 on a toy emulator file under spawn and fork (`test_the_pool_paths_give_every_pair_what_one_process_gives`, the fork case skipping where the platform lacks it, `:691-692`); the written-in start equal to the study's candidate bit for bit, the generator's draw order replayed, Σ_P in the record reweighted and direct, and a resume with other start constants refused (the four D95 tests, `:768-909`).
- `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py` (17 functions): numpy-only tests of the literals, the draws' layout and nesting, G3's checks and budget on hand-built numbers, `pilot_surfaces` with Σ_P (`:41-116`); the per-cell ladder, the scoring projection, fail-closed behaviour, the override line and the version guard with training and prediction replaced by stand-ins (`_fake_ladder`, `:407-543`); the Stage-1 and hash refusals of scoring the test set (`:545-588`); `require_pilot` and the resume refusals (`:592-636`); the entry point's hold and refusals (`:393-404`, branching on torch). Six torch tests, marked `_needs_torch` (`:24`) and skipped without torch: the models and losses, the training loop's statuses, the ensemble's outputs and `nre_posterior` through `NRETarget` on a hand-built logit with the kept mode checked against the exact one-dimensional mode, autograd's gradient against a float64 finite difference on a toy ReLU trunk with the route through `run_chains`, a toy G3 ladder end to end with resume, and the seeds, retraining and `score_set` on a toy set.
- `test_p5_stage2.py` (15 functions): the literals including v3's record name; binning, floors and distinct θ; the measures, m from a toy store and line 75's exclusions; the seed floor; the weighted median and crossed bootstrap; the verdict rule and the null; an end-to-end run on synthetic records with a planted reference failure and network failure, including resume and the refusals of duplicated and mismatched inputs; thin bins; the null for a faithful calibrator; Stage 1 alone and Stage 2's refusals without it or on altered inputs, with the stop above 10% failures; the secondary analysis and lost G3 cells; `main` for the hold, `--stage1` and the version guard; an empty bin from the command line; the emulator-hash agreement where records meet. The synthetic records carry `\"runs\": {}` and no `start` or Σ_P.
- `test_p5_v3_study.py` (12 functions, 13 cases): the study's settings as literals and the sealed-seed refusal; the candidate recovering the Gaussian posterior with its diagnostics; the draw order; mode retention and the mass rule with the bimodal gradient-route target; Σ from the Jacobian and its clipping; the prior's derivatives, the direct-run polish and ε; the candidate installed inside spawned and forked workers at pool 2 with the checks, mass lines and stand-ins, and the written-in sampler giving the same chains (the fork case skipping on Windows, `:353-354`); the stand-in targets and the gradient route; the mass outside the first grid; the verdict and the comparison with v2; hold mode and the refusals; a small `--run` end to end on toy records at pool 1 with resume.
- `test_p5_v3_transcription.py` (4 functions): the names and literals; a toy study record reproduced through the reference's own worker on the reweighted and direct paths, each changed field or array caught and named; the entry point on toy records with the refusals and hold mode; v2's surfaces from the study's seeds.
- Skimmed: `test_p5_g0_pilot.py` (8) and `test_p5_g0_main.py` (9) guard G0's machinery on cheap pricer settings; `test_p5_g1_emulator.py` (13) the Chebyshev mathematics, toy builds, the version guard and `require_g1`/`emulator_for_run`/`require_same_emulator`/`refuse_overwrite` (`:362-411`). None skips.
- CI (`.github/workflows/tests.yml`): Ubuntu, Python 3.12, `requirements.txt` and pytest, `python -m pytest -q`: 372 collected, 8 skipped (the six torch tests above and the two of `test_layer3_deep_hedging.py`), the two fork cases running; everything that touches torch, `NRETarget`, `nre_posterior`, `run_g3`, `train_seeds` and `score_set` for real runs only in `.venv-p5` on the author's machine.

###### 2b. The run paths after the freeze, and what has exercised each (part b)

Exercised by a test (what it stubs) / by a recorded run / neither:

- **G2 `--pilot`, `launch` with `_worker` and `reference_pair`, the least-squares route, at pool 4 on G̃:** test at pool 1 on an in-memory toy (`test_toy_pilot_record_resume_and_report`) and at pool 2 on a toy file (`test_the_pool_paths...`, `run_set` and `g2_report` called directly, `EMULATOR_PATH` pointed away from G̃); recorded by D96 at pool 4 on G̃ with `R._worker` (`p5_v3_transcription.py:194-205`) on v2's 36 seen surfaces at the gate's lengths and acceptance, and by D94 with the study's worker wrapping `R._worker`. `run_set` itself (make_jobs → header → launch) has run on toys only; `main --pilot`'s branch has never run past the emulator refusal in any test (the identical branch runs for `--pilot-retry` with recorders).
- **The repeat at doubled lengths with the new start:** test on the toy (`test_repeat_then_reference_failure`, `split_rhat` replaced); never on G̃ (TESTS-2).
- **Direct runs under A and B with the polish:** tests (forced by `kish_min=1e12`, `:193-198`, `:880-884`); D94 and D96 (19 direct runs on G̃).
- **m > 1 through the least-squares route, the polish-merge branch:** neither (TESTS-3); `keep_solutions` unit-tested; m = 2 only by the gradient route on a toy.
- **`g2_report`'s grid pool with `emulator_path`, the whole-box checks, `project_cost`, the report:** tests at pool 1 and 2 on toys (`grid_n` 4 to 8, `whole_n` 4); D94 at pool 4 on G̃ at the written grids (`p5_v3_study.py:857-878`), 36 first grids and 12 whole-box grids, the projection and `go`.
- **`g2_status`, `require_g2`:** tests on hand-written reports (`:404-428`, `:556-574`); no recorded run (the study runs no gate).
- **`--pilot-retry` with a failed first report: doubled lengths, seeds 167/173/179, the require of the first report, the hand-off through `g2_status`:** test with `run_set` and `g2_report` replaced by recorders, `require_g1` and `get_emulator` replaced (`:506-574`); the seeds and surfaces real (`g2_surfaces(retry=True)`, `:391-395`); never run.
- **`--g3-pilot` and `--test` after a passing G2: `require_g2`, the lengths from `g2_status`, the extra header:** the same recorder test (`:567-574`); the refusals of `--n` and `--noise-seed` real (`:627-631`); the sealed draw itself never made, by rule (recorders at `:547-555`, `_NoDraw` at `:459-491`).
- **G3 `--g3` (`run_g3` with `nre_posterior` on reference records carrying Σ_P, the gradient route on a trained ReLU network, `require_pilot`):** torch tests on toy-trained networks at relaxed acceptance (`test_toy_g3_ladder_end_to_end`, `test_seeds_scoring...`); numpy tests with `get_or_train` and `predict_rows` replaced (`_fake_ladder`); `require_pilot` on hand-built records; never run; the entry point's hand-off untested (TESTS-4); the route at the written acceptance never exercised (TESTS-6).
- **`--train`, `--score g3`, `--score test` (`score_set` reading `under[P][\"sigma_P\"]`, the doubled lengths via `post_kw`, `require_stage1`):** torch tests on toy sets with all-`ok` references (`:341-390`); the Stage-1 and hash refusals (`:545-588`); never run; a failed reference pair in the set crashes it (TESTS-1).
- **Stage 1 (`run_stage1` on test references whose attempts carry `start`) and Stage 2 (`run`, `refs_digest` over such records):** tests on synthetic records without `runs` or `start` (`test_p5_stage2.py:225`); nothing in `p5_stage2.py` reads the attempts (`load_refs`, `:208-237`; `refs_digest` hashes whole records, `:177-187`), so the records' `start` changes only the digest's input and the files' size (about 10 MB for the test set, D95 (7)); never run on real records.
- **A resume after an interruption, with `lengths[\"start\"]` in the header:** tests of the header check and of a pair lacking its samples (`:268-269`, `:287-291`, `:893-909`) at pool 1 on a toy; an interrupted pool (`launch`'s `except BaseException`, `p5_g2_reference.py:1186-1190`) then a resume is in no test and no recorded run (D96 launched with none done before).
- **`p5_v3_transcription.py`:** its paths run and recorded (D96); tests on toy records (`test_p5_v3_transcription.py`).

###### 2c. What an end-to-end tiny run would need, for each path marked neither (part c)

Whether to make any of these before the freeze is the author's decision; none was run here.

- **The repeat on G̃.** `reference_pair(y, s, em, i, j, burn=1000, sample=4000, ess_min=1e9)` in a Python session on one of v2's 36 seen surfaces (its `y` from `output/p5v3_study.jsonl`), G̃ loaded through `require_g1`; the forced failure makes both attempts run (about 5,000 and 10,000 steps per chain: by D94's 68 s per 50,000 steps, well under a minute plus two optimiser runs of about a second). It writes nothing if called in memory (or to a temporary jsonl through `run_set` with a paths dict under a temporary folder, never under `output/p5v3_*` or v2's names). It sees a posterior of a surface already seen and declared; it draws `[83, i, j]`, no sealed seed.
- **m > 1 by the least-squares route.** A toy emulator in memory with two exact modes (TESTS-3), `run_chains` under \"uniform\" and \"A\" at burn 300, sample 900: seconds; writes nothing; sees nothing. No surface of G̃ is known to be multimodal, so it cannot be forced on G̃ without a search that would itself see new posteriors.
- **`main --pilot` end to end.** On G̃ it is forbidden before the freeze: `g2_surfaces()` draws v3's G2 surfaces (seeds 151, 157, 163) and the posterior of any of them would be a reference posterior of v3's own gate (draft line 7). On a toy file it needs `E.require_g1` pointed at the toy and `SETS` redirected to a temporary folder, as the tests do, and the written lengths and grids (they are module constants, not flags): 18 pairs at 50,000 steps on a tiny Chebyshev toy at pool 4, with 18 grids of 32^4 cells and 6 whole-box grids — of the order of half an hour to an hour; it writes the three g2 files under the redirected paths only; it sees nothing. The same code runs in the recorder test for `--pilot-retry`.
- **`p5_g3_calibrators.main` past its refusals (`--g3`, `--train`, `--score`).** Real objects cannot exist before the freeze (the G3-pilot record is a gate output). On toys: the recorder test of TESTS-4 (seconds, numpy only), or, in `.venv-p5`, a toy G3-pilot record of 48 pairs from a toy emulator file with `require_g1`, `R.SETS`, `PATHS` and `scores_path` redirected and the ladder shortened by monkeypatch: minutes; writes under the redirected paths; sees nothing.
- **The gradient route on a trained ReLU network at the written acceptance.** In `.venv-p5`, `nre_posterior` on the ladder test's toy-trained network at burn 10,000, sample 40,000, rhat_max 1.01, ess_min 2,000: 200,000 forward passes of a 39→128→128→128→1 network per chain, a few minutes on the CPU; in memory, writes nothing; sees nothing of G̃; it shows L-BFGS-B's statuses and m on a ReLU logit, the diagnostic D95 (6) defers to G3's pilot.
- **Stage 1 and Stage 2 on records carrying `start`.** Cheapest as a test: give the synthetic records a `runs` dict with attempts carrying a 6 KB `start` and assert `refs_digest` and `run_stage1` run (seconds). A real alternative exists: `py -3 p5_stage2.py --stage1 --refs output/p5v3_transcription.jsonl --out-dir <a folder outside output/>` would accept that record (36 pairs of `n_points` 12, G̃'s hash in the header, no `g2` key), run in seconds and write `p5_stage1_report.json`, `p5_stage1.csv` and the PNG there; but it would apply Stage 1's binning and coverage to seen pilot posteriors, a new reading of seen data that the author must weigh (it looks at no hypothesis and no test surface).
- **A resume after an interruption.** On the toy file at pool 2: interrupt `run_set` mid-pool (a KeyboardInterrupt in a wrapper), then call it again and assert the record and samples equal a pool-1 run's: one to two minutes; writes under temporary paths; sees nothing.
- **The sealed draw.** Never, by rule; the recorder tests stand in for it.

### 9.20 Skeptic, dimension 7, correctness lens

#### D97 skeptic's check of the dimension-7 findings (the tests, CI and the unexercised paths)

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: correctness, against the files at HEAD (3fad362). Every cited line was read in its surrounding code, with the other side of each comparison (the frozen blob at 59ca589, the draft, the plan, the log entries, the tests).

Of the eight findings, none is refuted and none is re-graded: TESTS-1 stands as must-fix; TESTS-2, TESTS-3 and TESTS-4 stand as same-round; TESTS-5 to TESTS-8 stand as notes. Three findings carry small pointer or wording slips that do not touch their substance (TESTS-3's :783 and ROADMAP 5165-5167; TESTS-6's description of the second toy test's size). Three own observations are added, all notes.

##### 2. The findings

###### TESTS-1 (must-fix): stands

`score_set` selects every record of the set at the level with no filter on `status` (p5_g3_calibrators.py:887) and passes `((r.get("under") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get("sigma_P")` to `nre_posterior` (:906-907). `NRETarget.__init__` raises `ValueError` when `sigma_P` is None (:432-434); the constructor is evaluated as an argument of `R._run_with_repeat` inside `nre_posterior` (:487-488), and neither `nre_posterior` (:479-498), `score_set` nor `main` (:976-986) catches it. `reference_pair` initialises `"under": {}` (p5_g2_reference.py:817) and returns it on the uniform run's failure (:822-825); a failure in a direct run (:844-847) leaves the earlier prior's summary in place, so a failed record lacks Σ_P under at least one prior. `_worker` adds `y`, `theta`, `task` and `set` to the failed record (:1058), so it reaches the ensemble path silently and the ratio-estimator path with Σ_P None. v2's `score_set` took the same unfiltered rows (59ca589:p5_g3_calibrators.py:818) and called `nre_posterior` without Σ_P (:837): the crash is new with D95.

Rows are appended as they are made (:897-911), so the ensemble rows of the cell precede the crash; a resume rebuilds `todo` from the pair whose row was never written (:891) and crashes again. The tests at :341-390 and :545-588 use all-`ok` refs (:351) or empty refs (:561-583); the missing-Σ_P tests are the direct `ValueError`s at :235-236 and :293-294. Stage 2 reads only the accepted `pairs` (p5_stage2.py:216-221, :456-459); its completeness checks are at (calibrator, prior, level, slot) granularity (:846-852, :463-467); the synthetic inputs write score rows for the planted failed pair (test_p5_stage2.py:223, :248-262) and Stage 2 ignores them (:275-276). Line 101 tolerates test reference failures up to 10% per level and `require_pilot` accepts a G3-pilot record holding failures (p5_g3_calibrators.py:622-635), so both `--score g3` and `--score test` take the path after the freeze. The plan's binding "the scoring" (P5_v3_plan.md:78) is the rule of lines 75 and 101, which never asks a failed pair to be scored; the proposed filter changes no registered rule. Must-fix stands.

###### TESTS-2 (same-round): stands

The test monkeypatches `split_rhat` alone (test_p5_g2_reference.py:164) and runs `reference_pair` at (200, 400) (:165). `_run_with_repeat` calls `run_chains` twice on the same generator object (p5_g2_reference.py:790-792); each call draws 16 × 4 fresh uniforms first (:577); each attempt record carries `start` (:797). The assertions (:166-170) check lengths, `accepted`, `rhat["H"]`, `seed`, `under` and `c_A` only. The fields the proposed assertions name exist in `start` (:614-615). D94 (ROADMAP.md:5116-5117) and D96 (:5605-5608) state every run was accepted at its first attempt; no other test in the suite builds a doubled-length attempt.

###### TESTS-3 (same-round): stands

The reference's tests use only `LinearEm` (test_p5_g2_reference.py:26-42). `m == 1` is asserted at :145; :783 asserts the route and the polish, with m = 1 following from :782's equality with the study's candidate rather than from a direct assertion (a minor misattribution). m = 2 arises only through the gradient route on `_TwoGaussians` (:784-788; test_p5_v3_study.py:209-213), where `_modes_gradient` polishes and merges nothing (p5_g2_reference.py:478-515). The multi-solution bookkeeping of `_modes_least_squares` (:444-473) and its merge branch (:459-463) run in no test with m > 1; the study's own copy is exercised at m = 1 under A (test_p5_v3_study.py:289). D94's "one kept solution in every run" is at ROADMAP.md:5151-5152 (the finding cites 5165-5167); D96 at :5605-5606. The proposed toy follows from the claim with two cautions for the author: `toy_surface`'s default θ is `CENTRE`, where a fold about the centre gives one mode, so θ must sit off the fold; and under A the mass window keeps the mirrored mode only for u roughly in (0.19, 0.81), since (a − b)·log(u/(1 − u)) with a − b = −6.5 must stay within log 10⁴.

###### TESTS-4 (same-round): stands

`test_entry_points_hold_and_refuse` stops at "is not G̃", "no emulator" or "torch is not in this environment" (test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:393-404); the other `C.main` calls (:539-542) are the hold and the version guard. `require_pilot` on the record (p5_g3_calibrators.py:948-949), `st = R.require_g2()` and `post_kw` (:950-951), the `trained_path` round trip (:967-978) and the `hashes` dict (:983) run in no test; the function tests pass `post_kw` themselves (:321, :359-361, :447). The reference's hand-off is tested through its entry point with recorders (test_p5_g2_reference.py:533-574). `p5_stage2.main`'s `--run` branch (p5_stage2.py:966-971) is driven by no test: `S.main` is called for the hold (test_p5_stage2.py:313, :533, :536) and `--stage1` (:538-539, :557), `S.run` directly.

TESTS-1's status filter covers both calibrators.

### 9.21 Skeptic, dimension 7, consequence lens

#### D97, dimension 7 (the tests, CI and the unexercised paths): the skeptic's pass

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: consequence. Each of the eight findings was taken as true, checked against the files at HEAD (3fad362) and, where it matters, against the frozen v2 blobs at 59ca589, and then weighed: does it break a binding rule of the plan, make a declaration false or incomplete, change what a run after the freeze does or records, touch the sealed set, or leave a step irreproducible.

Result: none refuted; eight stand; one re-graded (TESTS-7, note to same-round). Everything was read, nothing run: no script, test, pricer or import, no emulator or archive opened, no committed jsonl read (the figures the findings take from output/p5v3_transcription.jsonl were instead confirmed from D94 and D96's text).

##### 2. The findings

###### TESTS-1 (must-fix; stands as must-fix)

`score_set` takes every record of the set at the level with no filter on status (p5_g3_calibrators.py:887) and, for the ratio estimator, passes `((r.get(\"under\") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get(\"sigma_P\")` to `nre_posterior` (:906-907); `NRETarget.__init__` raises `ValueError` on `None` (:432-434), and neither `nre_posterior` (:479-498) nor `score_set` catches it. Failed references do reach those rows: `_worker` adds `y`, `task` and `set` to every record (p5_g2_reference.py:1058), and `reference_pair` returns `\"under\": {}` when the uniform run fails (:816-818, :822-825). One precision: when a direct run fails after the other prior was reweighted, `under` holds that prior only (:843-847), so prior A's cell scores the pair (wasted work) and prior B's crashes; either way the run stops. v2 scored failed pairs too but needed no Σ_P (59ca589:p5_g3_calibrators.py:818, :837), so the crash is v3's. No test covers it (test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:351-354 all `ok`; :561-583 empty refs; :235-236 and :293-294 the direct refusal only). Stage 2 reads none of these rows: `load_refs` drops non-`ok` records (p5_stage2.py:217-221), `measures` iterates the accepted pairs (:456-467) and `load_scores` builds `present` from the rows that exist (:268-276). A resume crashes at the same pair, since `todo` is rebuilt from `done` (:891).

Consequence: a crash on a path the protocol itself anticipates (line 101 tolerates reference failures up to 10% per level), mid-scoring, after the freeze. Must-fix is right. The proposed fix, rows with `status == \"ok\"` and a test with a planted failure, is the smallest; it changes no registered rule (line 75's failure clause concerns the calibrator's own sampler; line 101 removes a failed reference from the count) and needs a line in D97's record, not a change of OSF text.

###### TESTS-2 (same-round; stands)

The test patches `split_rhat` only (test_p5_g2_reference.py:164) and calls `reference_pair` (:165); `_run_with_repeat` runs the real `run_chains` twice on one generator (p5_g2_reference.py:790-800), each run drawing its 16 x 4 starts and 4 x 4 normals first (:577-578), so the repeat with fresh starts is exercised. The assertions (:166-170) check lengths, `accepted`, `rhat[\"H\"]`, the seed, `under` and `c_A`, nothing about `start`. The keys the fix would assert exist (`start` on each attempt, :797; `route`, `n_starts`, `starts`, :614-615). No repeat has run on G-tilde: D94 (ROADMAP.md:5116-5117) and D96 (:5605-5606) say every run was accepted at its first attempt. A weak test, not a bug: same-round is right, and the two assertions are the right size.

###### TESTS-3 (same-round; stands)

Every least-squares run in the tests is on `LinearEm`, one mode: `m == 1` at test_p5_g2_reference.py:145 and in the study's tests (test_p5_v3_study.py:91, :289), `n_merged_after_polish == 0` (:101, :297); `m == 2` only through the gradient route on `_TwoGaussians` (test_p5_g2_reference.py:784-788), whose `_modes_gradient` has no polish (p5_g2_reference.py:513). The remap through `pos_map`, the per-solution `n_starts_reached` and the merge branch (:444-475, :459-463) run with one kept solution in every test; `keep_solutions` alone is unit-tested (:850-851). D94 records one kept solution in every run (ROADMAP.md:5151-5152); D96 says the keeping of several lay outside the check (:5605-5609). Reading finds no bug in the remap. Same-round is right. The folded toy is plausible (equal cost and equal J give equal mass; `chain_solution == [0, 1, 0, 1]` follows from :403); the merge toy is harder to build and may be left to the author.

###### TESTS-4 (same-round; stands, with one correction to the fix)

`test_entry_points_hold_and_refuse` stops at the hold or the refusals (test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:393-404); the other `main` calls are the hold and the version guard (:539-542). `require_pilot` on the record, `R.require_g2()`, `post_kw` from `st` (p5_g3_calibrators.py:948-951), the `trained_path` round trip (:967-978) and the `hashes` dict (:983-985) run in no test; `run_g3`, `train_seeds` and `score_set` get `post_kw` from the tests. For Stage 2, `S.main` is driven for the hold and `--stage1` only (test_p5_stage2.py:313, :533-539, :557); the `--run` branch (p5_stage2.py:966-978) runs in no test. The hand-off reads correctly against the reference's (p5_g2_reference.py:1494, :1499). Same-round is right. The proposed test needs one change: `torch` is `None` when absent (p5_g3_calibrators.py:105) and `main` prints `torch.__version__` under `_HAS_TORCH` (:936), so forcing the flag would raise; stub `require_torch` (:153-155) instead, or install a stand-in `C.torch` with `__version__`.

###### TESTS-5 (note; stands)

CI: Python 3.12, requirements.txt (numpy, scipy, matplotlib) and pytest (.github/workflows/tests.yml:17-29). The suite's only skip markers are `_needs_torch` in test_p5_g3_calibrators.py (:24; six uses at :120, :143, :175, :239, :305, :341) and test_layer3_deep_hedging.py (:18; :63, :68), and the fork `pytest.skip` at test_p5_g2_reference.py:691-692 and test_p5_v3_study.py:353-354; no `importorskip` or `xfail`. So eight skip in CI and the fork cases run there. The count reproduces: 339 `def test_` plus 33 parametrised extras (binance 5 cases at test_binance_data.py:69-82, identifiability 4, kline 3, layer1c 3 x 3, p5_g2 2, p5_v3_study 2, rough_heston_cf 5 strikes x 2, roughvol_core 5 + 2 + 2, rv_series 3) = 372, as CONTRIBUTING.md:30 says; no conftest.py, pytest.ini, pyproject.toml, setup.cfg or tox.ini. ### 9.22 The completeness critic

#### P5 v3 pre-freeze review, D97: completeness critique

Read at `3fad362`, the tracked tree clean (`git status --porcelain --untracked-files=no` empty). Read-only throughout: `git show`, `git diff --stat`, `git ls-files`, `git rev-parse`, `sed`, `grep`, `cut`, `awk`, `sha256sum`; one standard-library arithmetic command (section 5). No P5 script, test or pricer run; no repository module imported; no record under `output/` opened (the hashes of the four blobs were taken from `git show HEAD:<path> | sha256sum`); no `.npz` touched; no seed drawn.

##### 1. Verdict on the review's completeness

The review is substantially complete. Each of the seven briefs' checks has a report with path:line evidence, and the skeptics' verdicts added evidence where a report was thin. Every file the context lists shows signs of having been read by at least one dimension except `p5_g0_main.py`, which `git diff --stat 59ca589 HEAD` shows byte-identical to v2's (only `p5_g2_reference.py` and `p5_g3_calibrators.py` changed among the thirteen bound files), so nothing was lost there. The four declarations of the draft (lines 7, 9, 15, 200) and the amendment's summary and "What had been seen" were each verified against the log by at least one dimension, and in most cases by two or three with agreeing results. The six comparisons the brief names were each made in full by at least one dimension: the 18 lines against the plan (dimensions 1, 4, 5, 6 agree on the set); the constants against the study's settings (dimensions 1, 2, 4, key by key, the float `fd_rel_step` included); the hashes (dimension 5 for every blob the amendment states, dimension 4 for the records' headers and G̃); the seeds (dimensions 1, 4, 6, the rule checked arithmetically); the sealed draws (dimension 4's survey of every call site and guard); the order of acts (dimension 5). D95's Declared bullets (1) to (7) are each covered by some finding or checked-sound item (G2R-1; V3TEXT's title-line item; DECL-6; D2's `_start_of`; the header checks; DECL-4, TESTS-6, NRE-5; TESTS on Stage 1 and 2); D96's one Declared bullet is not (C-5).

The must-fix findings stand on this critic's own reading. NRE-1 = TESTS-1: `score_set` takes every record of the set at the level (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:887`), hands `under[prior]["sigma_P"]` to `nre_posterior` (:906-907), and `NRETarget` raises `ValueError` on `None` (:432-434) with no catch in `nre_posterior` (:479-498) or `score_set`; `reference_pair` leaves `under` empty on a uniform-run failure and partly filled on a direct-run failure (`p5_g2_reference.py:816-847`). AMD-1: the amendment's :37 and :47 drop the plan's exception at `P5_v3_plan.md:74`. DECL-1 = V3TEXT-2: `ROADMAP.md:4694-4700` and :4715-4718 split D91 between "the session that guides the author" and the Opus session; the attribution in line 200 traces to D95's :5277-5280, not to D91.

What the review misses falls into four kinds, recorded as C-1 to C-12: three run paths after the freeze that are in neither of dimension 7's lists and that no test or recorded run exercises (C-1 to C-3); points the skeptics found that no finding list carries (C-4, C-8, C-9, C-11, C-12); two declarations passed by one dimension and faulted by another's skeptic (C-5, C-6); and five sentences in the reports' own findings or proposed fixes that are inexact and must not be copied into the fixes (C-7), with three process declarations for D97's record (C-10). None is a further must-fix.

##### 2. Gaps, by dimension

**Dimension 1, the text against the plan.** Complete on every check of its brief. Two of its checked-sound items are contradicted by other dimensions and are resolved against it in section 3: line 75's "H within ε of the faces" (NRE-2) and line 15's "in place of" (DECL-3). Its consequence skeptic's observation that line 68's "the start included" is a change the plan does not name is carried by no finding (C-11). V3TEXT-9's proposed clause is inexact (C-7 (iv)).

**Dimension 2, the sampler in the code.** Complete on (a) to (e); the port was diffed function by function and the unexercised paths reasoned through. Its correctness skeptic's third docstring shortfall, step 4's clip clause for a run with a target, is in no finding list (C-4). Its checked-sound gloss of the merge ("polished to one point ... as line 68 says") is corrected by V3TEXT-4 from `p5_g2_reference.py:459-463`. D2's "κ can reach 1.5e12" is confirmed by arithmetic from `PRIOR_AB` (A: 1.5e12; B: 9.2e12 at u = 1e-6).

**Dimension 3, the ratio estimator's sampler.** Complete on its brief, NRE-1 confirmed. Three gaps: the ratio estimator's repeat and failure status are checked sound from the code alone and exercised by no test (C-3); the keep rule's prior term differing between routes, raised by its consequence skeptic, is a reading to declare (C-8); NRE-5's sentence on L-BFGS-B is false of `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:297-300` (C-7 (iii)), and NRE-3's magnitude claim rests on estimation (C-10).

**Dimension 4, G2 and the records.** Complete on (a) to (e), the headers and reports read for stated figures only. `g2_report` on the retry's record at attempt 2 is exercised by no test and no run (C-2, shared with dimension 7). G2R-3's "no other use" sentence is inexact at `test_p5_v3_transcription.py:55` (C-7 (i)). Its correctness skeptic's third pin of the stale wording (`test_p5_v3_study.py:625`) is confirmed and belongs with G2R-1's fix.

**Dimension 5, the amendment file.** Complete on (a) to (e), every hash verified. Line 41's test clause was passed and is inexact (C-6); AMD-5's proposed clause is false for D88's sitting (C-7 (ii)); step 2's silence on v2's hash caveat and the new ones (C-9) and the fate of line 7's lead-in (AMD-2, resolved as a decision for the author) are the two points on the freeze commit's text.

**Dimension 6, the declarations.** Complete on its brief, DECL-1 confirmed. D96's Declared bullet is not covered (C-5); DECL-3's proposed wording undercounts the seeds still drawn (C-7 (i)); the two line-200 wording points raised by skeptics are in no finding list (C-12). Its read of `output/p5_g1_tasks.jsonl` is outside the brief's list of readable records (C-10).

**Dimension 7, the tests and the unexercised paths.** Complete on (a) and (c), and (b)'s two lists are long; three paths after the freeze are in neither list: a start that raises, with its `task_error` line, its resume loop and Stage 1's refusal of an incomplete record (`p5_stage2.py:400-404`) (C-1); `g2_report` at attempt 2 on a record at doubled lengths (C-2); the ratio estimator's repeat and failure status (C-3). TESTS-4's proposed test would raise without torch (C-7 (v)). Its count over every task line of the transcription record is at the edge of the brief's rule on the records (C-10). Its claim that "364 passed, 8 skipped" is not in the log is confirmed by grep.

**Cross-dimension.** The one run path on which the dimensions' lists and findings disagree in grade, "best kept solution" = `kept[0]` (V3TEXT-5 note, D2-1 same-round), is resolved as same-round in section 3. The fixes to NRE-1 differ only in scope (the ratio estimator's branch alone, or both calibrators); both skeptic pairs confirm Stage 2 reads no row of a failed pair (`p5_stage2.py:217-221`, :456-467), so the author's choice is free of consequence for the measures.

##### 3. Contradictions and their resolution

Fourteen are listed in the structured field, each with the file that settles it. In summary: three findings were graded differently by two dimensions on the same fact (D91's attribution, line 7's gloss, `kept[0]`), resolved as must-fix, same-round and same-round; two checked-sound items of dimension 1 are overturned by dimensions 3 and 6 from the files (line 75's "within ε of the faces"; line 15's "in place of"); dimension 2's gloss of the merge is corrected by V3TEXT-4 from `p5_g2_reference.py:459-463`; NRE-5's sentence on L-BFGS-B is corrected by TESTS-6 from `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:297-300`; NRE-1's and TESTS-1's "under == {}" is refined from `p5_g2_reference.py:838-847` without touching the grade; DECL-3's and G2R-3's seed sentences are corrected from `test_p5_v3_transcription.py:55, 240-243`; AMD-2 is resolved as a decision for the author from v2's frozen line 137; TESTS-7 is resolved as same-round in effect; the brief's CI figure is unsupported by the log; and five pointer slips are resolved in the skeptics' favour without effect on any finding.

### 9.23 Skeptic of the critic's items, correctness lens

#### Skeptic's report on the completeness critic's findings C-1 to C-12 (P5 v3, D97)

##### 1. Lens and summary

Lens: correctness. Each claim was read against the files at HEAD (3fad362), v2's blobs at 59ca589 and the decision log; nothing was run beyond two standard-library header reads (section 4). Of the twelve findings, one is refuted (C-10, on the brief's default rule, since it concerns the dimension reports rather than the files, and one of its premises is wrong), eleven stand, and two are re-graded downwards (C-1 is a note with a narrower gap than claimed; C-2 is a note, the attempt-2 body being v2's and having run in D89). The severities of C-3, C-4, C-6 and C-7 (same-round) and C-5, C-8, C-9, C-11 and C-12 (note) stand.

##### 2. The findings

###### C-1 — a start that raises: stands, note, with a correction
The raises are at `p5_g2_reference.py:441-442`, `507-508` and `589-590`; `launch` records a `task_error` line and no record (`1158-1164`), both pool paths catch `Exception` (`1177-1192`), `read_jsonl` keeps only `kind == \"task\"` lines as tasks (`p5_g0_pilot.py:608-613`), so `done` (`1148-1149`) omits the pair and a resume re-runs it from the same generator; `g2_report` refuses a missing pair (`1301-1302`); `run_stage1` refuses an incomplete record (`p5_stage2.py:400-402`); `nre_posterior`, `predict_rows` and `score_set` catch nothing (`p5_g3_calibrators.py:479-498, 555-570, 897-910`). The only tests naming `task_error` assert its absence (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:726`, `test_p5_v3_study.py:374`). Correction: `launch`'s task-error line and the resume of errored pairs were exercised by D89's attempt 1 at `7e97ac8` (ROADMAP.md:4489-4512, fourteen TypeErrors then the completion with `n_done_before` 4), and `launch` is byte-identical at 59ca589, 7e97ac8 and HEAD. The unexercised path is narrower: a raise inside the start and its deterministic repetition on resume.

The signature, the header's lengths, `base_steps` and the report's `attempt` are as cited (`p5_g2_reference.py:1281-1283, 1295-1299, 1336-1338`); every test call leaves `attempt` at 1 (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:278, 294, 524, 644, 647, 716, 720, 731`), the hand-off test uses a recorder (`539-546, 563`), and the study called it with `attempt=1` (`p5_v3_study.py:877-878`). ### 9.25 The synthesis

#### D97 synthesis: P5 protocol version 3 before the freeze

Repository `C:\Users\micha\OneDrive\Documents\roughvollab` at `3fad362`, the tracked tree clean (checked with `git status --porcelain`, untracked files aside). Read-only throughout. The seven dimensions' findings, the fourteen skeptics' verdicts and observations, and the critic's twelve items were synthesised; the facts behind every must-fix item and every severity disagreement were re-read from the files at HEAD before grading (section 7).

##### 1. Verdict

**Freeze after fixes; no redesign.**

The sampler change is the plan's and nothing more. The draft differs from v2's frozen text in exactly the 18 lines D95 names (confirmed by three dimensions from `git show 59ca589:docs/protocols/P5_protocol_v2_draft.md` against HEAD's draft). The constants of candidate (A) in line 68 equal `p5_g2_reference.py:171-182`, the study's `p5_v3_study.py:103-113` and the committed headers; the six seeds are the six smallest primes above 149 in line 143's order; G̃ hashes to `c3adb2e2…`; the sealed seeds 20261002 and 20261022 are drawn on no path; the port of the study's sampler into the script differs only in declared places; the code agrees with the text on every path the study's 55 reference runs and the check's 55 exercised.

Four items must change before freezing. One is a bug on a path a run after the freeze will take whenever a test or G3-pilot reference fails, an outcome lines 101 and 144 anticipate: `score_set` hands the ratio estimator no Σ_P for a failed pair and stops with an uncaught `ValueError` (NRE-1 = TESTS-1). One is a contradiction between the registration text and the plan's binding list (AMD-1). One is an incomplete AI-use declaration (DECL-1 = V3TEXT-2). One is a sentence of line 68 that disagrees with the code on a reachable path (V3TEXT-4). Each is a small, local fix; none changes a registered rule, the sampler's behaviour on any exercised path, a seed, a tolerance or the budget. The rest is wording, staleness, missing clauses, docstrings and weak tests, to be made in the same round.

The paths no test or run has exercised (the repeat at doubled lengths with its fresh start, m ≥ 2 on the least-squares route and the merge after the polish, the gradient route on a trained network with its repeat and failure status, `g2_report` at attempt 2, the entry points' hand-off of doubled lengths, a start that raises) are correct by reading in every dimension that read them; they are a decision for the author (section 4, first item).

##### 2. What must change before freezing

Ordered by severity.

1. **NRE-1 = TESTS-1 (must-fix; four skeptics agree; the critic confirmed; confirmed here).** `p5_g3_calibrators.py:887` builds the rows from every record of the set at the level with no status filter; `:906-907` passes `((r.get("under") or {}).get(prior) or {}).get("sigma_P")` to `nre_posterior`, which constructs `NRETarget` (`:487-488`); `NRETarget.__init__` raises `ValueError` on `None` (`:432-434`); nothing in `nre_posterior` (`:479-498`), `score_set` (`:885-912`) or `main` catches it. A `reference_failure` record returns with `under = {}` when the uniform run fails (`p5_g2_reference.py:817`, `:822-825`) and with `under["A"]` only, Σ_P included, when the direct run under B fails (`:838-839` written before `:844-847` returns). So `--score g3` and `--score test` stop with a traceback at the first ratio-estimator cell holding a failed pair, after the header (`:879-882`) and that cell's ensemble rows (`:894-903`), and again on every resume (`:891`). `pilot_surfaces` removes failures for G3 (`:513-515`); `score_set` does not, for either set. Stage 2 never reads a failed pair's rows (`p5_stage2.py:217-221`, `:456-467`), so skipping them is safe. v2 scored failed pairs without needing Σ_P (`59ca589:p5_g3_calibrators.py:818`, `:837`), so the crash is v3's. No test scores a set holding a failed record (`test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:351-354`).
   *Fix.* In `score_set`, restrict the rows the ratio estimator scores to records with status `"ok"` (a filter on Σ_P present would still score the failed pair under the prior whose Σ_P survived); record the skipped pairs by label; add a test with one planted `reference_failure` record asserting it is skipped and the rest scored, keeping the direct `ValueError` test for an `ok` record without Σ_P; one clause in line 75's record clause (not beside line 101, which is not a plan-named line and which line 7 declares byte-identical) that a pair whose reference failed is not scored by the ratio estimator, its pair being outside every measure by Stage 1. D97's record states that the fix changes no registered rule and that the transcription check never ran `p5_g3_calibrators.py`, so D96's attestation is untouched by it. Whether the ensemble's branch is filtered too is the author's (section 4).

2. **AMD-1 (must-fix; four skeptics agree; the critic confirmed; confirmed here).** `docs/protocols/P5_v3_amendment.md:37` lists "the calibrators, their architecture and training" as unchanged, citing the plan's "What v3 does not change", whose line `docs/protocols/P5_v3_plan.md:74` carries the exception "except the start and first proposal of the ratio estimator's sampler, if Decision 3 extends (A) to it" (answered with the gradient route, `ROADMAP.md:4672-4674`); `:47`, the paragraph for 695FW, says "the hypotheses, priors, calibrators, tolerances and the sealed test set are unchanged" and names no change to the ratio estimator's sampler, while draft line 75 (under Architecture and training) changes it, draft line 7 names it and the amendment's own bullet at `:31` states it. Both texts are pasted on OSF at steps 3 and 4.
   *Fix.* Line 37: "the calibrators, their architecture and training, except the start and first proposal of the ratio estimator's sampler, by the gradient route (above)". Line 47: name the ratio estimator's sampler among the changes and write "the calibrators' architecture and training otherwise".

3. **DECL-1 = V3TEXT-2 (must-fix under the brief's definition; V3TEXT-2's skeptics graded same-round, DECL-1's must-fix; the critic resolved must-fix; confirmed here).** Draft line 200: "D91 (the plan, 25 October 2026) ran on Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code". `ROADMAP.md:4694-4696`: "The first version was written on 25 October 2026, after D89, in the session that guides the author.    *Fix.* Line 200 for D91: the plan's first version came from the session that guides the author, whose model D91 does not record; its read-only review by nine agents on Claude Fable 5.1 and the corrected copy came from the Claude Code session on Claude Opus 5.5. A deterministic toy emulator with two least-squares minima (θ off the fold; the mirrored mode inside the mass window, roughly u in (0.19, 0.81) under A per the TESTS correctness skeptic) driving `run_chains` under "uniform" to m = 2 (`chain_solution [0, 1, 0, 1]`, reached plus `starts_elsewhere` = 16, `accept` fails, `reference_pair` records a reference failure with both attempts' `start`) and under prior A through the polish; a second toy, if the author builds it, whose two minima polish to within 0.01 (`n_merged_after_polish` 1, m 1, the survivor's `n_starts_reached` the sum, `reached` re-mapped). `test_repeat_then_reference_failure` (`test_p5_g2_reference.py:161-171`) to assert each attempt's `start` carries 16 least-squares starts and that the second attempt's starts differ from the first's, optionally replayed from the generator as `:812-836` does. A torch test monkeypatching `R.split_rhat` to 1.5 and running `nre_posterior` on the Quadratic module, asserting status "failure", two attempts at (b, s) and (2b, 2s) with differing trimmed starts, `H_hat None`, and the row `score_set` writes. A numpy-only test driving `C.main` through `--g3`, `--train` and `--score test` with recorders, stubbing `require_torch` rather than forcing `_HAS_TORCH` (torch is `None` without it and `main` prints `torch.__version__`, `p5_g3_calibrators.py:103-106`, `:934-936`), asserting `post_kw` {burn 20000, sample 80000} from a passed retry report reaches `run_g3` and `score_set` and that `hashes` carries G̃, the G3 report and the set's references; one drive of `S.main --run` on the synthetic inputs. One tuple of every seed in use (plan line 47's 19 plus v3's six) asserted disjoint from each script's own seeds in `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:52`, `test_p5_stage2.py:32` and `test_p5_g1_emulator.py:38`. All outside the hash list; `CONTRIBUTING.md`'s count changes with each.

**`p5_v3_study.py`** (G2R-1, with the G2 skeptics' observations). `SOURCES` and `SOURCE_REPORTS` read `R.SETS["g2"]` and `["g2r"]` (`:139-140`), since D95 `output/p5v3_g2_*` (`p5_g2_reference.py:198-203`), under the comment "v2's committed records (D89)" and docstrings saying v2's; after v3's G2 the hold mode's `estimate()` will print v3's report as "from v2's reports" and, with a retry record, `read_sources()` will list v3's 36 pairs as "v2 ok"; `--run` stays refused by `refuse_overwrite` on the committed report (`:1180`) and by launch's header check on seeds (`p5_g2_reference.py:1144`); `test_p5_v3_study.py:67`, `:625` and `:635` pin the stale reading. Either set the two dicts to v2's literal names (the first change to the script since the run, its blob at HEAD being the study's at 633b470, to be declared) or leave the script and extend D97's declaration to the state after v3's G2 (lean: the second).

##### 4. Decisions for the author

Each with a lean marked as such.

1. **An end-to-end run on a tiny setting of the unexercised paths** (the repeat at doubled lengths with its fresh start; m ≥ 2 on the least-squares route and the merge; the gradient route's repeat and failure status; `g2_report` at attempt 2; the entry points' hand-off; a start that raises). Lean: yes before the freeze for the numpy-only toy tests of section 3 (free of the hash list, minutes on LinearEm) and for one `nre_posterior` run in `.venv-p5` on a toy-trained ReLU network at the written acceptance and lengths (TESTS-6; writes nothing, sees nothing of G̃), its statuses and m recorded in the fixes' entry; no run on G̃ before the freeze, the plan's order of acts putting G2 first.
2. **D2-1 = V3TEXT-5.** Declare "best kept solution" = the first kept solution in the keep rule's pre-polish order (no code change, "as the study ran it" kept), or re-sort `kept` by the polished log-target after the loop (a no-op at m = 1 so the check still reproduces, but it re-maps `reached` and `chain_solution`, at m = 3 changes which solution gets two chains, and departs from the study's sampler on an unexercised path, to be declared). Lean: declare.
3. **V3TEXT-4's grade**, its skeptics split. The fix is made either way. Lean: must-fix, by the brief's test, with the note that the keep rule defines distinctness at 0.01.
4. **DECL-2 = V3TEXT-1's grade**, DECL-2's correctness skeptic must-fix against three graders' same-round. Lean: same-round with the fuller fix.
5. **NRE-1's breadth.** Filter the ratio estimator's branch alone (the narrowest change to code v2 registered; the ensemble's rows for failed pairs left as v2 wrote them, which Stage 2 never reads) or both calibrators. Lean: the ratio estimator's branch alone, skipped pairs recorded by label.
6. **AMD-7 and C-9.** If a fix touches `p5_g2_reference.py` (docstrings) or `p5_g3_calibrators.py` (NRE-1, NRE-2's docstring): re-run the transcription check, or declare that D96 attests the blobs at 579b525 and that the changes are off the sampler's path. Lean: declare for changes off the path, re-run for any change on it. Step 2 to say whether the appended hash list repeats v2's caveat on `p5_g0_pilot.py` and adds that `p5_g2_reference.py`'s and `p5_g3_calibrators.py`'s hashes are the fixes' commit's, neither the blob v2's G2 ran (7e97ac8) nor the study's (633b470). Lean: name them, in v2's form.
7. **AMD-2.** Line 7's last sentence stays as the list's lead-in (precedent: v2's frozen line 137) or is replaced at the freeze; step 2 to say which. Lean: keep it, the appended text continuing it.
8. **G2R-1.** Set `SOURCES` to v2's literal names (a declared first change since the run) or leave the script and extend the declaration. Lean: leave the script.
9. **V3TEXT-8 = D2-4 = C-1, a start that raises.** Declare the task-error outcome (the pair absent, re-run on resume from the same stream and raising again, `g2_report` and Stage 1 refusing until the author intervenes; unreachable on a finite G̃), or catch the start's exceptions in `_run_with_repeat` and record a reference failure, which changes line 68's definition of a reference failure. Lean: declare, no code change.
10. **NRE-4.** An early `SystemExit` check for Σ_P before training or scoring. Lean: leave as declared once NRE-1 is fixed.
11. **NRE-3.** Tighten the torch test's value tolerance from 1e-3 after one run in `.venv-p5` (1e-5 a candidate), the docstring stating the figure; D97 records that the bound is the file's figure and that the reviewer's estimate of the float32–float64 gap was not measured. Lean: tighten.
12. **TESTS-5.** Cite CI's Actions run for the review commit in D97's record or the freeze entry; the brief's "364 passed, 8 skipped" is in no entry at HEAD (D95 records local counts and 372 collected). Lean: cite the run.
13. **G2R-3.** One shared tuple of every seed in use, or the minimum additions; the plan's item 3 is met as written. Lean: the shared tuple.
14. **DECL-9.** Whether line 200 carries v2's post-freeze sittings D86 and D87, for which the log names no model, beyond the plan's item 6. Lean: leave it to the log.
15. **C-12.** Whether to name the per-sitting subagent reviewers of D92, D93, D95 and D96. Lean: one clause when line 200 is edited.
16. **AMD-10.** Whether the fixes' entry appends its outcome to step 1 as D83 did. Lean: D83's precedent.
17. **C-5.** D96's declared staleness of `p5_v3_transcription.py:18` carried forward, or line 18 reworded. Lean: carry it forward, leave the script.
18. **AMD-9.** Reword "their hashes listed in the protocol's Status line" after a list including `ROADMAP.md`, or leave v2's identical wording declared. Lean: the small rewording.
19. **G2R-4** (no change). Record that the timed window is `run_chains` plus `accept`, as in v2, so the core-seconds include the acceptance diagnostics as well as the optimiser's, conservatively. Lean: record it.
20. **AMD-5's granularity.** Lean: sitting by sitting in line 15, one sentence in the amendment's line 41.
21. **TESTS-8.** Extend the CI workflow's comment or leave it. Lean: leave it.
22. **C-2** (both skeptics re-graded to note). A toy test of `g2_report` at attempt 2, the only untested composition being the pass-through of `int(attempt)`; the attempt-2 body ran at 7e97ac8 for v2's retry. Lean: list the path in D97's record, no test.

##### 6. Refuted findings

The claim is about the dimension reports, which are not in the repository, so it cannot be verified from the files and is refuted by the brief's default. Part (iii), NRE-3's estimate, is already marked as such by NRE-3's skeptics and carried into decision 11.

No other finding was refuted. C-2 stands but both skeptics re-graded it from same-round to note (decision 22).

##### 7. Contested findings settled

No finding carries the status "contested". The severity disagreements and the contradictions the critic listed were settled by reading the source at HEAD:

- **V3TEXT-4**: `p5_g2_reference.py:459` merges within `DISTINCT` = 0.01 (`:180`), not at coincidence; the text and the code differ on a reachable path, so must-fix by the brief's test, the fix being wording only (section 2, item 4).
- **DECL-2 = V3TEXT-1**: line 7 read in full; the gloss omits the go rule's clause, line 137's entries and the checklist while the anchor sentence stays true; same-round with the fuller fix (section 3).
- **NRE-1 = TESTS-1**: `p5_g2_reference.py:838-839` writes `under["A"]` with its Σ_P before a B-direct failure returns at `:844-847`; the skeptics' partial-`under` case holds, so the filter must be on status "ok".
- **NRE-2**: lines 68 and 75 read; line 68 says "inside the faces", line 75 "within ε of the faces"; `p5_g3_calibrators.py:442` bounds H to `[ε, 1 − ε]`.
- **DECL-3 / C-6 / C-7(i)**: `test_p5_v3_transcription.py:240-243` draws all six of v2's seeds; DECL-3's proposed wording is wrong in its count.
- **TESTS-7**: line 15 contains no "107".
- **NRE-5 against TESTS-6**: `test_p5_g3_calibrators.py:297-300` runs `R.run_chains` with `target=tg` (an `NRETarget` on a ReLU trunk) and asserts route, m and Σ only; TESTS-6's account is the accurate one (the trunk's untrained construction at `:246-247` is as the skeptics cite; not re-read here).
- **AMD-1**: amendment `:37` and `:47` and plan `:74` read; the exception is dropped in both OSF texts.
- **DECL-1**: `ROADMAP.md:4694-4696` and `:4715-4718` read; D95:5277-5278 read.
- **AMD-2**: settled on the skeptic's citation of v2's frozen line 137 (not re-read here) as a decision for the author, with a same-round clause in step 2.
- **The brief's "364 passed, 8 skipped"**: per TESTS-5 and both its skeptics, in no entry at HEAD; the log has 372 collected and local counts only.

Pointer slips the critic resolved, to be used by the fixes' sitting rather than the findings' citations: TESTS-3's "one kept solution in every run" is `ROADMAP.md:5151-5152`; the stand-ins' finite-difference gradient is D93's at `4868-4871`; DECL-4's "no repeat" is `5113`; D2-3's `chain_starts` test is `test_p5_v3_study.py:323-333`; `candidate_run_chains` is `p5_v3_study.py:398-470`.
